		public static IEnumerable<ACMModelInfo> GetAllACMModels()
		{
			List<ACMModelInfo> list = GetInstalledModels().ToList();
			List<ACMModelInfo> list2 = GetSavedModels().ToList();
			foreach (ACMModelInfo installedModel in list)
			{
				ACMModelInfo val = list2.FirstOrDefault((ACMModelInfo model) => model.ModelName == installedModel.ModelName && model.VersionString == installedModel.VersionString);
				if (val != null)
				{
					installedModel.ModelForEdit = val;
					list2.Remove(val);
				}
			}
			return from model in list.Concat(list2)
				orderby model.ModelName
				select model;
		}

		public static IEnumerable<ACMModelInfo> GetInstalledModels()
		{
			string[] array = ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_GetInstalledModels();
			double currentVersion = double.Parse(MMVersionHelper.MVGetVersionStringM(), NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
			string[] array2 = array;
			string text2 = default(string);
			string text3 = default(string);
			string text4 = default(string);
			string revisionGuid = default(string);
			object obj = default(object);
			foreach (string text in array2)
			{
				if (ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_GetInstalledModelInfo(text, ref text2, ref text3, ref text4, ref revisionGuid, ref obj) && double.TryParse(text3, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var result) && result >= 37.0 && result <= currentVersion)
				{
					string text5 = Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.LocalApplicationData), "AspenTech", "AES", "AM Models", text3, text2 + ".atmlz");
					yield return new ACMModelInfo(text5, (ModelType)0, obj)
					{
						ModelName = text2,
						DisplayName = text,
						VersionString = text3,
						RevisionGuid = revisionGuid
					};
				}
			}
		}

		public static IEnumerable<ACMModelInfo> GetInstalledModelsForVersion(string version)
		{
			string path = Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.LocalApplicationData), "AspenTech", "AES", "AM Models", version);
			if (Directory.Exists(path))
			{
				string[] files = Directory.GetFiles(path, "*.atmlz");
				foreach (string text in files)
				{
					yield return new ACMModelInfo(text, (ModelType)0, (object)null);
				}
			}
		}

		public static IEnumerable<ACMModelInfo> GetSavedModels()

		private static void ExtractAmcfFile(string sourceArchiveFile, string destFolder, string modelName)
		{
			ZipArchive val = ZipFile.OpenRead(sourceArchiveFile);
			try
			{
				ExtractToFile(val.GetEntry("CustomModeling/" + modelName + ".acmf"), destFolder);
			}
			finally
			{
				((IDisposable)val)?.Dispose();
			}
		}

		private static void ExtractToFile(ZipArchiveEntry file, string destPath)
		{
			string text = Path.Combine(destPath, file.FullName);
			string directoryName = Path.GetDirectoryName(text);
			Directory.CreateDirectory(directoryName);
			if (!directoryName.EndsWith("\\") && !directoryName.EndsWith("/"))
			{
				file.ExtractToFile(text, overwrite: true);
			}
		}

		public static Model GetModelByName(string modelName)
		{
			IAEditCategories iAEditCategories = ModelLibraryHelper.GetIAEditCategories("ACM Models");
			if (iAEditCategories != null)
			{
				foreach (Category managedCategory in ModelLibraryLoader.GetManagedCategories(iAEditCategories, false, true))
				{
					foreach (Model item in (Collection<Model>)(object)managedCategory.Models)
					{
						if (item.Header.Equals(modelName))
						{
							return item;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static bool DeleteModel(ACMModelInfo modelInfo)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0086: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0062: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0053: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if ((int)modelInfo.ModelType == 0)
			{
				ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_CloseModel(modelInfo.ModelName);
				CMEHelper.CloseModelInEngine(modelInfo.ModelName);
				if (!ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_UninstallModel(modelInfo.ModelName, modelInfo.VersionString))
				{
					MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(CustomModelsResources.DeleteModelFailed, modelInfo.ModelName), MMVersionHelper.MVGetProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
					return false;
				}
			}
			else if ((int)modelInfo.ModelType == 1)
			{
				try
				{
					File.Delete(modelInfo.AtmlzFilePath);
				}
				catch (Exception ex)
				{
					MMMessageBox.Show(ex.Message, MMVersionHelper.MVGetProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool ModelExists(ACMModelInfo modelInfo)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0007: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0032: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0056: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005c: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			if ((int)modelInfo.ModelType == 2)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(modelInfo.AtmlzFilePath))
			{
				return File.Exists(modelInfo.AtmlzFilePath);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(modelInfo.AtmlzRelativePath))
			{
				if ((int)modelInfo.ModelType == 0)
				{
					return File.Exists(Path.Combine(ModelInstalledPath, modelInfo.VersionString, modelInfo.AtmlzRelativePath));
				}
				if ((int)modelInfo.ModelType == 1)
				{
					return File.Exists(Path.Combine(FolderForSavingModelInProgress, modelInfo.AtmlzRelativePath));
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool ModelLocked(string atmlzFilePath)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(atmlzFilePath))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!File.Exists(atmlzFilePath))
			{
				return false;
			}
			bool result = false;
			FileStream fileStream = null;
			bool flag = false;
			try
			{
				fileStream = File.Open(atmlzFilePath, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.ReadWrite);
				flag = true;
			}
			catch
			{
				result = true;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				fileStream.Dispose();
				fileStream = null;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static IEnumerable<ACMModelInfo> GetUserTemplates()
		{
			if (Directory.Exists(UserTemplateFolder))
			{
				string[] files = Directory.GetFiles(UserTemplateFolder, "*.atmlz");
				foreach (string text in files)
				{
					yield return new ACMModelInfo(text, (ModelType)1, (object)null);
				}
			}
		}

		public static IEnumerable<ACMModelInfo> GetBultInTemplates()
		{
			if (Directory.Exists(BuiltInTemplateFolder))
			{
				string[] files = Directory.GetFiles(BuiltInTemplateFolder, "*.atmlz");
				foreach (string text in files)
				{
					yield return new ACMModelInfo(text, (ModelType)1, (object)null);
				}
			}
		}

		public static IEnumerable<ACMModelInfo> GetAllTemplates()
		{
			List<ACMModelInfo> userTemplates = GetUserTemplates().ToList();
			return (from t in GetBultInTemplates().ToList()
				where !userTemplates.Exists((ACMModelInfo p) => p.ModelName.Equals(t.ModelName, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
				select t).Union(userTemplates);
		}

		private static string GetInstalledTemplatesFolder()
		{
			return Path.Combine(Directory.GetParent(Directory.GetParent(Assembly.GetEntryAssembly().Location).FullName).FullName, "ACM Model Templates");
		}

		private bool EditModelCommand_CanExecute(object obj)
		{
			if (SelectedRowIds.Count != 1)
			{
				((FrameworkElement)(object)cmdEdit).ToolTip = CustomModelsResources.SelectOneModel;
				return false;
			}
			object tag = gridControl.DataSource.Rows.ElementAt(SelectedRowIds[0]).Tag;
			ACMModelInfo val = (ACMModelInfo)((tag is ACMModelInfo) ? tag : null);
			int num;
			if (val != null)
			{
				num = ((val.VersionString != "37.0") ? 1 : 0);
				if (num != 0)
				{
					((FrameworkElement)(object)cmdEdit).ToolTip = CustomModelsResources.ACMObjectManager_Edit_Tooltip;
					return (byte)num != 0;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				num = 0;
			}
			((FrameworkElement)(object)cmdEdit).ToolTip = CustomModelsResources.V11ModelNotAllowed;

		private string CreateNewVariableName(string name, string suffix, int maxLength = 256)
		{
			if (name.Length < maxLength - suffix.Length)
			{
				return name + suffix;
			}
			return name.Substring(0, maxLength - suffix.Length) + suffix;
		}

		private string GenerateDefaultModelName(IEnumerable<string> lstExistingName, string sNamePrefix)
		{
			int num = 0;
			string newName = null;
			do
			{
				newName = CreateNewVariableName(sNamePrefix, $"{++num}");
			}
			while (lstExistingName != null && lstExistingName.Any((string p) => p?.Equals(newName, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) ?? false) && num < 2000);
			return newName;
		}

				{
					((FrameworkElement)(object)cmdRename).ToolTip = CustomModelsResources.NotAllowRenameInstalledModel;
					return false;
				}

					return false;

		private void RenameModelCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			//IL_008a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0102: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_021b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_028d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ACMModelInfo modelInfo = default(ACMModelInfo);
			ref ACMModelInfo reference = ref modelInfo;
			object tag = gridControl.DataSource.Rows.ElementAt(SelectedRowIds[0]).Tag;
			reference = (ACMModelInfo)((tag is ACMModelInfo) ? tag : null);
			if (modelInfo == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!_allModels.Contains(modelInfo) || !File.Exists(modelInfo.AtmlzFilePath))
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(CustomModelsResources.ACMModelNotExist, modelInfo.ModelName), MMVersionHelper.MVGetProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
				UpdateModelList();
				return;
			}
			ScreenContentControl val = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IShellMainWindow>(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).FindScreen((Func<ScreenContentControl, bool>)delegate(ScreenContentControl p)
			{
				if (p.ScreenSubject is CustomModelsEditorScreenSubject)
				{
					ACMModelInfo obj2 = modelInfo;
					ScreenSubject screenSubject = p.ScreenSubject;
					return ((object)obj2).Equals((object)((CustomModelsEditorScreenSubject)((screenSubject is CustomModelsEditorScreenSubject) ? screenSubject : null)).ModelInfo);
				}
				return false;
			});
			if (val != null && val is CustomModelsEditor)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(CustomModelsResources.ACMModelCannotRenamed, modelInfo.ModelName) + "\n" + CustomModelsResources.ACMModelCloseFortRename, MMVersionHelper.MVGetProductNameM() + CustomModelsResources.ACMModelRename, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
				return;
			}
			string text = "";
			string modelName = modelInfo.ModelName;
			_ = modelInfo.DisplayName;
			List<string> slModelNameList = new List<string>();
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (ACMModelInfo allModel in _allModels)
			{
				text = allModel.ModelName;
				slModelNameList.Add(text);
				list.Add(allModel.DisplayName);
			}
			if (_DuplicateDlg != null)
			{
				_DuplicateDlg = null;
			}
			string text2 = "";
			string renameModelDlg_Title = CustomModelsResources.RenameModelDlg_Title;
			CustomModelsObjectManager cmom = this;
			_DuplicateDlg = new DuplicateDlg(ref cmom, ref slModelNameList, modelName, renameModelDlg_Title, 1);
			_DuplicateDlg.Owner = Window.GetWindow((DependencyObject)(object)this);
			_DuplicateDlg.ShowDialog();
			text2 = cmom._sRetDup;
			_DuplicateDlg = null;
			text2 = text2.Trim();
			if (IsReservedKeyword(text2))
			{
				string text3 = string.Format(CustomModelsResources.ModelName_IsReserved, text2);
				MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow((DependencyObject)(object)this), text3, SystemEnvironment.ApplicationName, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
			else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
			{
				MMVersionHelper.MVGetVersionStringM();
				string atmlzFilePath = modelInfo.AtmlzFilePath;
				ACMModel aCMModel = new ACMModel(modelInfo);
				aCMModel.ACMDoc.RenameTypeModName(aCMModel.ModelTypeName, text2, out var succeeded);
				if (!succeeded)
				{
					string text4 = string.Format(CustomModelsResources.ModelName_Type_Exists, text2);
					MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow((DependencyObject)(object)this), text4, SystemEnvironment.ApplicationName, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
					return;
				}
				aCMModel.SaveDocument(aCMModel.ModelPath);
				string fileNameATMLZ = Path.Combine(modelInfo.SavedFolder, text2 + ".atmlz");
				aCMModel.ExportModel(text2, fileNameATMLZ, aCMModel.ModelPath);
				aCMModel.Close();
				File.Delete(atmlzFilePath);
				UpdateModelList();
			}
		}

		}

		private bool IsReservedKeyword(string name)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(name) && !((ACMDocomentWrapper)Activator.CreateInstance(Marshal.GetTypeFromCLSID(new Guid("4C4883CF-8401-42B1-B410-782C5E8A4708")))).IsKeyword(name) && !name.Equals("IntegerParameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) && !name.Equals("RealParameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) && !name.Equals("StringParameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) && !name.Equals("LogicalParameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				return name.Equals("Valve", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase);
			}
			return true;
		}

			List<string> slModelNameList = new List<string>();
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (ACMModelInfo allModel in _allModels)
			{
				text = allModel.ModelName;
				slModelNameList.Add(text);
				list.Add(allModel.DisplayName);
			}
			if (_DuplicateDlg != null)
			{
				_DuplicateDlg = null;
			}
			string text2 = "";
			string createDuplicateModelDlg_Title = CustomModelsResources.CreateDuplicateModelDlg_Title;
			CustomModelsObjectManager cmom = this;
			_DuplicateDlg = new DuplicateDlg(ref cmom, ref slModelNameList, modelName, createDuplicateModelDlg_Title, 0);
			_DuplicateDlg.Owner = Window.GetWindow((DependencyObject)(object)this);
			_DuplicateDlg.ShowDialog();
			text2 = cmom._sRetDup;
			_DuplicateDlg = null;
			text2 = text2.Trim();
			if (IsReservedKeyword(text2))
			{
				string text3 = string.Format(CustomModelsResources.ModelName_IsReserved, text2);
				MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow((DependencyObject)(object)this), text3, SystemEnvironment.ApplicationName, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
			else
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					return;
				}
				MMVersionHelper.MVGetVersionStringM();
				if (modelInfo.ModelForEdit != null)
				{
					modelInfo = modelInfo.ModelForEdit;
				}
				ScreenContentControl val = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IShellMainWindow>(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).FindScreen((Func<ScreenContentControl, bool>)delegate(ScreenContentControl p)
				{
					if (p.ScreenSubject is CustomModelsEditorScreenSubject)
					{
						ACMModelInfo obj2 = modelInfo;
						ScreenSubject screenSubject2 = p.ScreenSubject;
						return ((object)obj2).Equals((object)((CustomModelsEditorScreenSubject)((screenSubject2 is CustomModelsEditorScreenSubject) ? screenSubject2 : null)).ModelInfo);
					}
					return false;
				});
				if (val != null && val is CustomModelsEditor customModelsEditor)
				{
					ScreenSubject screenSubject = ((ScreenContentControl)customModelsEditor).ScreenSubject;
					CustomModelsEditorScreenSubject val2 = (CustomModelsEditorScreenSubject)(object)((screenSubject is CustomModelsEditorScreenSubject) ? screenSubject : null);
					if (val2 != null && (int)val2.ModelInfo.ModelType == 0)
					{
						customModelsEditor.ForceCloseScreen();
					}
					else
					{
						customModelsEditor.SaveCommand.Execute(null);
					}
				}
				ACMModel aCMModel = new ACMModel(modelInfo);
				aCMModel.ACMDoc.RenameTypeModName(aCMModel.ModelTypeName, text2, out var succeeded);
				if (!succeeded)
				{
					string text4 = string.Format(CustomModelsResources.ModelName_Type_Exists, text2);
					MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow((DependencyObject)(object)this), text4, SystemEnvironment.ApplicationName, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
					return;
				}
				aCMModel.SaveDocument(aCMModel.ModelPath);
				string fileNameATMLZ = Path.Combine(modelInfo.SavedFolder, text2 + ".atmlz");
				aCMModel.ExportModel(text2, fileNameATMLZ, aCMModel.ModelPath);
				aCMModel.Close();
				UpdateModelList();
			}
		}

		private void ImportModelCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			//IL_0285: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01dd: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_019e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01a4: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0263: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_016b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0171: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0108: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			Microsoft.Win32.OpenFileDialog openFileDialog = new Microsoft.Win32.OpenFileDialog();
			openFileDialog.CheckFileExists = true;
			openFileDialog.DefaultExt = ".atmlz";
			openFileDialog.Filter = CustomModelsResources.ATMLZFileFilter;
			if (openFileDialog.ShowDialog() != true)
			{
				return;
			}
			string fileName = openFileDialog.FileName;
			object obj2 = ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_OpenModelFile(fileName);
			if (obj2 != null)
			{
				string text = ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_GetModelProperty(obj2, "Version");
				string modelName = ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_GetModelProperty(obj2, "Name");
				string text2 = ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_GetModelProperty(obj2, "RevisionGuid");
				DateTime dateTime = ACMModelInfo.ParseTime(ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_GetModelProperty(obj2, "Timestamp"));
				ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_CloseModelFile(obj2);
				ACMModelInfo val = ACMModelHelper.GetInstalledModels().FirstOrDefault((ACMModelInfo model) => model.ModelName.Equals(modelName, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase));
				double result;
				if (val != null)
				{
					if (val.VersionString == text)
					{
						if (text2 == val.RevisionGuid)
						{
							MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(CustomModelsResources.ModelAreadyInstalled, modelName, text), SystemEnvironment.ApplicationName, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
							return;
						}
						if ((int)MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(CustomModelsResources.ConfirmReplaceModel, val.ModelName, val.VersionString, val.ModifiledTime, modelName, text, dateTime), SystemEnvironment.ApplicationName, (MMMessageBoxButton)4, (MMMessageBoxImage)32, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1) != 6)
						{
							return;
						}
					}
					else if ((int)MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(CustomModelsResources.ConfirmReplaceNewVersion, modelName, val.VersionString, text), SystemEnvironment.ApplicationName, (MMMessageBoxButton)4, (MMMessageBoxImage)32, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1) != 6)
					{
						return;
					}
				}
				else if (!double.TryParse(text, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result))
				{
					MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(CustomModelsResources.InvalidModel, modelName), SystemEnvironment.ApplicationName, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
					return;
				}
				ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_CloseModel(modelName);
				CMEHelper.CloseModelInEngine(modelName);
				if (ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_InstallModel(fileName, true))
				{
					CMEHelper.UpdateModelInEngine(modelName);
					CMEHelper.RefreshACMModelLibrary(modelName, 0);
					((CompositePresentationEvent<EventArgs>)(object)UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<RefreshModelPaletteEvent>()).Publish(new EventArgs());
				}
				else
				{
					MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(CustomModelsResources.InstallModelFailed, modelName), SystemEnvironment.ApplicationName, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(CustomModelsResources.InvalidATMLZFile, fileName), SystemEnvironment.ApplicationName, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		private bool ExportModelCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			bool num = SelectedRowIds.Count == 1;
			if (num)
			{
				((FrameworkElement)(object)cmdExport).ToolTip = CustomModelsResources.Export_Tooltip;
				return num;
			}
			((FrameworkElement)(object)cmdExport).ToolTip = CustomModelsResources.SelectOneModel;
			return num;
		}

		private void ExportModelCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			//IL_00db: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0057: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ACMModelInfo val = _allModels.ElementAt(SelectedRowIds[0]);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!_allModels.Contains(val) || !File.Exists(val.AtmlzFilePath))
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(CustomModelsResources.ACMModelNotExist, val.ModelName), MMVersionHelper.MVGetProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
				UpdateModelList();
				return;
			}
			Microsoft.Win32.SaveFileDialog saveFileDialog = new Microsoft.Win32.SaveFileDialog();
			saveFileDialog.FileName = val.ModelName;
			saveFileDialog.Filter = CustomModelsResources.ATMLZFileFilter;
			saveFileDialog.Title = CustomModelsResources.Export;
			saveFileDialog.OverwritePrompt = true;
			if (saveFileDialog.ShowDialog() != true)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				File.Copy(val.AtmlzFilePath, saveFileDialog.FileName, overwrite: true);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(ex.Message, MMVersionHelper.MVGetProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		private bool RevertModelCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			//IL_003e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (SelectedRowIds.Count != 1)
			{
				((FrameworkElement)(object)cmdRevert).ToolTip = CustomModelsResources.SelectOneModel;
				return false;
			}
			ACMModelInfo val = _allModels.ElementAt(SelectedRowIds[0]);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			int num;
			if ((int)val.ModelType == 0)
			{
				num = ((val.ModelForEdit != null) ? 1 : 0);
				if (num != 0)
				{
					((FrameworkElement)(object)cmdRevert).ToolTip = CustomModelsResources.ACMObjectManager_RevertToDeployedVersion_Tooltip;
					return (byte)num != 0;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				num = 0;
			}
			((FrameworkElement)(object)cmdRevert).ToolTip = CustomModelsResources.CannotRevertModel;
			return (byte)num != 0;
		}

		private void RevertModelCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			//IL_007d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00b5: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00bb: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			ACMModelInfo modelInfo = _allModels.ElementAt(SelectedRowIds[0]);
			if (modelInfo == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!_allModels.Contains(modelInfo) || !File.Exists(modelInfo.AtmlzFilePath))
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(CustomModelsResources.ACMModelNotExist, modelInfo.ModelName), MMVersionHelper.MVGetProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
				UpdateModelList();
			}
			else if ((int)MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow((DependencyObject)(object)this), string.Format(CustomModelsResources.ConfirmRevertModel, modelInfo.ModelName), SystemEnvironment.ApplicationName, (MMMessageBoxButton)4, (MMMessageBoxImage)32, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1) == 6)
			{
				if (UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IShellMainWindow>(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).FindScreen((Func<ScreenContentControl, bool>)delegate(ScreenContentControl p)
				{
					if (p.ScreenSubject is CustomModelsEditorScreenSubject)
					{
						ACMModelInfo obj2 = modelInfo;
						ScreenSubject screenSubject = p.ScreenSubject;
						return ((object)obj2).Equals((object)((CustomModelsEditorScreenSubject)((screenSubject is CustomModelsEditorScreenSubject) ? screenSubject : null)).ModelInfo);
					}
					return false;
				}) is CustomModelsEditor customModelsEditor)
				{
					customModelsEditor.ForceCloseScreen();
				}
				ACMModelHelper.DeleteModel(modelInfo.ModelForEdit);
				modelInfo.ModelForEdit = null;
				UpdateModelList();
				((CompositePresentationEvent<EventArgs>)(object)UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<RefreshModelPaletteEvent>()).Publish(new EventArgs());
				((DispatcherObject)this).Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Input, (Action)delegate
				{
					UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IShellMainWindow>(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).ActivateScreen((ScreenContentControl)(object)this);
				});
			}
		}

		}

		internal static DataTemplate CreateCheckmarkTemplate(bool isChecked)
		{
			DataTemplate dataTemplate = new DataTemplate();
			FrameworkElementFactory frameworkElementFactory = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(System.Windows.Controls.Image));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(System.Windows.Controls.Image.SourceProperty, ImageResources.Checkmark);
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(FrameworkElement.MarginProperty, new Thickness(4.0));
			if (isChecked)
			{
				frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, Visibility.Visible);
			}
			else
			{
				frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, Visibility.Collapsed);
			}
			dataTemplate.VisualTree = frameworkElementFactory;
			return dataTemplate;
		}

		private DataTemplate CreateDeleteFieldTemplate(ACMModelInfo modelInfo)
		{
			DataTemplate dataTemplate = new DataTemplate();
			FrameworkElementFactory frameworkElementFactory = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(MMDeleteButton));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(System.Windows.Controls.Control.BackgroundProperty, System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent);
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(System.Windows.Controls.Control.BorderBrushProperty, System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent);
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(FrameworkElement.HorizontalAlignmentProperty, System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Stretch);
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.ButtonBase.CommandProperty, DeleteModelCommand);
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.ButtonBase.CommandParameterProperty, modelInfo);
			frameworkElementFactory.SetBinding(UIElement.IsEnabledProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Row.Tag")
			{
				Converter = new CanModelBeDeletedConverter()
			});
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(ToolTipService.ShowOnDisabledProperty, true);
			Style style = new Style();
			Trigger item = new Trigger
			{
				Property = UIElement.IsEnabledProperty,
				Value = false,
				Setters = { (SetterBase)new Setter
				{
					Property = FrameworkElement.ToolTipProperty,
					Value = CustomModelsResources.DeleteACMModelTooltip
				} }
			};
			style.Triggers.Add(item);
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(FrameworkElement.StyleProperty, style);
			dataTemplate.VisualTree = frameworkElementFactory;
			return dataTemplate;
		}

		private string GetModelStatus(ACMModelInfo modelInfo)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0020: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0024: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0026: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			if ((int)modelInfo.ModelType == 0)
			{
				if (modelInfo.ModelForEdit == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				modelInfo = modelInfo.ModelForEdit;
			}
			ModelStatus status = modelInfo.Status;
			if ((int)status != 0)
			{
				if ((int)status == 2)
				{
					return CustomModelsResources.ACMObjectManager_Incomplete;
				}
				return null;
			}
			return CustomModelsResources.ACMObjectManager_ReadyToDeploy;
		}

		public static bool IsModelInUse(ACMModelInfo modelInfo)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			IList<string> list = default(IList<string>);
			if ((int)modelInfo.ModelType == 0)
			{
				return CMEHelper.GetBlocksUsingModelM(modelInfo.DisplayName, ref list) > 0;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void GridControl_CellValueChanged(object sender, CellValueChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			object tag = e.Data.Row.Tag;
			ACMModelInfo val = (ACMModelInfo)((tag is ACMModelInfo) ? tag : null);
			if (e.Data.Column.Tag is string text && text == "Description")
			{
				if (val.ModelForEdit != null)
				{
					val.ModelForEdit.Description = e.NewValue as string;
				}
				else
				{
					val.Description = e.NewValue as string;
				}
				((CompositePresentationEvent<EventArgs>)(object)UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<RefreshACMModelManagerEvent>()).Publish(new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		protected void NotifyPropertyChanged(string propName)
		{
			this.PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
		}

		private void GridControl_DefaultContextMenuOpening(object sender, DefaultContextMenuEventArgs e)
		{
			e.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Add(new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem
			{
				Header = ApwnResources.ApwnShellCopy,
				Command = ApplicationCommands.Copy,
				CommandTarget = (IInputElement)gridControl
			});
			e.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Add(new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem
			{
				Header = ApwnResources.ApwnShellPaste,
				Command = ApplicationCommands.Paste,
				CommandTarget = (IInputElement)gridControl
			});
			if (e.IsOpenFromRowHeader)
			{
				IEnumerable<DataRow> selectedRows = gridControl.SelectedRows;
				bool flag = false;
				IList<string> list = default(IList<string>);
				foreach (DataRow item in selectedRows)
				{
					object tag = item.Tag;
					ACMModelInfo val = (ACMModelInfo)((tag is ACMModelInfo) ? tag : null);
					if (val != null && CMEHelper.GetBlocksUsingModelM(val.ModelName, ref list) == 0)
					{
						flag = true;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					e.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Add(new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem
					{
						Header = ApwnResources.Delete,
						Command = DeleteMultipleModelsCommand,
						CommandTarget = (IInputElement)gridControl
					});
				}
			}
			((RoutedEventArgs)(object)e).Handled = true;
		}

		private void gridControl_PreviewMouseDoubleClick(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			DataCellInfo currentCell = gridControl.CurrentCell;
			if (((DataCellInfo)(ref currentCell)).Column != null)
			{
				string text = ((DataCellInfo)(ref currentCell)).Column.Tag?.ToString();
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && text == "Description")
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			if (EditModelCommand.CanExecute(null))
			{
				EditModelCommand.Execute(null);
			}
		}

		private void UpdateTemplateList()
		{
			//IL_0009: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0019: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0029: Expected O, but got Unknown
			List<ACMModelInfo> first = new List<ACMModelInfo>
			{
				new ACMModelInfo((string)null, (ModelType)1, (object)null)
				{
					ModelName = "Blank",
					DisplayName = CustomModelsResources.Blank
				}
			};
			TemplateList = first.Union(ACMModelHelper.GetAllTemplates()).ToList();
		}

		private void OnUpdateACMModelTemplatesEvent(EventArgs obj)
		{
			UpdateTemplateList();
		}

		private void CreateFromTemplateCommand_Executed(object args)
		{
			ACMModelInfo val = (ACMModelInfo)((args is ACMModelInfo) ? args : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				if (val.ModelName.Equals("Blank", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					NewCustomModelCommand_Executed();
				}
				else
				{
					CreateModelFromTemplate(val);
				}
			}
		}

		private void CreateModelFromTemplate(ACMModelInfo templateModelInfo)
		{
			//IL_0063: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

			//IL_0284: Expected O, but got Unknown

			//IL_00d8: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0136: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			IEnumerable<ACMModelInfo> allModels = _allModels;
			string text = GenerateDefaultModelName(allModels.Select((ACMModelInfo p) => p.ModelName), templateModelInfo.ModelName + "_");
			string newModelName;
			while (true)
			{
				OneIdDialog val = new OneIdDialog(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, CustomModelsResources.CreateModelDialog_Prompt, text, (string[])null, (maskType)7, CustomModelsResources.CreateModelDialog_Title, (int?)27);
				if (((Window)(object)val).ShowDialog() != true)
				{
					return;
				}
				newModelName = val.NewID.Trim();
				if (IsReservedKeyword(newModelName))
				{
					string text2 = string.Format(CustomModelsResources.ModelName_IsReserved, newModelName);
					MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow((DependencyObject)(object)this), text2, SystemEnvironment.ApplicationName, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
					continue;
				}
				if (allModels.FirstOrDefault((ACMModelInfo p) => p.ModelName.Equals(newModelName, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase)) != null)
				{
					string text3 = string.Format(CustomModelsResources.ModelExists, newModelName);
					MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow((DependencyObject)(object)this), text3, SystemEnvironment.ApplicationName, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
					continue;
				}
				if (!Directory.Exists(ACMModelHelper.FolderForSavingModelInProgress))
				{
					bool flag = false;
					try
					{
						Directory.CreateDirectory(ACMModelHelper.FolderForSavingModelInProgress);
						flag = true;
					}
					catch
					{
					}
					if (!flag)
					{
						MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(CustomModelsResources.FailedToCreateFolderForModel, ACMModelHelper.FolderForSavingModelInProgress), MMVersionHelper.MVGetProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
						UpdateModelList();
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			string text4 = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), templateModelInfo.ModelName + ".atmlz");
			File.Copy(templateModelInfo.AtmlzFilePath, text4, overwrite: true);
			MMVersionHelper.MVGetVersionStringM();
			ACMModel aCMModel = new ACMModel(new ACMModelInfo(text4, (ModelType)1, (object)null));
			aCMModel.ACMDoc.RenameTypeModName(aCMModel.ModelTypeName, newModelName, out var _);
			aCMModel.SaveDocument(aCMModel.ModelPath);
			string text5 = Path.Combine(ACMModelHelper.FolderForSavingModelInProgress, newModelName + ".atmlz");
			aCMModel.ExportModel(newModelName, text5, aCMModel.ModelPath);
			aCMModel.Close();
			File.Delete(text4);
			((CompositePresentationEvent<ShowScreenEventArgs>)(object)UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<ShowScreenEvent>()).Publish(new ShowScreenEventArgs
			{
				ScreenFactoryKey = ScreenFactoryKeys.CustomModelsEditorScreenFactory,
				CreateNewTab = true,
				InitialLocation = (ScreenLocation)0,
				ScreenSubject = (ScreenSubject)new CustomModelsEditorScreenSubject
				{
					ModelInfo = new ACMModelInfo(text5, (ModelType)3, (object)null)
				}
			});
		}

	public class DateTimeComparer : IComparer<object>
	{
		public static readonly DateTimeComparer Default = new DateTimeComparer();

		protected DateTimeComparer()
		{
		}

		public int Compare(object x, object y)
		{
			if (x == DBNull.Value)
			{
				x = null;
			}
			if (y == DBNull.Value)
			{
				y = null;
			}
			if (x == null && y == null)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			if (x == null)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			if (y == null)
			{
				return 1;
			}
			x.GetType();
			y.GetType();
			DateTime result;
			if (x is DateTime)
			{
				result = (DateTime)x;
				if (y is DateTime value)
				{
					return result.CompareTo(value);
				}
			}
			if (DateTime.TryParse(x.ToString(), out result) && DateTime.TryParse(y.ToString(), out var result2))
			{
				return result.CompareTo(result2);
			}
			return x.ToString().CompareTo(y.ToString());
		}
	}
	internal class CanModelBeDeletedConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			ACMModelInfo val = (ACMModelInfo)((value is ACMModelInfo) ? value : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				return !CustomModelsObjectManager.IsModelInUse(val);
			}
			return false;
		}

		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}

	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)

			if (val != null && !val.ModelName.Equals("Blank", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				ACMModel aCMModel = new ACMModel(val);
				ImageSource result = (ModelLibraryLoader.CreateDrawingElement(aCMModel.Icons.FirstOrDefault((ACMIcon ic) => ic.IsDefault).TargetModelIcon, new SolidColorBrush(System.Windows.Media.Color.FromRgb(222, 231, 247)), (double?)50.0, (double?)50.0) as System.Windows.Controls.Image)?.Source;
				aCMModel.Close();
				return result;
			}
			return null;
		}

	internal class ModelTemplateTooltipConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			ACMModelInfo val = (ACMModelInfo)((value is ACMModelInfo) ? value : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				if (!val.ModelName.Equals("Blank", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					return val.Description;
				}
				return CustomModelsResources.BlankModelTooltip;

			return null;
		}
