		public static ScreenContentControl CreateScreen(IUnityContainer container, ScreenSubject screenSubject, ScreenState screenState)
		{
			//IL_002a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0030: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			CustomModelsEditor customModelsEditor = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<CustomModelsEditor>(container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
			CustomModelsEditorScreenSubject val = (CustomModelsEditorScreenSubject)(object)((screenSubject is CustomModelsEditorScreenSubject) ? screenSubject : null);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			ACMModelInfo modelInfo = val.ModelInfo;
			if (!ACMModelHelper.ModelExists(modelInfo))
			{
				return null;
			}
			if ((int)modelInfo.ModelType == 1 && ACMModelHelper.ModelLocked(modelInfo.AtmlzFilePath))
			{
				return null;
			}
			((ScreenContentControl)customModelsEditor).InitializeScreen(container, screenSubject, screenState);
			return (ScreenContentControl)(object)customModelsEditor;
		}

		public override void InitializeScreen(IUnityContainer container, ScreenSubject screenSubjectOpaque, ScreenState screenStateOpaque)
		{
			//IL_00f9: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00ff: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0102: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0108: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_011d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0123: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer = container;
			((ScreenContentControl)this).ScreenSubject = screenSubjectOpaque;
			ScreenSubject screenSubject = ((ScreenContentControl)this).ScreenSubject;
			ACMModelInfo modelInfo = ((CustomModelsEditorScreenSubject)((screenSubject is CustomModelsEditorScreenSubject) ? screenSubject : null)).ModelInfo;
			NavigationHeader = string.Format(CustomModelsResources.NavigationPanel_Header, modelInfo.ModelName);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(modelInfo.DisplayName))
			{
				((ScreenContentControl)this).Header = CustomModelsResources.ACMModel + " (" + modelInfo.ModelName + ")";
			}
			else
			{
				((ScreenContentControl)this).Header = CustomModelsResources.ACMModel + " (" + modelInfo.DisplayName + ")";
			}
			Model = OpenModel(modelInfo);
			if (Model.NotEditable)
			{
				modelInfo.NotEditable = true;
			}
			else
			{
				new ComAwareEventInfo(typeof(_DACMDocomentWrapperEvents_Event), "Message").AddEventHandler(Model.ACMDoc, new _DACMDocomentWrapperEvents_MessageEventHandler(ACMDoc_Message));
				RootTreeNode = new ACMTreeNode
				{
					Children = GenerateNavigationTreeNodes()
				};
				((FrameworkElement)this).DataContext = this;
				if ((int)modelInfo.ModelType == 2 || (int)modelInfo.ModelType == 3)
				{
					SaveCommand?.Execute(null);
					if ((int)modelInfo.ModelType == 3)
					{
						modelInfo.ModelType = (ModelType)1;
					}
				}
				_commandsUpdater = new DispatcherTimer(DispatcherPriority.Render)
				{
					Interval = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(300.0)
				};
				_commandsUpdater.Tick += _commandsUpdater_Tick;
				_commandsUpdater.Start();
				SubscribeEvents();
			}
			((ScreenContentControl)this).InitializeScreen(container, screenSubjectOpaque, screenStateOpaque);
		}

		protected override void OnIsActiveChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			if (newValue && !oldValue)
			{
				Model.SetActiveModel();
			}
			else if (!newValue && oldValue)
			{
				foreach (Window ownedWindow in System.Windows.Application.Current.MainWindow.OwnedWindows)
				{
					if (ownedWindow is FindAndReplace)
					{
						ownedWindow.Close();
					}
				}
			}
			((ApwnDocumentControl)this).OnIsActiveChanged(oldValue, newValue);
		}

		private void SubscribeEvents()
		{
			((CompositePresentationEvent<CloseCustomModelEditorEventArgs>)(object)UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<CloseCustomModelEditorEvent>()).Subscribe((Action<CloseCustomModelEditorEventArgs>)OnCloseCustomModelEditorEvent);
			((CompositePresentationEvent<SaveCustomModelEditorEventArgs>)(object)UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<SaveCustomModelEditorEvent>()).Subscribe((Action<SaveCustomModelEditorEventArgs>)OnSaveCustomModelEditorEvent);
			((CompositePresentationEvent<BeforeSavingEventArgs>)(object)UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<BeforeSavingEvent>()).Subscribe((Action<BeforeSavingEventArgs>)OnBeforeSaveSimulationEvent);
		}

		private void UnSubscribeEvents()
		{
			((CompositePresentationEvent<CloseCustomModelEditorEventArgs>)(object)UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<CloseCustomModelEditorEvent>()).Unsubscribe((Action<CloseCustomModelEditorEventArgs>)OnCloseCustomModelEditorEvent);
			((CompositePresentationEvent<SaveCustomModelEditorEventArgs>)(object)UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<SaveCustomModelEditorEvent>()).Unsubscribe((Action<SaveCustomModelEditorEventArgs>)OnSaveCustomModelEditorEvent);
			((CompositePresentationEvent<BeforeSavingEventArgs>)(object)UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<BeforeSavingEvent>()).Unsubscribe((Action<BeforeSavingEventArgs>)OnBeforeSaveSimulationEvent);
		}

		private bool IsModelModified()
		{
			bool flag = Model.IsModified;
			if (!flag)
			{
				bool flag2 = false;
				foreach (Collection<IAcmEditor> value in AllEditors.Values)
				{
					if (flag2)
					{
						break;
					}
					foreach (IAcmEditor item in value)
					{
						if (item.IsModified)
						{
							flag = true;
							flag2 = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return flag;
		}

		private void OnBeforeSaveSimulationEvent(BeforeSavingEventArgs obj)
		{
			if (_needSave.HasValue)
			{
				if (_needSave == true)
				{
					SaveCommand?.Execute(null);
				}
				else if (IsModelModified())
				{
					SaveCommand?.Execute(null);
				}
			}
			else if (IsModelModified())
			{
				SaveCommand?.Execute(null);
			}
		}

		private void OnSaveCustomModelEditorEvent(SaveCustomModelEditorEventArgs obj)
		{
			if (obj.ModelName == null || obj.ModelName.Equals(Model.Name, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				SaveCommand?.Execute(null);
			}
		}

		private void OnCloseCustomModelEditorEvent(CloseCustomModelEditorEventArgs obj)
		{
			ForceCloseScreen(obj.SaveModel);
		}

		private void _commandsUpdater_Tick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			(IncreaseCommand as RelayActionCommand<object>)?.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			(DecreaseCommand as RelayActionCommand<object>)?.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			(CompleteKeyCommand as RelayActionCommand<object>)?.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
		}

		private void ACMDoc_Message(string message)
		{
			LogMessage(message);
		}

		private void LogMessage(string message)
		{
			if (message.EndsWith("\n"))
			{
				messagePanel.Items.Add(message.TrimEnd('\n'));
				messagePanel.Items.Add("");
			}
			else
			{
				messagePanel.Items.Add(message);
			}
		}

		private void MessagePanel_MouseDoubleClick(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (messagePanel.SelectedItem != null && IsTextEditorDisplayed)
			{
				string text = messagePanel.SelectedItem.ToString();
				int num = text.IndexOf(':');
				if (num > 0 && int.TryParse(text.Substring(0, num), NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var result))
				{
					(rightPanel.Content as TextEditor).GotoLine(result);
				}
			}
		}

		private void MessagePanel_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			System.Windows.Controls.ListBox root = (System.Windows.Controls.ListBox)sender;
			ScrollViewer scrollViewer = FindScrollViewer(root);
			if (scrollViewer == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			scrollViewer.ScrollChanged += delegate(object o, ScrollChangedEventArgs args)
			{
				if (Math.Abs(args.ExtentHeightChange) > 0.0)
				{
					scrollViewer.ScrollToBottom();
				}
			};
		}

		private static ScrollViewer FindScrollViewer(DependencyObject root)
		{
			Queue<DependencyObject> queue = new Queue<DependencyObject>(new DependencyObject[1] { root });
			do
			{
				DependencyObject dependencyObject = queue.Dequeue();
				if (dependencyObject is ScrollViewer)
				{
					return (ScrollViewer)dependencyObject;
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(dependencyObject); i++)
				{
					queue.Enqueue(VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(dependencyObject, i));
				}
			}
			while (queue.Count > 0);
			return null;
		}

		public void ForceCloseScreen(bool save = false)
		{
			_needSave = save;
			UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IShellMainWindow>(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).CloseScreen((ScreenContentControl)(object)this);
		}

		protected override void OnClosing(CancelEventArgs args)
		{
			//IL_0014: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0042: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0047: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0048: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004a: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0055: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0057: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			if (!_needSave.HasValue)
			{
				bool num = IsModelModified();
				MMMessageBoxResult val = (MMMessageBoxResult)7;
				if (num)
				{
					val = MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow((DependencyObject)(object)this), string.Format(CustomModelsResources.ConfirmSaveModel, Model.Name), SystemEnvironment.ApplicationName, (MMMessageBoxButton)3, (MMMessageBoxImage)32, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
					if ((int)val == 2)
					{
						args.Cancel = true;
						return;
					}
					_needSave = (int)val == 6;
				}
			}
			foreach (Collection<IAcmEditor> value in AllEditors.Values)
			{
				foreach (IAcmEditor item in value)
				{
					item.OnClosing();
				}
			}
			if (_needSave == true)
			{
				SaveCommand_Executed(null);
			}
			CMEHelper.HCheckInACMEModel();
			((ApwnDocumentControl)this).OnClosing(args);
		}

		protected override void OnClosed(EventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			foreach (Collection<IAcmEditor> value in AllEditors.Values)
			{
				foreach (IAcmEditor item in value)
				{
					item.OnClosed();
				}
			}
			if (_FindDlg != null)
			{
				_FindDlg.Close();
			}
			if (_typeTypeDefinitionDlg != null)
			{
				_typeTypeDefinitionDlg.Close();
			}
			Model.Close();
			((CompositePresentationEvent<CustomModelEditorClosedEventArgs>)(object)UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<CustomModelEditorClosedEvent>()).Publish(new CustomModelEditorClosedEventArgs(Model));
			UnSubscribeEvents();
			((ApwnDocumentControl)this).OnClosed(eventArgs);
		}

		private bool AddObjectCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (!(arg is ACMTreeNode aCMTreeNode))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (aCMTreeNode == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return aCMTreeNode.NodeType == NodeType.Folder;
		}

		private void AddObjectCommand_Executed(object arg)
		{
			//IL_0033: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0039: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_01e7: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_022b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!(arg is ACMTreeNode { NodeType: NodeType.Folder } aCMTreeNode))
			{
				return;
			}
			OneIdDialog val = new OneIdDialog(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, CustomModelsResources.NavigationPanel_Name, (string)null, (string[])null, (maskType)7, CustomModelsResources.NavigationPanel_Create, (int?)27);
			CustomTypeInput customTypeInput = null;
			if (aCMTreeNode.ModelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomVariableType || aCMTreeNode.ModelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomParameterType)
			{
				if (aCMTreeNode.ModelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomVariableType)
				{
					((Window)(object)val).Title = CustomModelsResources.AddNewVariableType;
				}
				else if (aCMTreeNode.ModelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomParameterType)
				{
					((Window)(object)val).Title = CustomModelsResources.AddNewParameterType;
				}
				((FrameworkElement)(object)val).Width = double.NaN;
				((FrameworkElement)(object)val).Height = double.NaN;
				((Window)(object)val).SizeToContent = SizeToContent.WidthAndHeight;
				if (((ContentControl)(object)val).Content is StackPanel stackPanel)
				{
					TextType textType = ((aCMTreeNode.ModelObject.Type != ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomVariableType) ? TextType.Parameter : TextType.Variable);
					customTypeInput = new CustomTypeInput(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, Model, textType)
					{
						Margin = new Thickness(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 11.0)
					};
					stackPanel.Children.Insert(stackPanel.Children.Count - 1, customTypeInput);
				}
			}
			if (((Window)(object)val).ShowDialog() != true)
			{
				return;
			}
			string newName = val.NewID;
			try
			{
				if (Model.ACMDoc.IsKeyword(newName) || newName.Equals("IntegerParameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) || newName.Equals("RealParameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) || newName.Equals("StringParameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) || newName.Equals("LogicalParameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					MMMessageBox.Show(CustomModelsResources.VarNameError_IsKeyword, MMVersionHelper.MVGetProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)16, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
					return;
				}
				if (customTypeInput != null && string.Equals(newName, customTypeInput.InheritedType, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(CustomModelsResources.TypeDefinitionAlreadyExists, newName), MMVersionHelper.MVGetProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)16, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
					return;
				}
				ACMObject aCMObject = null;
				if (aCMTreeNode.ModelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomVariableType)
				{
					ACMCustomVariableType aCMCustomVariableType = new ACMCustomVariableType(Model, newName);
					if (customTypeInput != null)
					{
						aCMCustomVariableType.InheritedType = customTypeInput.InheritedType;
						aCMCustomVariableType.Text = customTypeInput.GetText(newName);
						Model.ACMDoc.GetTypedAssignments(0, aCMCustomVariableType.InheritedType, out var _, out var value, out var sPhysicalQuantity, out var sUnits, out var lower, out var upper, out var _);
						if (double.TryParse(customTypeInput.Value, out var result))
						{
							aCMCustomVariableType.Value = result;
						}
						else
						{
							aCMCustomVariableType.Value = value;
						}
						if (double.TryParse(customTypeInput.LowerValue, out var result2))
						{
							aCMCustomVariableType.Lower = result2;
						}
						else
						{
							aCMCustomVariableType.Lower = lower;
						}
						if (double.TryParse(customTypeInput.UpperValue, out var result3))
						{
							aCMCustomVariableType.Upper = result3;
						}
						else
						{
							aCMCustomVariableType.Upper = upper;
						}
						aCMCustomVariableType.PhyscialType = sPhysicalQuantity;
						aCMCustomVariableType.Units = sUnits;
					}
					aCMObject = Model.Add(aCMTreeNode.ModelObject.Type, newName, aCMCustomVariableType);
				}
				else if (aCMTreeNode.ModelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomParameterType)
				{
					ACMCustomParameterType aCMCustomParameterType = new ACMCustomParameterType(Model, newName);
					if (customTypeInput != null)
					{
						aCMCustomParameterType.InheritedType = customTypeInput.InheritedType;
						aCMCustomParameterType.ParameterType = customTypeInput.ParameterType;
						aCMCustomParameterType.Text = customTypeInput.GetText(newName);
						aCMCustomParameterType.Lower = null;
						aCMCustomParameterType.Upper = null;
						Model.ACMDoc.GetParameterValues(aCMCustomParameterType.InheritedType, out var _, out var _, out var sUnits2, out var sPhysicalQuantity2, out var sValue, out var sUpper, out var sLower);
						if (customTypeInput.ParameterType == ParameterTypes.Integer || customTypeInput.ParameterType == ParameterTypes.Real)
						{
							aCMCustomParameterType.Value = ((!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(customTypeInput.Value)) ? customTypeInput.Value : sValue);
							aCMCustomParameterType.Lower = ((!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(customTypeInput.LowerValue)) ? customTypeInput.LowerValue : sLower);
							aCMCustomParameterType.Upper = ((!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(customTypeInput.UpperValue)) ? customTypeInput.UpperValue : sUpper);
						}
						else if (customTypeInput.ParameterType == ParameterTypes.String)
						{
							if (customTypeInput.StringParameterDefaultValue != null)
							{
								aCMCustomParameterType.Value = customTypeInput.StringParameterDefaultValue.StringValue;
							}
						}
						else
						{
							aCMCustomParameterType.Value = customTypeInput.ParamValue;
						}
						aCMCustomParameterType.PhyscialType = sUnits2;
						aCMCustomParameterType.Units = sPhysicalQuantity2;
					}
					aCMObject = Model.Add(aCMTreeNode.ModelObject.Type, newName, aCMCustomParameterType);
				}
				else
				{
					aCMObject = Model.Add(aCMTreeNode.ModelObject.Type, newName);
				}
				if (aCMObject == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (aCMObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMIcon)
				{
					UpdateIconsNode();
					aCMObject = Model.Icons.FirstOrDefault((ACMIcon icon) => icon.Name == newName);
					SelectFormFromObjectManager(aCMObject);
				}
				else
				{
					AddObjectToNavigationTree(aCMObject);
				}
				Model.IsModified = true;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		private bool DeleteObjectCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (!(arg is ACMTreeNode aCMTreeNode))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (aCMTreeNode.NodeType != NodeType.Form)
			{
				return false;
			}
			ACMObject modelObject = aCMTreeNode.ModelObject;
			if (modelObject == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (modelObject.IsBuiltIn)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMHelp)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMText)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if ((modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMSubmodel || modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomParameterType || modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomVariableType || modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMProcedure) && IsSubTextObjectInUse(modelObject as ACMText))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public List<int> AllIndexesOf(ref string str, string svalue)
		{
			List<int> list = new List<int>();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(svalue))
			{
				return list;
			}
			int startIndex = 0;
			while (true)
			{
				startIndex = str.IndexOf(svalue, startIndex, StringComparison.Ordinal);
				if (startIndex == -1)
				{
					break;
				}
				list.Add(startIndex);
				startIndex += svalue.Length;
			}
			return list;
		}

		public bool FindInUseA(ACMObjectTypes type, string modelText, string objectName)
		{
			string[] array = modelText.Split(Environment.NewLine.ToCharArray(), StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
			int num = 0;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			string empty = string.Empty;
			string empty2 = string.Empty;
			string[] array2 = array;
			for (int i = 0; i < array2.Length; i++)
			{
				empty = array2[i].Trim();
				if (empty.StartsWith("//"))
				{
					continue;
				}
				num = empty.IndexOf("//");
				if (num > 0)
				{
					empty = empty.Substring(0, num);
				}
				num = empty.IndexOf(";");
				if (num > 0)
				{
					while (num > 0)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(empty.Substring(0, num + 1));
						empty2 = stringBuilder.ToString();
						switch (type)
						{
						case ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomParameterType:
						case ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomVariableType:
						case ACMObjectTypes.ACMSubmodel:
							if (Regex.IsMatch(empty2, "\\bas\\b.*\\b" + objectName + "[\\s|(|;]", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase))
							{
								return true;
							}
							break;
						case ACMObjectTypes.ACMProcedure:
							if (Regex.IsMatch(empty2, "\\bcall\\s*\\(.*=\\s*" + objectName + "\\s*\\(", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase))
							{
								return true;
							}
							break;
						}
						stringBuilder.Clear();
						empty = empty.Substring(num + 1);
						num = empty.IndexOf(";");
						if (num < 0)
						{
							stringBuilder.Append(empty);
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(empty + " ");
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool IsInheritedType(string sModelText, string sSubModelName)
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			string[] array = sModelText.Split(new char[2] { ' ', '\n' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
			if (array.Count() >= 4 && string.Equals(array[2], "Uses", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				text = array[3];
			}
			return text.Equals(sSubModelName, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase);
		}

		public long FindInUse(ref string sModelText, string sSubModelName, ref string sLine)
		{
			sLine = "";
			long result = 1L;
			long num = 1L;
			if (sModelText.IndexOf(sSubModelName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) != -1)
			{
				num = 0L;
			}
			string svalue = "/*";
			List<int> list = AllIndexesOf(ref sModelText, svalue);
			string svalue2 = "*/";
			List<int> list2 = AllIndexesOf(ref sModelText, svalue2);
			int count = list.Count;
			int count2 = list2.Count;
			bool flag = false;
			if (count > 0 || count2 > 0)
			{
				flag = true;
			}
			string[] separator = new string[1] { "\n" };
			string[] array = sModelText.Split(separator, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
			int num2 = array.Count();
			List<string> list3 = new List<string>();
			for (int i = 0; i < num2; i++)
			{
				array[i] = array[i].Trim();
				array[i] = array[i].Trim('\r');
				array[i] = array[i].Trim();
				if (!array[i].StartsWith("//"))
				{
					list3.Add(array[i]);
				}
			}
			string text = "";
			int num3 = 0;
			int num4 = list3.Count();
			for (int j = 0; j < num4; j++)
			{
				text = list3[j];
				num3 = text.IndexOf("//");
				if (num3 > 0)
				{
					text = text.Substring(0, num3);
					text = text.Trim();
					list3[j] = text;
				}
			}
			long num5 = 1L;
			foreach (string item in list3)
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					text = item;
					List<int> list4 = AllIndexesOf(ref text, svalue);
					List<int> list5 = AllIndexesOf(ref text, svalue2);
					int count3 = list4.Count;
					int count4 = list5.Count;
					if (count3 == 1 && count4 == 1)
					{
						text = text.Remove(list4[0], list5[0] + 2 - list4[0]);
						if (text.IndexOf(sSubModelName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
						{
							int length = text.IndexOf(sSubModelName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
							string text2 = text.Substring(0, length).Trim();
							int length2 = text2.Length;
							if (text2.Substring(length2 - 3).ToLower() == " as")
							{
								num5 = 0L;
								sLine = item;
								break;
							}
						}
					}
					else if (item.IndexOf(sSubModelName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
					{
						int length3 = item.IndexOf(sSubModelName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
						string text3 = item.Substring(0, length3).Trim();
						int length4 = text3.Length;
						if (text3.Substring(length4 - 3).ToLower() == " as")
						{
							num5 = 0L;
							sLine = item;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				else if (item.IndexOf(sSubModelName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
				{
					int length5 = item.IndexOf(sSubModelName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
					string text4 = item.Substring(0, length5).Trim();
					int length6 = text4.Length;
					if (text4.Substring(length6 - 3).ToLower() == " as")
					{
						num5 = 0L;
						sLine = item;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (num == 0L && num5 == 0L)
			{
				result = num5;
			}
			if (num == 0L && num5 == 1)
			{
				result = num5;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public void GetInUseSubTextObjects(ACMText textObject, out Dictionary<string, bool> inUseDictionary)
		{
			inUseDictionary = new Dictionary<string, bool>();
			IEnumerable<ACMText> enumerable = null;
			if (textObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMSubmodel)
			{
				enumerable = Model.SubModels;
			}
			else if (textObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomParameterType)
			{
				enumerable = Model.CustomParameterTypes;
			}
			else if (textObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomVariableType)
			{
				enumerable = Model.CustomVariableTypes;
			}
			else if (textObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMProcedure)
			{
				enumerable = Model.Procedures;
			}
			if (enumerable == null || enumerable.Count() == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_allEditors.TryGetValue(typeof(ACMText), out var value))
			{
				_ = string.Empty;
				foreach (ACMText item in enumerable)
				{
					if (!(value.FirstOrDefault() is TextEditor textEditor))
					{
						return;
					}
					string editorText = textEditor.axEdCtrl.GetEditorText();
					bool flag = FindInUseA(textObject.Type, editorText, item.Name);
					if (!flag)
					{
						editorText = Model.ACMDoc.GetModelText(Model.Name);
						flag = FindInUseA(textObject.Type, editorText, item.Name);
					}
					inUseDictionary.Add(item.Name, flag);
				}
				foreach (KeyValuePair<string, bool> item2 in inUseDictionary.Where((KeyValuePair<string, bool> x) => !x.Value).ToList())
				{
					string key = item2.Key;
					bool flag2 = false;
					if (_allEditors.TryGetValue(typeof(ACMSubmodel), out var value2))
					{
						foreach (TextEditor item3 in value2)
						{
							if (!(item3.Name == key))
							{
								string editorText2 = item3.axEdCtrl.GetEditorText();
								flag2 = FindInUseA(textObject.Type, editorText2, key);
								if (flag2)
								{
									inUseDictionary[key] = flag2;
									break;
								}
							}
						}
					}
					if (flag2)
					{
						continue;
					}
					foreach (ACMSubmodel subModel in Model.SubModels)
					{
						string modelText = Model.ACMDoc.GetModelText(subModel.Name);
						flag2 = FindInUseA(textObject.Type, modelText, key);
						if (flag2)
						{
							inUseDictionary[key] = flag2;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (textObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomParameterType)
			{
				foreach (KeyValuePair<string, bool> item4 in inUseDictionary.Where((KeyValuePair<string, bool> x) => !x.Value).ToList())
				{
					string key2 = item4.Key;
					bool flag3 = false;
					if (_allEditors.TryGetValue(typeof(ACMCustomParameterType), out var value3))
					{
						foreach (TextEditor item5 in value3)
						{
							if (!(item5.Name == key2))
							{
								string editorText3 = item5.axEdCtrl.GetEditorText();
								flag3 = IsInheritedType(editorText3, key2);
								if (flag3)
								{
									inUseDictionary[key2] = flag3;
									break;
								}
							}
						}
					}
					if (!flag3)
					{
						foreach (ACMCustomParameterType customParameterType in Model.CustomParameterTypes)
						{
							string modelText2 = Model.ACMDoc.GetModelText(customParameterType.Name);
							flag3 = IsInheritedType(modelText2, key2);
							if (flag3)
							{
								inUseDictionary[key2] = flag3;
								break;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				return;
			}
			if (textObject.Type != ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomVariableType)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, bool> item6 in inUseDictionary.Where((KeyValuePair<string, bool> x) => !x.Value).ToList())
			{
				string key3 = item6.Key;
				bool flag4 = false;
				if (_allEditors.TryGetValue(typeof(ACMCustomVariableType), out var value4))
				{
					foreach (TextEditor item7 in value4)
					{
						if (!(item7.Name == key3))
						{
							string editorText4 = item7.axEdCtrl.GetEditorText();
							flag4 = IsInheritedType(editorText4, key3);
							if (flag4)
							{
								inUseDictionary[key3] = flag4;
								break;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if (flag4)
				{
					continue;
				}
				foreach (ACMCustomVariableType customVariableType in Model.CustomVariableTypes)
				{
					string modelText3 = Model.ACMDoc.GetModelText(customVariableType.Name);
					flag4 = IsInheritedType(modelText3, key3);
					if (flag4)
					{
						inUseDictionary[key3] = flag4;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsSubTextObjectInUse(ACMText subTextObject)
		{
			GetInUseSubTextObjects(subTextObject, out var inUseDictionary);
			bool value;
			return inUseDictionary.TryGetValue(subTextObject.Name, out value) && value;
		}

		private void DeleteObjectCommand_Executed(object arg)
		{
			//IL_0055: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005b: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			if (!(arg is ACMTreeNode { NodeType: NodeType.Form } aCMTreeNode) || aCMTreeNode.ModelObject.IsBuiltIn)
			{
				return;
			}
			ACMObject modelObject = aCMTreeNode.ModelObject;
			if ((int)MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow((DependencyObject)(object)this), string.Format(CustomModelsResources.ConfirmDeleteObject, modelObject.Name), MMVersionHelper.MVGetProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)1, (MMMessageBoxImage)32, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1) != 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMSubmodel)
			{
				string name = ((ACMSubmodel)aCMTreeNode.ModelObject).Name;
				bool flag = true;
				string sLine = "";
				string sModelLineName = "";
				Dictionary<Type, Collection<IAcmEditor>> allEditors = _allEditors;
				Collection<IAcmEditor> value;
				bool num = allEditors.TryGetValue(typeof(ACMText), out value);
				string text = "";
				string text2 = "";
				if (num && value.Count > 0)
				{
					foreach (TextEditor item in value)
					{
						if (!(item.AcmObject.GetType() == typeof(ACMText)))
						{
							continue;
						}
						text2 = item.AcmObject.Name;
						text = item.axEdCtrl.GetEditorText();
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
						{
							flag = true;
							continue;
						}
						text = text.Trim();
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
						{
							flag = true;
							continue;
						}
						sModelLineName = text2;
						flag = !FindInUseA(modelObject.Type, text, name);
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					int num2 = 0;
					string text3 = "";
					string text4 = "";
					List<string> list = new List<string>();
					if (allEditors.TryGetValue(typeof(ACMSubmodel), out var value2) && value2.Count > 0)
					{
						foreach (TextEditor item2 in value2)
						{
							if (!(item2.AcmObject.GetType() == typeof(ACMSubmodel)))
							{
								continue;
							}
							text3 = item2.AcmObject.Name;
							if (!(text3 != name))
							{
								continue;
							}
							list.Add(text3);
							text4 = item2.axEdCtrl.GetEditorText();
							if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
							{
								flag = true;
								continue;
							}
							text4 = text4.Trim();
							if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
							{
								flag = true;
								continue;
							}
							sModelLineName = text3;
							flag = !FindInUseA(modelObject.Type, text4, name);
							if (flag)
							{
								continue;
							}
							break;
						}
						if (flag)
						{
							num2 = list.Count;
							if (Model.SubModels.Count > 0)
							{
								foreach (ACMSubmodel subModel in Model.SubModels)
								{
									text3 = subModel.Name;
									if (!(name != text3))
									{
										continue;
									}
									bool flag2 = false;
									if (num2 > 0)
									{
										flag2 = list.Contains(text3);
									}
									if (flag2)
									{
										continue;
									}
									text4 = Model.ACMDoc.GetModelText(text3);
									if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
									{
										flag = true;
										continue;
									}
									text4 = text4.Trim();
									if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
									{
										flag = true;
										continue;
									}
									sModelLineName = text3;
									flag = !FindInUseA(modelObject.Type, text4, name);
									if (flag)
									{
										continue;
									}
									break;
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if (Model.RemoveSubModel(aCMTreeNode.ModelObject, flag, ref sModelLineName, ref sLine))
				{
					RemoveObjectFromNavigationTree(aCMTreeNode.ModelObject);
					DeleteEditorIfExists(aCMTreeNode.ModelObject);
				}
			}
			else if (modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMIcon)
			{
				ACMIcon aCMIcon = modelObject as ACMIcon;
				Model.ACMDoc.DeleteIcon(Model.Name, aCMIcon.TargetModelIcon);
				DeleteEditorIfExists(aCMTreeNode.ModelObject);
				UpdateIconsNode();
			}
			else if (modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMProcedure)
			{
				Model.ACMDoc.DeleteProcedure(Model.Name, aCMTreeNode.ModelObject.Name, out var succeeded);
				if (succeeded)
				{
					Model.Remove(aCMTreeNode.ModelObject);
					RemoveObjectFromNavigationTree(aCMTreeNode.ModelObject);
					DeleteEditorIfExists(aCMTreeNode.ModelObject);
				}
			}
			else if (modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomVariableType || modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomParameterType)
			{
				if (Model.ACMDoc.RemoveSubModel(modelObject.Name) == 1)
				{
					Model.Remove(modelObject);
					RemoveObjectFromNavigationTree(modelObject);
					DeleteEditorIfExists(modelObject);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				Model.Remove(aCMTreeNode.ModelObject);
				RemoveObjectFromNavigationTree(aCMTreeNode.ModelObject);
				DeleteEditorIfExists(aCMTreeNode.ModelObject);
			}
		}

		private bool EditObjectCommand_CanExecute(object obj)
		{
			if (!(obj is ACMTreeNode aCMTreeNode))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (aCMTreeNode.NodeType == NodeType.Folder)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (aCMTreeNode.ModelObject == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			ACMObject modelObject = aCMTreeNode.ModelObject;
			if (modelObject == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (modelObject is ACMIcon && modelObject.IsBuiltIn)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (modelObject.GetType() == typeof(ACMTable) && modelObject.Name.ToLower() == "all variables")
			{
				if (obj != null && obj.GetType() == typeof(MouseButtonEventArgs))
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void EditObjectCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			ACMObject modelObject = (obj as ACMTreeNode).ModelObject;
			if (modelObject != null)
			{
				SelectFormFromObjectManager(modelObject);
			}
		}

		private bool RenameObjectCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (!(arg is ACMTreeNode aCMTreeNode))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (aCMTreeNode.NodeType == NodeType.Folder)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (aCMTreeNode.ModelObject == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			ACMObject modelObject = aCMTreeNode.ModelObject;
			if (modelObject == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMSubmodel)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMTable)
			{
				if (modelObject.Name.ToLower() == "all variables")
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			else if (modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMIcon)
			{
				if (modelObject.Name.ToLower() == "system")
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMHelp)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMProcedure)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMScript)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMText)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if ((modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomParameterType || modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomVariableType) && IsSubTextObjectInUse(modelObject as ACMText))
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void RenameObjectCommand_Executed(object arg)
		{
			//IL_025d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0264: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ACMTreeNode selectedNode = arg as ACMTreeNode;
			ACMObject modelObject = selectedNode.ModelObject;
			ACMTreeNode node = RootTreeNode.Children.FirstOrDefault((ACMTreeNode p) => p.NodeType == NodeType.Folder && p.Children.Contains(selectedNode));
			if (modelObject == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			ACMObject objToCopy;
			if (modelObject.GetType() == typeof(ACMTable))
			{
				ACMTable aCMTable = modelObject as ACMTable;
				objToCopy = new ACMTable(modelObject.ParentModel, modelObject.Name)
				{
					Variables = aCMTable.Variables,
					Attributes = aCMTable.Attributes,
					Description = aCMTable.Description,
					IsAllVariables = false,
					IsBuiltIn = false,
					IsADuplicate = true
				};
			}
			else if (modelObject.GetType() == typeof(ACMPlot))
			{
				ACMPlot aCMPlot = modelObject as ACMPlot;
				aCMPlot.LoadDefinition();
				objToCopy = new ACMPlot(modelObject.ParentModel, modelObject.Name)
				{
					Content = aCMPlot.Content,
					Is3D = aCMPlot.Is3D,
					Description = aCMPlot.Description,
					PlotInputFile = aCMPlot.PlotInputFile,
					ProfileDimensions = aCMPlot.ProfileDimensions,
					Profiles = aCMPlot.Profiles,
					ProfileVariables = aCMPlot.ProfileVariables,
					VariableNumbers = aCMPlot.VariableNumbers,
					XProfile = aCMPlot.XProfile,
					XVariables = aCMPlot.XVariables,
					YProfile = aCMPlot.YProfile,
					YVariables = aCMPlot.YVariables,
					IsADuplicate = true,
					IsBuiltIn = false
				};
			}
			else if (modelObject.GetType() == typeof(ACMScript))
			{
				ACMScript aCMScript = modelObject as ACMScript;
				aCMScript.Text = aCMScript.ParentModel.ACMDoc.GetScriptText(aCMScript.ParentModel.Name, aCMScript.Name);
				objToCopy = new ACMScript(modelObject.ParentModel, modelObject.Name, aCMScript.Text)
				{
					Description = aCMScript.Description,
					IsBuiltIn = false,
					IsADuplicate = true
				};
			}
			else
			{
				if (modelObject is ACMIcon aCMIcon)
				{
					string text = string.Format(Resources.RenameConfirmPrompt, aCMIcon.Name);
					OneIdDialog val = new OneIdDialog(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, text, aCMIcon.Name, (string[])null, (maskType)7, Resources.strRenameChild, (int?)27);
					if (((Window)(object)val).ShowDialog() != true)
					{
						return;
					}
					string newID = val.NewID;
					if (!(newID == aCMIcon.Name))
					{
						try
						{
							Model.ACMDoc.RenameIcon(Model.Name, aCMIcon.TargetModelIcon, newID);
							Model.IsModified = true;
							DeleteEditorIfExists(aCMIcon);
							UpdateIconsNode();
							return;
						}
						catch (Exception)
						{
							return;
						}
					}
					return;
				}
				objToCopy = modelObject;
			}
			RenameFromObjectManager(node, modelObject.Name, objToCopy);
		}

		private bool DuplicateObjectCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (!(arg is ACMTreeNode aCMTreeNode))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (aCMTreeNode.NodeType == NodeType.Folder)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (aCMTreeNode.ModelObject == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			ACMObject modelObject = aCMTreeNode.ModelObject;
			if (modelObject == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMSubmodel)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMHelp)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMProcedure)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMScript)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (modelObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMText)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (modelObject.Type != ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomVariableType)
			{
				_ = modelObject.Type;
				_ = 1;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void DuplicateObjectCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			ACMTreeNode aCMTreeNode = obj as ACMTreeNode;
			ACMObject acmObjectInfo = aCMTreeNode.ModelObject;
			if (acmObjectInfo == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			ACMObject objToCopy;
			if (acmObjectInfo.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMTable)
			{
				ACMTable aCMTable = acmObjectInfo as ACMTable;
				objToCopy = new ACMTable(acmObjectInfo.ParentModel, acmObjectInfo.Name)
				{
					Variables = aCMTable.Variables,
					Attributes = aCMTable.Attributes,
					Description = aCMTable.Description,
					IsAllVariables = false,
					IsBuiltIn = false,
					IsADuplicate = true
				};
			}
			else if (acmObjectInfo.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMPlot)
			{
				ACMPlot aCMPlot = acmObjectInfo as ACMPlot;
				aCMPlot.LoadDefinition();
				objToCopy = new ACMPlot(acmObjectInfo.ParentModel, acmObjectInfo.Name)
				{
					Content = aCMPlot.Content,
					Is3D = aCMPlot.Is3D,
					Description = aCMPlot.Description,
					PlotInputFile = aCMPlot.PlotInputFile,
					ProfileDimensions = aCMPlot.ProfileDimensions,
					Profiles = aCMPlot.Profiles,
					ProfileVariables = aCMPlot.ProfileVariables,
					VariableNumbers = aCMPlot.VariableNumbers,
					XProfile = aCMPlot.XProfile,
					XVariables = aCMPlot.XVariables,
					YProfile = aCMPlot.YProfile,
					YVariables = aCMPlot.YVariables,
					IsADuplicate = true,
					IsBuiltIn = false
				};
			}
			else if (acmObjectInfo.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMScript)
			{
				ACMScript aCMScript = acmObjectInfo as ACMScript;
				aCMScript.Text = aCMScript.ParentModel.ACMDoc.GetScriptText(aCMScript.ParentModel.Name, aCMScript.Name);
				objToCopy = new ACMScript(acmObjectInfo.ParentModel, acmObjectInfo.Name, aCMScript.Text)
				{
					Description = aCMScript.Description,
					IsBuiltIn = false,
					IsADuplicate = true
				};
			}
			else if (acmObjectInfo.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMIcon)
			{
				objToCopy = acmObjectInfo;
			}
			else if (acmObjectInfo.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMProcedure)
			{
				ACMProcedure aCMProcedure = acmObjectInfo as ACMProcedure;
				aCMProcedure.Text = aCMProcedure.ParentModel.ACMDoc.GetProcedureText(aCMProcedure.Name);
				objToCopy = new ACMProcedure(acmObjectInfo.ParentModel, acmObjectInfo.Name, aCMProcedure.Text)
				{
					Description = aCMProcedure.Description,
					IsBuiltIn = false,
					IsADuplicate = true
				};
			}
			else if (acmObjectInfo.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomVariableType)
			{
				ACMCustomVariableType aCMCustomVariableType = acmObjectInfo as ACMCustomVariableType;
				aCMCustomVariableType.Text = aCMCustomVariableType.ParentModel.ACMDoc.GetModelText(aCMCustomVariableType.Name);
				objToCopy = new ACMCustomVariableType(acmObjectInfo.ParentModel, acmObjectInfo.Name, aCMCustomVariableType.Text)
				{
					IsADuplicate = true,
					InheritedType = aCMCustomVariableType.InheritedType,
					Value = aCMCustomVariableType.Value,
					Lower = aCMCustomVariableType.Lower,
					Upper = aCMCustomVariableType.Upper,
					PhyscialType = aCMCustomVariableType.PhyscialType,
					Units = aCMCustomVariableType.Units
				};
			}
			else if (acmObjectInfo.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomParameterType)
			{
				ACMCustomParameterType aCMCustomParameterType = acmObjectInfo as ACMCustomParameterType;
				aCMCustomParameterType.Text = aCMCustomParameterType.ParentModel.ACMDoc.GetModelText(aCMCustomParameterType.Name);
				objToCopy = new ACMCustomParameterType(acmObjectInfo.ParentModel, acmObjectInfo.Name, aCMCustomParameterType.Text)
				{
					IsADuplicate = true,
					ParameterType = aCMCustomParameterType.ParameterType,
					InheritedType = aCMCustomParameterType.InheritedType,
					Value = aCMCustomParameterType.Value,
					Lower = aCMCustomParameterType.Lower,
					Upper = aCMCustomParameterType.Upper,
					PhyscialType = aCMCustomParameterType.PhyscialType,
					Units = aCMCustomParameterType.Units
				};
			}
			else
			{
				objToCopy = acmObjectInfo;
			}
			ACMTreeNode aCMTreeNode2 = RootTreeNode.Children.FirstOrDefault((ACMTreeNode p) => p.ModelObject != null && p.ModelObject.GetType() == acmObjectInfo.GetType() && p.NodeType == NodeType.Folder);
			if (aCMTreeNode2 != null)
			{
				string defaultName = GenerateDefaultAcmObjectName(aCMTreeNode2.Children.Select((ACMTreeNode p) => p.ModelObject.Name), "DP_" + acmObjectInfo.Name);
				DuplicateFromObjectManager(aCMTreeNode2, defaultName, objToCopy);
			}
		}

		private string CreateNewVariableName(string name, string suffix, int maxLength = 256)
		{
			if (name.Length < maxLength - suffix.Length)
			{
				return name + suffix;
			}
			return name.Substring(0, maxLength - suffix.Length) + suffix;
		}

		private string GenerateDefaultAcmObjectName(IEnumerable<string> lstExistingName, string sNamePrefix)
		{
			int num = 0;
			string newName = null;
			do
			{
				newName = CreateNewVariableName(sNamePrefix, $"{++num}", 27);
			}
			while (lstExistingName != null && lstExistingName.Any((string p) => p.Equals(newName, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase)) && num < 2000);
			return newName;
		}

		private bool ExportObjectCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (!(arg is ACMTreeNode aCMTreeNode))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (aCMTreeNode.NodeType == NodeType.Folder)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (aCMTreeNode.ModelObject == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			ACMObject modelObject = aCMTreeNode.ModelObject;
			if (modelObject == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (modelObject.GetType() == typeof(ACMProcedure))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void ExportObjectCommand_Executed(object arg)
		{
			ACMObject modelObject = (arg as ACMTreeNode).ModelObject;
			if (modelObject != null && modelObject.GetType() == typeof(ACMProcedure))
			{
				Model.ACMDoc.GetWorkingDir(out var workingDir);
				ExportCodeDialog exportCodeDialog = new ExportCodeDialog
				{
					Owner = Window.GetWindow((DependencyObject)(object)this)
				};
				exportCodeDialog.WorkingDir = workingDir;
				if (exportCodeDialog.ShowDialog() == true)
				{
					ExportworkingDirectory = exportCodeDialog.WorkingDir;
					Model.ACMDoc.ExportProcedure(modelObject.ParentModel.Name, modelObject.Name, ExportworkingDirectory);
				}
			}
		}

		private bool GenerateCodeObjectCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (!(arg is ACMTreeNode aCMTreeNode))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (aCMTreeNode.NodeType == NodeType.Folder)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (aCMTreeNode.ModelObject == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			ACMObject modelObject = aCMTreeNode.ModelObject;
			if (modelObject == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (modelObject.GetType() == typeof(ACMProcedure))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void GenerateCodeObjectCommand_Executed(object arg)
		{
			ACMObject modelObject = (arg as ACMTreeNode).ModelObject;
			if (modelObject != null && modelObject.GetType() == typeof(ACMProcedure))
			{
				Model.ACMDoc.GetWorkingDir(out var workingDir);
				GenerateCodeDialog generateCodeDialog = new GenerateCodeDialog
				{
					Owner = Window.GetWindow((DependencyObject)(object)this)
				};
				generateCodeDialog.IsGenTemplChecked = generateTemplates;
				generateCodeDialog.IsGenWrappChecked = generateWrappers;
				generateCodeDialog.IsGenMfChecked = generateMakefile;
				generateCodeDialog.IsGenDebugChecked = generateDebugCode;
				generateCodeDialog.IsExeMfChecked = executeMakefile;
				generateCodeDialog.WorkingDir = workingDir;
				if (generateCodeDialog.ShowDialog() == true)
				{
					generateTemplates = generateCodeDialog.IsGenTemplChecked;
					generateWrappers = generateCodeDialog.IsGenWrappChecked;
					generateMakefile = generateCodeDialog.IsGenMfChecked;
					generateDebugCode = generateCodeDialog.IsGenDebugChecked;
					executeMakefile = generateCodeDialog.IsExeMfChecked;
					workingDirectory = generateCodeDialog.WorkingDir;
					Model.ACMDoc.GenerateProcedures(modelObject.ParentModel.Name, modelObject.Name, generateTemplates, generateWrappers, generateMakefile, generateDebugCode, executeMakefile, workingDirectory);
				}
			}
		}

		private bool MakeDefaultObjectCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (!(arg is ACMTreeNode aCMTreeNode))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (aCMTreeNode.NodeType == NodeType.Folder)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (aCMTreeNode.ModelObject == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			ACMObject modelObject = aCMTreeNode.ModelObject;
			if (modelObject == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (modelObject.GetType() == typeof(ACMIcon))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void MakeDefaultObjectCommand_Executed(object arg)
		{
			ACMObject modelObject = (arg as ACMTreeNode).ModelObject;
			if (modelObject != null && modelObject.GetType() == typeof(ACMIcon))
			{
				ACMIcon aCMIcon = modelObject as ACMIcon;
				Model.ACMDoc.SetDefaultIcon(Model.Name, aCMIcon.TargetModelIcon);
				Model.UpdateIcons();
				Model.IsModified = true;
				UpdateCompileButtonStatus(enable: true);
			}
		}

		private ACMModel OpenModel(ACMModelInfo modelInfo)
		{
			ACMModel aCMModel = new ACMModel(modelInfo);
			if (!aCMModel.NotEditable)
			{
				CMEHelper.HCheckOutACMEModel();
			}
			return aCMModel;
		}

		private void AddObjectToNavigationTree(ACMObject newAcmObject)
		{
			ACMTreeNode aCMTreeNode = RootTreeNode.Children.FirstOrDefault((ACMTreeNode p) => p.ModelObject != null && p.ModelObject.GetType() == newAcmObject.GetType() && p.NodeType == NodeType.Folder);
			if (aCMTreeNode != null)
			{
				ACMTreeNode aCMTreeNode2 = new ACMTreeNode
				{
					Text = newAcmObject.Name,
					ModelObject = newAcmObject,
					NodeType = NodeType.Form
				};
				aCMTreeNode2.IsSelected = true;
				List<string> list = aCMTreeNode.Children.Select((ACMTreeNode p) => p.ModelObject.Name).ToList();
				list.Add(aCMTreeNode2.ModelObject.Name);
				list.Sort();
				int index = list.IndexOf(aCMTreeNode2.ModelObject.Name);
				aCMTreeNode.Children.Insert(index, aCMTreeNode2);
			}
		}

		private void RemoveObjectFromNavigationTree(ACMObject deletedAcmObject)
		{
			ACMTreeNode aCMTreeNode = RootTreeNode.Children.FirstOrDefault((ACMTreeNode p) => p.ModelObject != null && p.ModelObject.GetType() == deletedAcmObject.GetType() && p.NodeType == NodeType.Folder);
			if (aCMTreeNode != null)
			{
				ACMTreeNode aCMTreeNode2 = aCMTreeNode.Children.FirstOrDefault((ACMTreeNode p) => p.ModelObject.Equals(deletedAcmObject));
				if (aCMTreeNode2 != null)
				{
					aCMTreeNode.Children.Remove(aCMTreeNode2);
				}
			}
		}

		private ObservableCollection<ACMTreeNode> GenerateNavigationTreeNodes()
		{
			return new ObservableCollection<ACMTreeNode>
			{
				new ACMTreeNode
				{
					Image = ImageResources.ModelText16,
					Text = CustomModelsResources.NavigationPanel_ModelText,
					ModelObject = new ACMText(Model, Model.Name, Model.ModelText),
					NodeType = NodeType.Form,
					IsSelected = true
				},
				GenerateHelpsNode(),
				GenerateSubModelsNode(),
				GenerateCustomParametersNode(),
				GenerateCustomVariablesNode(),
				GeneratePlotsNode(),
				GenerateTablesNode(),
				GenerateScriptsNode(),
				GenerateIconsNode(),
				GenerateProcuduresNode()
			};
		}

		public void UpdateIconsNode()
		{
			Model.UpdateIcons();
			ACMTreeNode aCMTreeNode = RootTreeNode.Children.FirstOrDefault((ACMTreeNode p) => p.NodeType == NodeType.Folder && p.ModelObject is ACMIcon);
			ACMTreeNode aCMTreeNode2 = GenerateIconsNode();
			if (aCMTreeNode == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			ACMTreeNode aCMTreeNode3 = null;
			List<ACMTreeNode> list = new List<ACMTreeNode>();
			foreach (ACMTreeNode node in aCMTreeNode.Children)
			{
				if (node.IsSelected && aCMTreeNode2.Children.FirstOrDefault((ACMTreeNode n) => n.Text == node.Text) != null)
				{
					aCMTreeNode3 = node;
				}
				else
				{
					list.Add(node);
				}
			}
			foreach (ACMTreeNode child in aCMTreeNode.Children)
			{
				if (child != aCMTreeNode3)
				{
					DeleteEditorIfExists(child.ModelObject);
				}
			}
			aCMTreeNode.Children.Clear();
			foreach (ACMTreeNode child2 in aCMTreeNode2.Children)
			{
				aCMTreeNode.Children.Add(child2);
				if (aCMTreeNode3 != null && child2.Text == aCMTreeNode3.Text)
				{
					aCMTreeNode3 = child2;
				}
			}
			if (aCMTreeNode3 != null)
			{
				aCMTreeNode3.IsSelected = true;
			}
			navigationTree.Items.Refresh();
			navigationTree.UpdateLayout();
		}

		private ACMTreeNode GenerateHelpsNode()
		{
			return new ACMTreeNode
			{
				Text = CustomModelsResources.NavigationPanel_ModelHelp,
				Children = new ObservableCollection<ACMTreeNode>(),
				ModelObject = Model.Help,
				NodeType = NodeType.Form,
				Image = ImageResources.ModelHelp16
			};
		}

		private ACMTreeNode GenerateIconsNode()
		{
			ACMTreeNode aCMTreeNode = new ACMTreeNode
			{
				Text = CustomModelsResources.NavigationPanel_Icons,
				Children = new ObservableCollection<ACMTreeNode>(),
				ModelObject = new ACMIcon(null, Model.Name),
				NodeType = NodeType.Folder
			};
			foreach (ACMIcon item in Model.Icons.OrderBy((ACMIcon icon) => icon.Name))
			{
				System.Windows.Controls.Image image = ModelLibraryLoader.CreateDrawingElement(item.TargetModelIcon, System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent, (double?)null, (double?)null) as System.Windows.Controls.Image;
				aCMTreeNode.Children.Add(new ACMTreeNode
				{
					Image = image.Source,
					Text = item.Name,
					ModelObject = item,
					NodeType = NodeType.Form
				});
			}
			return aCMTreeNode;
		}
