		private void NavigationTree_SelectedItemChanged(object sender, RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<object> e)
		{
			ACMTreeNode selectedNode = e.NewValue as ACMTreeNode;
			if (selectedNode == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			((DispatcherObject)this).Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Input, (Action)delegate
			{
				if (!((ScreenContentControl)this).IsClosed)
				{
					if (selectedNode.ModelObject != null && selectedNode.NodeType == NodeType.Form)
					{
						ShowForm(selectedNode.ModelObject);
					}
					else if (selectedNode.NodeType == NodeType.Folder)
					{
						if (selectedNode.ModelObject != null && (selectedNode.ModelObject.GetType() == typeof(ACMTable) || selectedNode.ModelObject.GetType() == typeof(ACMPlot) || selectedNode.ModelObject.GetType() == typeof(ACMScript) || selectedNode.ModelObject.GetType() == typeof(ACMIcon) || selectedNode.ModelObject.GetType() == typeof(ACMSubmodel) || selectedNode.ModelObject.GetType() == typeof(ACMProcedure) || selectedNode.ModelObject.GetType() == typeof(ACMCustomParameterType) || selectedNode.ModelObject.GetType() == typeof(ACMCustomVariableType)))
						{
							ShowManager(selectedNode);
						}
						else
						{
							ShowForm(null);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						ShowForm(null);
					}
				}
			});
		}

		public void ShowForm(ACMObject acmObject)
		{
			if (rightPanel.Content is IAcmEditor acmEditor)
			{
				acmEditor.OnUnloaded();
			}
			IAcmEditor acmEditor2 = CreateEditorIfMissing(acmObject);
			rightPanel.Content = acmEditor2;
			if (acmEditor2 != null)
			{
				acmEditor2.OnLoaded();
				bool enableCompileBtn = acmEditor2.GetEnableCompileBtn();
				UpdateCompileButtonStatus(enableCompileBtn);
			}
		}

		private IAcmEditor CreateEditorIfMissing(ACMObject acmObject)
		{
			if (acmObject != null)
			{
				if (acmObject is ACMIcon { IsBuiltIn: not false })
				{
					return null;
				}
				Type type = acmObject.GetType();
				if (AllEditors.ContainsKey(type))
				{
					Collection<IAcmEditor> collection = AllEditors[type];
					IAcmEditor acmEditor = collection.FirstOrDefault((IAcmEditor control) => acmObject.Equals(control.AcmObject));
					if (acmEditor == null)
					{
						acmEditor = AcmEditorCreator.CreateEditor(acmObject);
						if (acmEditor != null)
						{
							collection.Add(acmEditor);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						acmEditor.OnLoaded();
					}
					return acmEditor;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private void ShowManager(ACMTreeNode node)
		{
			if (rightPanel.Content is IAcmEditor acmEditor)
			{
				acmEditor.OnUnloaded();
			}
			IAcmObjectmanager acmObjectmanager = CreateObjetManagerIfMissing(node);
			rightPanel.Content = acmObjectmanager;
			if (acmObjectmanager != null)
			{
				acmObjectmanager.OnLoaded();
				bool isModified = Model.IsModified;
				UpdateCompileButtonStatus(isModified);
			}
		}

		private IAcmObjectmanager CreateObjetManagerIfMissing(ACMTreeNode node)
		{
			return new AcmObjectManager(Model, this, node);
		}

		protected internal void SelectFormFromObjectManager(ACMObject obj)
		{
			foreach (ACMTreeNode child in RootTreeNode.Children)
			{
				child.IsSelected = false;
				if (child.NodeType != NodeType.Folder)
				{
					continue;
				}
				foreach (ACMTreeNode child2 in child.Children)
				{
					if (child2.NodeType == NodeType.Form)
					{
						child2.IsSelected = false;
					}
				}
			}
			ACMTreeNode aCMTreeNode = RootTreeNode.Children.FirstOrDefault((ACMTreeNode p) => p.ModelObject != null && p.ModelObject.GetType() == obj.GetType() && p.NodeType == NodeType.Folder);
			if (aCMTreeNode != null)
			{
				ACMTreeNode aCMTreeNode2 = aCMTreeNode.Children.FirstOrDefault((ACMTreeNode p) => p.ModelObject != null && p.ModelObject.Name == obj.Name && p.NodeType == NodeType.Form);
				aCMTreeNode.IsSelected = true;
				aCMTreeNode2.IsSelected = true;
			}
			else
			{
				RootTreeNode.Children.FirstOrDefault((ACMTreeNode p) => p.ModelObject != null && p.ModelObject.Name == obj.Name && p.NodeType == NodeType.Form && p.ModelObject.Name == obj.Name).IsSelected = true;
			}
			navigationTree.Items.Refresh();
			navigationTree.UpdateLayout();
		}

		protected internal void DeleteFromObjectManager(ACMObject obj)
		{
			ACMTreeNode arg = RootTreeNode.Children.FirstOrDefault((ACMTreeNode p) => p.ModelObject != null && p.ModelObject.GetType() == obj.GetType() && p.NodeType == NodeType.Folder).Children.FirstOrDefault((ACMTreeNode p) => p.ModelObject != null && p.ModelObject.Name == obj.Name && p.NodeType == NodeType.Form);
			if (DeleteObjectCommand_CanExecute(arg))
			{
				DeleteObjectCommand_Executed(arg);
				Model.IsModified = true;
				UpdateCompileButtonStatus(enable: true);
			}
		}

		public void UpdateCompileButtonStatus(bool enable)
		{
			((UIElement)(object)btnCompile).IsEnabled = enable;
		}

		protected internal void AddNewFromObjectManager(ACMTreeNode node)
		{
			if (AddObjectCommand_CanExecute(node))
			{
				AddObjectCommand_Executed(node);
			}
		}

		protected internal void DuplicateFromObjectManager(ACMTreeNode node, string defaultName, ACMObject objToCopy)
		{
			//IL_003a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0040: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (!AddObjectCommand_CanExecute(node))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (node == null || node.NodeType != NodeType.Folder)
			{
				return;
			}
			OneIdDialog val = new OneIdDialog(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, CustomModelsResources.NavigationPanel_Name, defaultName, (string[])null, (maskType)7, CustomModelsResources.NavigationPanel_Create, (int?)27);
			if (((Window)(object)val).ShowDialog() != true)
			{
				return;
			}
			string name = (objToCopy.Name = val.NewID);
			try
			{
				ACMObject aCMObject = Model.Add(node.ModelObject.Type, name, objToCopy);
				if (aCMObject is ACMIcon)
				{
					UpdateIconsNode();
				}
				else
				{
					CreateEditorIfMissing(aCMObject);
					if (aCMObject != null)
					{
						AddObjectToNavigationTree(aCMObject);
						ShowForm(aCMObject);
					}
				}
				Model.IsModified = true;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		protected internal void RenameFromObjectManager(ACMTreeNode node, string defaultName, ACMObject objToCopy)
		{
			//IL_0059: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005f: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0104: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_029d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!AddObjectCommand_CanExecute(node))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (node == null || node.NodeType != NodeType.Folder)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = string.Format(Resources.RenameConfirmPrompt, objToCopy.Name);
			OneIdDialog val = new OneIdDialog(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, text, defaultName, (string[])null, (maskType)7, Resources.strRenameChild, (int?)27);
			if (((Window)(object)val).ShowDialog() != true)
			{
				return;
			}
			string newID = val.NewID;
			if (newID == objToCopy.Name)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (Model.ACMDoc.IsKeyword(newID) || newID.Equals("IntegerParameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) || newID.Equals("RealParameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) || newID.Equals("StringParameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) || newID.Equals("LogicalParameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(CustomModelsResources.VarNameError_IsKeyword, MMVersionHelper.MVGetProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)16, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			if (objToCopy.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMSubmodel || objToCopy.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomParameterType || objToCopy.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomVariableType || objToCopy.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMProcedure)
			{
				foreach (ACMSubmodel subModel in Model.SubModels)
				{
					if (newID == subModel.Name)
					{
						flag = true;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					foreach (ACMCustomParameterType customParameterType in Model.CustomParameterTypes)
					{
						if (newID == customParameterType.Name)
						{
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					foreach (ACMCustomVariableType customVariableType in Model.CustomVariableTypes)
					{
						if (newID == customVariableType.Name)
						{
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					foreach (ACMProcedure procedure in Model.Procedures)
					{
						if (newID == procedure.Name)
						{
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					string text2 = string.Format(CustomModelsResources.TypeDefinitionAlreadyExists, newID);
					string aCMObjectManager_Rename = CustomModelsResources.ACMObjectManager_Rename;
					MMMessageBox.Show(text2, aCMObjectManager_Rename, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
					return;
				}
			}
			if (objToCopy.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMProcedure)
			{
				objToCopy.ParentModel.ACMDoc.DeleteProcedure(objToCopy.ParentModel.Name, defaultName, out var succeeded);
				if (!succeeded)
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (objToCopy is ACMText aCMText && string.IsNullOrEmpty(aCMText.Text))
				{
					aCMText.Text = Model.ACMDoc.GetModelText(defaultName);
				}
				Model.ACMDoc.RemoveSubModel(defaultName);
			}
			try
			{
				ACMTreeNode aCMTreeNode = RootTreeNode.Children.FirstOrDefault((ACMTreeNode p) => p.ModelObject != null && p.ModelObject.GetType() == objToCopy.GetType() && p.NodeType == NodeType.Folder).Children.FirstOrDefault((ACMTreeNode p) => p.ModelObject != null && p.ModelObject.Name == objToCopy.Name && p.NodeType == NodeType.Form);
				if (DeleteObjectCommand_CanExecute(aCMTreeNode) && aCMTreeNode != null && aCMTreeNode.NodeType == NodeType.Form && !aCMTreeNode.ModelObject.IsBuiltIn)
				{
					Model.Remove(aCMTreeNode.ModelObject);
					RemoveObjectFromNavigationTree(aCMTreeNode.ModelObject);
					DeleteEditorIfExists(aCMTreeNode.ModelObject);
				}
				objToCopy.Name = newID;
				ACMObject addedAcmObject = Model.Add(node.ModelObject.Type, newID, objToCopy);
				CreateEditorIfMissing(addedAcmObject);
				if (addedAcmObject != null)
				{
					ACMTreeNode aCMTreeNode2 = RootTreeNode.Children.FirstOrDefault((ACMTreeNode p) => p.ModelObject != null && p.ModelObject.GetType() == addedAcmObject.GetType() && p.NodeType == NodeType.Folder);
					if (aCMTreeNode2 != null)
					{
						ACMTreeNode item = new ACMTreeNode
						{
							Text = addedAcmObject.Name,
							ModelObject = addedAcmObject,
							NodeType = NodeType.Form
						};
						aCMTreeNode2.Children.Add(item);
					}
					Model.IsModified = true;
					UpdateCompileButtonStatus(enable: true);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		internal void DeleteEditorIfExists(ACMObject acmObject)
		{
			if (acmObject == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			Type type = acmObject.GetType();
			if (AllEditors.ContainsKey(type))
			{
				Collection<IAcmEditor> collection = AllEditors[type];
				IAcmEditor acmEditor = collection.FirstOrDefault((IAcmEditor control) => acmObject.Equals(control.AcmObject));
				if (acmEditor != null)
				{
					collection.Remove(acmEditor);
					acmEditor.OnClosed();
					acmEditor = null;
				}
			}
		}

		private void NavigationExpander_Collapsed(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			((FrameworkElement)(object)navigationBorder).Tag = ((FrameworkElement)(object)navigationBorder).ActualWidth;
			((FrameworkElement)(object)navigationBorder).Width = double.NaN;
			navigationBorder.IsResizeEnable = false;
		}

		private void NavigationExpander_Expanded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (((FrameworkElement)(object)navigationBorder).Tag != null)
			{
				((FrameworkElement)(object)navigationBorder).Width = (double)((FrameworkElement)(object)navigationBorder).Tag;
				navigationBorder.IsResizeEnable = true;
			}
		}

		private void messageExpander_Collapsed(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			((FrameworkElement)(object)messageBorder).Tag = ((FrameworkElement)(object)messageBorder).ActualHeight;
			((FrameworkElement)(object)messageBorder).Height = double.NaN;
			messageBorder.IsResizeEnable = false;
		}

		private void messageExpander_Expanded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (((FrameworkElement)(object)messageBorder).Tag != null)
			{
				((FrameworkElement)(object)messageBorder).Height = (double)((FrameworkElement)(object)messageBorder).Tag;
			}
			else
			{
				((FrameworkElement)(object)messageBorder).Height = 200.0;
			}
			messageBorder.IsResizeEnable = true;
		}

		private void OnPropertyChanged(string propName)
		{
			this.PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
		}

		private void UpdateModel()
		{
			if (CreateEditorIfMissing(new ACMText(Model, Model.Name, Model.ModelText)) is TextEditor textEditor)
			{
				textEditor.SaveTextToModel();
			}
		}

		private void SetModelDirty(bool isDirty)
		{
			if (!isDirty && CreateEditorIfMissing(new ACMText(Model, Model.Name, Model.ModelText)) is TextEditor textEditor)
			{
				textEditor.SetSavePoint();
			}
		}

		private bool IsScriptsModified()
		{
			bool result = false;
			Dictionary<Type, Collection<IAcmEditor>> allEditors = _allEditors;
			if (allEditors == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (allEditors.TryGetValue(typeof(ACMScript), out var value) && value.Count > 0)
			{
				_ = Model.Name;
				_ = Model.Scripts;
				foreach (TextEditor item in value)
				{
					if (item.IsModified)
					{
						result = true;
						return result;
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private bool IsModified()
		{
			if (Model.IsModified)
			{
				return true;
			}
			bool result = true;
			if (CreateEditorIfMissing(new ACMText(Model, Model.Name, Model.ModelText)) is TextEditor textEditor)
			{
				result = textEditor.IsModified;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void SaveEditedScripts()
		{
			if (!_allEditors.TryGetValue(typeof(ACMScript), out var value) || value.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			string name = Model.Name;
			_ = Model.Scripts;
			foreach (TextEditor item in value)
			{
				string editorText = item.axEdCtrl.GetEditorText();
				string name2 = ((IAcmEditor)item).AcmObject.Name;
				Model.ACMDoc.SetScriptText(name, name2, editorText);
				item.axEdCtrl.SetSavePoint();
			}
		}

		private void SaveCommand_Executed(object args)
		{
			//IL_004d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0060: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_008f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0429: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0433: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_011c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0122: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			System.Windows.Input.Cursor overrideCursor = Mouse.OverrideCursor;
			Mouse.OverrideCursor = System.Windows.Input.Cursors.Wait;
			if (!Directory.Exists(ACMModelHelper.FolderForSavingModelInProgress))
			{
				Directory.CreateDirectory(ACMModelHelper.FolderForSavingModelInProgress);
			}
			string text = Path.Combine(ACMModelHelper.FolderForSavingModelInProgress, Model.Name + ".atmlz");
			if (((int)Model.ModelType == 0 || (int)Model.ModelType == 2) && ACMModelHelper.ModelLocked(text))
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(CustomModelsResources.ACMModelLocked, Model.Name), MMVersionHelper.MVGetProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
				Mouse.OverrideCursor = overrideCursor;
				ForceCloseScreen();
				return;
			}
			messageExpander.IsExpanded = true;
			foreach (Collection<IAcmEditor> value in AllEditors.Values)
			{
				foreach (IAcmEditor item in value)
				{
					item.Save();
				}
			}
			if (IsModified() || (int)Model.ModelType == 2)
			{
				Model.sCompiled = "0";
			}
			else
			{
				Model.sCompiled = "1";
			}
			string sCompiled = Model.sCompiled;
			Model.ACMDoc.SetDocIsSaving(1);
			if (sCompiled == "1")
			{
				Model.ACMDoc.SetDocIsCompiled(1);
			}
			if (sCompiled == "0")
			{
				Model.ACMDoc.SetDocIsCompiled(0);
			}
			if (Model.SaveDocument(Model.ModelPath))
			{
				SetModelDirty(isDirty: false);
				Model.Locked = false;
				Model.ACMDoc.GetWorkingDir(out var workingDir);
				foreach (ACMProcedure procedure in Model.Procedures)
				{
					Model.ACMDoc.GetProcdllName(Model.Name, procedure.Name, out var workingDir2);
					workingDir2 += ".dll";
					try
					{
						IEnumerable<string> enumerable = Directory.EnumerateFiles(workingDir, workingDir2, SearchOption.AllDirectories);
						if (!enumerable.Any())
						{
							workingDir = Directory.GetParent(Environment.CurrentDirectory).ToString();
							enumerable = Directory.EnumerateFiles(workingDir, workingDir2, SearchOption.AllDirectories);
						}
						foreach (string item2 in enumerable)
						{
							string destFileName = Path.Combine(path2: item2.Substring(workingDir.Length + 1), path1: Path.GetDirectoryName(Model.ModelPath));
							File.Copy(item2, destFileName, overwrite: true);
						}
					}
					catch (Exception ex)
					{
						Console.WriteLine(ex.Message);
					}
				}
				if (Model.ExportModel(Model.ModelTypeName, text, Model.ModelPath))
				{
					bool flag = false;
					foreach (Collection<IAcmEditor> value2 in AllEditors.Values)
					{
						foreach (IAcmEditor item3 in value2)
						{
							if (item3 is HelpEditor && ((HelpEditor)item3).isHelpModified)
							{
								((HelpEditor)item3).isHelpModified = false;
								flag = true;
								break;
							}
						}
					}
					if (flag)
					{
						object obj = ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_OpenModelFile(text);
						string fileNameWithoutExtension = Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(text);
						string[] files = Directory.GetFiles(Model.ModelTempFolder + "\\AM_" + fileNameWithoutExtension, "*.htmlz");
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(files[0]))
						{
							ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_SetModelProperty(obj, "Help", (object)files[0]);
							ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_ModelSaveTo(obj, text, true);
						}
						ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_CloseModelFile(obj);
					}
				}
				Model.ModelAtmlzPath = text;
				Model.ModelType = (ModelType)1;
				ScreenSubject screenSubject = ((ScreenContentControl)this).ScreenSubject;
				ScreenSubject obj2 = ((screenSubject is CustomModelsEditorScreenSubject) ? screenSubject : null);
				((CustomModelsEditorScreenSubject)obj2).ModelInfo = new ACMModelInfo(Model.ModelAtmlzPath, (ModelType)1, (object)null);
				((CustomModelsEditorScreenSubject)obj2).ModelInfo.Compiled = sCompiled;
				Model.Locked = true;
				((CompositePresentationEvent<EventArgs>)(object)UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<RefreshACMModelManagerEvent>()).Publish(new EventArgs());
				UpdateIconsNode();
			}
			Model.ACMDoc.SetDocIsSaving(0);
			Model.ACMDoc.SetDocIsCompiled(-1);
			Mouse.OverrideCursor = overrideCursor;
		}

		private bool CompileAllModels()
		{
			Dictionary<string, bool> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, bool>();
			foreach (Collection<IAcmEditor> value5 in AllEditors.Values)
			{
				foreach (IAcmEditor item in value5)
				{
					dictionary[item.AcmObject.Name] = item.IsModified;
					if (item.IsModified)
					{
						item.Save();
					}
				}
			}
			bool result = true;
			foreach (ACMCustomParameterType item2 in Model.CustomParameterTypes.OrderBy((ACMCustomParameterType x) => x.Name))
			{
				if (!Model.dicSubModNameCompFlag.ContainsKey(item2.Name))
				{
					Model.dicSubModNameCompFlag[item2.Name] = false;
				}
				if (!Model.dicSubModNameCompModText.ContainsKey(item2.Name))
				{
					Model.dicSubModNameCompModText[item2.Name] = string.Empty;
				}
				if (!Model.dicSubModNameCompFlag[item2.Name] || (dictionary.TryGetValue(item2.Name, out var value) && value))
				{
					LogMessage($" ");
					string modelText = Model.ACMDoc.GetModelText(item2.Name);
					if (!Model.CompileSubModel(item2.Name, modelText))
					{
						Model.dicSubModNameCompFlag[item2.Name] = false;
						result = false;
					}
					else
					{
						Model.dicSubModNameCompFlag[item2.Name] = true;
						Model.dicSubModNameCompModText[item2.Name] = modelText;
					}
				}
			}
			foreach (ACMCustomVariableType item3 in Model.CustomVariableTypes.OrderBy((ACMCustomVariableType x) => x.Name))
			{
				if (!Model.dicSubModNameCompFlag.ContainsKey(item3.Name))
				{
					Model.dicSubModNameCompFlag[item3.Name] = false;
				}
				if (!Model.dicSubModNameCompModText.ContainsKey(item3.Name))
				{
					Model.dicSubModNameCompModText[item3.Name] = string.Empty;
				}
				if (!Model.dicSubModNameCompFlag[item3.Name] || (dictionary.TryGetValue(item3.Name, out var value2) && value2))
				{
					LogMessage($" ");
					string modelText2 = Model.ACMDoc.GetModelText(item3.Name);
					if (!Model.CompileSubModel(item3.Name, modelText2))
					{
						Model.dicSubModNameCompFlag[item3.Name] = false;
						result = false;
					}
					else
					{
						Model.dicSubModNameCompFlag[item3.Name] = true;
						Model.dicSubModNameCompModText[item3.Name] = modelText2;
					}
				}
			}
			foreach (string item4 in Model.submodelList.OrderBy((string x) => x))
			{
				if (!Model.dicSubModNameCompFlag.ContainsKey(item4))
				{
					Model.dicSubModNameCompFlag[item4] = false;
				}
				if (!Model.dicSubModNameCompModText.ContainsKey(item4))
				{
					Model.dicSubModNameCompModText[item4] = string.Empty;
				}
				if (!Model.dicSubModNameCompFlag[item4] || (dictionary.TryGetValue(item4, out var value3) && value3))
				{
					LogMessage($" ");
					string modelText3 = Model.ACMDoc.GetModelText(item4);
					if (!Model.CompileSubModel(item4, modelText3))
					{
						Model.dicSubModNameCompFlag[item4] = false;
						result = false;
					}
					else
					{
						Model.dicSubModNameCompFlag[item4] = true;
						Model.dicSubModNameCompModText[item4] = modelText3;
					}
				}
			}
			foreach (ACMProcedure item5 in Model.Procedures.OrderBy((ACMProcedure x) => x.Name))
			{
				if (!Model.dicSubModNameCompFlag.ContainsKey(item5.Name))
				{
					Model.dicSubModNameCompFlag[item5.Name] = false;
				}
				if (!Model.dicSubModNameCompModText.ContainsKey(item5.Name))
				{
					Model.dicSubModNameCompModText[item5.Name] = string.Empty;
				}
				if (!Model.dicSubModNameCompFlag[item5.Name] || (dictionary.TryGetValue(item5.Name, out var value4) && value4))
				{
					LogMessage($" ");
					string modelText4 = Model.ACMDoc.GetModelText(item5.Name);
					if (!Model.CompileSubModel(item5.Name, modelText4))
					{
						Model.dicSubModNameCompFlag[item5.Name] = false;
						result = false;
					}
					else
					{
						Model.dicSubModNameCompFlag[item5.Name] = true;
						Model.dicSubModNameCompModText[item5.Name] = modelText4;
					}
				}
			}
			LogMessage($" ");
			string modelTypeName = Model.ModelTypeName;
			string modelText5 = Model.ACMDoc.GetModelText(modelTypeName);
			if (!Model.CompileSubModel(modelTypeName, modelText5))
			{
				result = false;
			}
			else
			{
				Model.CompiledModelText = modelText5;
			}
			LogMessage($" ");
			return result;
		}

		private void CompileCommand_Executed(object args)
		{
			System.Windows.Input.Cursor overrideCursor = Mouse.OverrideCursor;
			Mouse.OverrideCursor = System.Windows.Input.Cursors.Wait;
			messageExpander.IsExpanded = true;
			bool num = CompileAllModels();
			if (!num)
			{
				Model.IsModified = true;
			}
			else
			{
				Model.IsModified = false;
			}
			RefreshTypesForAllEditors();
			RefreshVariableAndParameterTypes();
			SetModelDirty(isDirty: false);
			if (num)
			{
				Model.sCompiled = "1";
			}
			else
			{
				Model.sCompiled = "0";
			}
			SaveCommand_Executed(null);
			Mouse.OverrideCursor = overrideCursor;
		}

		private bool InstallCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (IsScriptsModified())
			{
				return false;
			}
			bool num = IsModified();
			string sCompiled = Model.sCompiled;
			if (!num && sCompiled == "1")
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void RefreshTypesForAllEditors()
		{
			foreach (Collection<IAcmEditor> value in AllEditors.Values)
			{
				foreach (IAcmEditor item in value)
				{
					if (item is TextEditor textEditor && textEditor.expander1.Content is ModelAssistant modelAssistant)
					{
						modelAssistant.RefreshAllTypeList();
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void RefreshVariableAndParameterTypes()
		{
			foreach (ACMCustomVariableType customVariableType in Model.CustomVariableTypes)
			{
				Model.ReadVariableTypeProperties(customVariableType);
			}
			foreach (ACMCustomParameterType customParameterType in Model.CustomParameterTypes)
			{
				Model.ReadParameterTypeProperties(customParameterType);
			}
		}

		private void InstallCommand_Executed(object args)
		{
			//IL_0313: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_031d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			System.Windows.Input.Cursor overrideCursor = Mouse.OverrideCursor;
			Mouse.OverrideCursor = System.Windows.Input.Cursors.Wait;
			messageExpander.IsExpanded = true;
			if (!CompileAllModels())
			{
				LogMessage(string.Format(CustomModelsResources.UnableDeployDueToCompilationErrors, Model.Name));
				return;
			}
			if (Model.SaveDocument(Model.ModelPath))
			{
				SetModelDirty(isDirty: false);
				if (!Directory.Exists(ACMModelHelper.FolderForSavingModelInProgress))
				{
					Directory.CreateDirectory(ACMModelHelper.FolderForSavingModelInProgress);
				}
				Model.Locked = false;
				Model.ModelAtmlzPath = Path.Combine(ACMModelHelper.FolderForSavingModelInProgress, Model.Name + ".atmlz");
				Model.ACMDoc.GetWorkingDir(out var workingDir);
				foreach (ACMProcedure procedure in Model.Procedures)
				{
					Model.ACMDoc.GetProcdllName(Model.Name, procedure.Name, out var workingDir2);
					workingDir2 += ".dll";
					try
					{
						IEnumerable<string> enumerable = Directory.EnumerateFiles(workingDir, workingDir2, SearchOption.AllDirectories);
						if (!enumerable.Any())
						{
							workingDir = Directory.GetParent(Environment.CurrentDirectory).ToString();
							enumerable = Directory.EnumerateFiles(workingDir, workingDir2, SearchOption.AllDirectories);
						}
						foreach (string item in enumerable)
						{
							string destFileName = Path.Combine(path2: item.Substring(workingDir.Length + 1), path1: Path.GetDirectoryName(Model.ModelPath));
							File.Copy(item, destFileName, overwrite: true);
						}
					}
					catch (Exception ex)
					{
						Console.WriteLine(ex.Message);
					}
				}
				if (Model.ExportModel(Model.ModelTypeName, Model.ModelAtmlzPath, Model.ModelPath))
				{
					object obj = ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_OpenModelFile(Model.ModelAtmlzPath);
					if (obj != null)
					{
						ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_SetModelProperty(obj, "Compiled", (object)"1");
						ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_SetModelProperty(obj, "Description", (object)Model.sDescription);
						ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_ModelSaveTo(obj, Model.ModelAtmlzPath, true);
						ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_CloseModelFile(obj);
						ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_CloseModel(Model.Name);
						CMEHelper.CloseModelInEngine(Model.Name);
						if (ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_InstallModel(Model.ModelAtmlzPath, true))
						{
							CMEHelper.UpdateModelInEngine(Model.Name);
							ACMModelInfo val = ACMModelHelper.GetAllACMModels().FirstOrDefault((ACMModelInfo model) => model.ModelName.Equals(Model.Name, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) && model.ModelForEdit != null);
							if (val != null)
							{
								ACMModelHelper.DeleteModel(val.ModelForEdit);
								val.ModelForEdit = null;
								Model.ModelType = (ModelType)0;
								Model.ModelAtmlzPath = Path.Combine(ACMModelHelper.ModelInstalledPath, MMVersionHelper.MVGetVersionStringM(), Path.GetFileName(Model.ModelAtmlzPath));
								ScreenSubject screenSubject = ((ScreenContentControl)this).ScreenSubject;
								((CustomModelsEditorScreenSubject)((screenSubject is CustomModelsEditorScreenSubject) ? screenSubject : null)).ModelInfo = new ACMModelInfo(Model.ModelAtmlzPath, (ModelType)0, (object)null);
							}
							LogMessage(string.Format(CustomModelsResources.DeploySucceeded, Model.Name));
							RefreshModelPalette();
							Mouse.OverrideCursor = overrideCursor;
							return;
						}
					}
				}
				Model.Locked = true;
			}
			LogMessage(string.Format(CustomModelsResources.DeployFailed, Model.Name));
			Mouse.OverrideCursor = overrideCursor;
		}

		private void RefreshModelPalette()
		{
			//IL_0037: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0041: Expected O, but got Unknown
			CMEHelper.RefreshACMModelLibrary(Model.Name, 0);
			IEventAggregator obj = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
			((CompositePresentationEvent<EventArgs>)(object)obj.GetEvent<RefreshModelPaletteEvent>()).Publish(new EventArgs());
			((CompositePresentationEvent<RefreshPlotWizardRibbonEventArgs>)(object)obj.GetEvent<RefreshPlotWizardRibbonEvent>()).Publish(new RefreshPlotWizardRibbonEventArgs());
		}

		private bool SaveAsTemplateCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			return InstallCommand.CanExecute(arg);
		}

		private void SaveAsTemplateCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			//IL_0109: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_006b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0070: Invalid comparison between I4 and Unknown
			//IL_00c5: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00ca: Invalid comparison between I4 and Unknown
			if (!Directory.Exists(ACMModelHelper.UserTemplateFolder))
			{
				Directory.CreateDirectory(ACMModelHelper.UserTemplateFolder);
			}
			string text = Path.Combine(ACMModelHelper.UserTemplateFolder, Model.Name + ".atmlz");
			if ((!File.Exists(text) || 1 == (int)MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow((DependencyObject)(object)this), string.Format(CustomModelsResources.AskOverwriteUserTemplate, Model.Name), MMVersionHelper.MVGetProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)1, (MMMessageBoxImage)32, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1)) && (!File.Exists(Path.Combine(ACMModelHelper.BuiltInTemplateFolder, Model.Name + ".atmlz")) || 1 == (int)MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow((DependencyObject)(object)this), string.Format(CustomModelsResources.BultinTempalteExists, Model.Name), MMVersionHelper.MVGetProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)1, (MMMessageBoxImage)32, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1)))
			{
				messageExpander.IsExpanded = true;
				try
				{
					File.Copy(Model.ModelAtmlzPath, text, overwrite: true);
				}
				catch (Exception ex)
				{
					MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow((DependencyObject)(object)this), ex.Message, MMVersionHelper.MVGetProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)0, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
					LogMessage(CustomModelsResources.SaveTemplateFailed);
					return;
				}
				LogMessage(CustomModelsResources.SaveTemplateSucceeded);
				((CompositePresentationEvent<EventArgs>)(object)UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UpdateACMModelTemplatesEvent>()).Publish(new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		private void CompleteKeyCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			TextEditor textEditor = rightPanel.Content as TextEditor;
			axEdCtrl = textEditor.axEdCtrl;
			TypeMode mode = TypeMode.Keyword;
			string text = "";
			TypeDefinitionWindowDialog(mode, text);
		}

		private void TypeDefinitionWindowDialog(TypeMode mode, string text)
		{
			if (_typeTypeDefinitionDlg != null && !_typeTypeDefinitionDlg.IsVisible)
			{
				_typeTypeDefinitionDlg = null;
			}
			if (_typeTypeDefinitionDlg == null)
			{
				_typeTypeDefinitionDlg = new TypeDefinition(mode, text, ref axEdCtrl)
				{
					Owner = Window.GetWindow((DependencyObject)(object)this)
				};
				if (_typeTypeDefinitionDlg._iVisual == 1)
				{
					_typeTypeDefinitionDlg.Show();
				}
			}
		}

		private bool BtnFindCommand_CanExcute(object arg)
		{
			if (rightPanel.Content is TextEditor)
			{
				bool num = ReplaceDlgVisible();
				bool flag = GotoDlgVisible();
				if (num || flag)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void BtnFindCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			LaunchFindAndReplaceDialogF(SearchMode.Find);
		}

		private void LaunchFindAndReplaceDialogF(SearchMode mode)
		{
			TextEditor textEditor = rightPanel.Content as TextEditor;
			axEdCtrl = textEditor.axEdCtrl;
			if (axEdCtrl == null)
			{
				_FindDlg = null;
				return;
			}
			if (_FindDlg != null && !_FindDlg.IsLoaded)
			{
				_FindDlg = null;
			}
			if (_FindDlg == null)
			{
				_FindDlg = new FindAndReplace(axEdCtrl)
				{
					Owner = Window.GetWindow((DependencyObject)(object)this)
				};
				_FindDlg.Show();
			}
			string selectedText = axEdCtrl.GetSelectedText();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(selectedText))
			{
				_FindDlg.SearchText = selectedText;
			}
			_FindDlg.SearchMode = mode;
			_FindDlg.Activate();
		}

		private bool BtnReplaceCommand_CanExcute(object arg)
		{
			if (rightPanel.Content is TextEditor)
			{
				bool num = FindDlgVisible();
				bool flag = GotoDlgVisible();
				if (num || flag)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void BtnReplaceCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			LaunchFindAndReplaceDialogR(SearchMode.Replace);
		}

		private void LaunchFindAndReplaceDialogR(SearchMode mode)
		{
			TextEditor textEditor = rightPanel.Content as TextEditor;
			axEdCtrl = textEditor.axEdCtrl;
			if (axEdCtrl == null)
			{
				_ReplaceDlg = null;
				return;
			}
			if (_ReplaceDlg != null && !_ReplaceDlg.IsLoaded)
			{
				_ReplaceDlg = null;
			}
			if (_ReplaceDlg == null)
			{
				_ReplaceDlg = new FindAndReplace(axEdCtrl)
				{
					Owner = Window.GetWindow((DependencyObject)(object)this)
				};
				_ReplaceDlg.Show();
			}
			string selectedText = axEdCtrl.GetSelectedText();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(selectedText))
			{
				_ReplaceDlg.SearchText = selectedText;
			}
			_ReplaceDlg.SearchMode = mode;
			_ReplaceDlg.Activate();
		}

		private bool BtnGotoLineCommand_CanExcute(object arg)
		{
			if (rightPanel.Content is TextEditor)
			{
				bool num = FindDlgVisible();
				bool flag = ReplaceDlgVisible();
				if (num || flag)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void BtnGotoLineCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			TextEditor textEditor = rightPanel.Content as TextEditor;
			axEdCtrl = textEditor.axEdCtrl;
			GotoLine gotoLine = new GotoLine(axEdCtrl.GetCurrentLine(), axEdCtrl.GetLineCount())
			{
				Owner = Window.GetWindow((DependencyObject)(object)this)
			};
			if (gotoLine.ShowDialog() == true)
			{
				textEditor.GotoLine(gotoLine.LineNumber);
			}
		}

		private bool FindDlgVisible()
		{
			if (_FindDlg != null && _FindDlg.IsVisible)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private bool ReplaceDlgVisible()
		{
			if (_ReplaceDlg != null && _ReplaceDlg.IsVisible)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private bool GotoDlgVisible()
		{
			if (_GotoLineDlg != null && _GotoLineDlg.IsVisible)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void MessagePanel_ContextMenuOpening(object sender, ContextMenuEventArgs e)
		{
			if (messagePanel.ContextMenu == null)
			{
				System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu contextMenu = new System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu();
				contextMenu.Items.Add(new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem
				{
					Header = CustomModelsResources.MessagePanelContextMenu_Clear,
					Command = ClearMessagesCommand,
					CommandTarget = messagePanel,
					InputGestureText = "Del",
					Name = "miClear"
				});
				contextMenu.Items.Add(new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem
				{
					Header = CustomModelsResources.TextEditorContextMenu_Copy,
					Command = ApplicationCommands.Copy,
					CommandTarget = messagePanel,
					Name = "miCopy"
				});
				contextMenu.Items.Add(new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem
				{
					Header = CustomModelsResources.TextEditorContextMenu_SelectAll,
					Command = ApplicationCommands.SelectAll,
					CommandTarget = messagePanel,
					Name = "miSelectAll"
				});
				messagePanel.ContextMenu = contextMenu;
			}
		}

		private void CopyOutputMessageCommand_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.CanExecute = messagePanel.SelectedItems != null && messagePanel.SelectedItems.Count > 0;
		}

		private void CopyOutputMessageCommand_Execute(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			foreach (object selectedItem in messagePanel.SelectedItems)
			{
				stringBuilder.AppendLine(selectedItem.ToString());
			}
			System.Windows.Clipboard.SetText(stringBuilder.ToString());
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private void SelectAllMessagesCommand_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.CanExecute = messagePanel.Items.Count > 0;
		}

		private void SelectAllMessagesCommand_Execute(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			messagePanel.SelectAll();
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private bool ClearMessagesCommand_CanExecute()
		{
			return messagePanel.Items.Count > 0;
		}

		private void ClearMessagesCommand_Executed()
		{
			messagePanel.Items.Clear();
		}

	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty DataProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Data", typeof(object), typeof(BindingProxy), new UIPropertyMetadata(null));

		public object Data
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(DataProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DataProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected override Freezable CreateInstanceCore()
		{
			return new BindingProxy();
		}

	public class CustomModelsModule : IModule
	{
		public IUnityContainer Container { get; private set; }

		public IScreenFactoryCatalog ScreenFactoryCatalog { get; private set; }

		public CustomModelsModule(IUnityContainer container, IScreenFactoryCatalog screenFactoryCatalog)
		{
			Container = container;
			ScreenFactoryCatalog = screenFactoryCatalog;
		}

		public void Initialize()
		{
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0038: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0042: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ScreenFactoryCatalog.RegisterScreenFactory((IScreenFactory)new DelegateScreenFactory(ScreenFactoryKeys.CustomModelsEditorScreenFactory, (Func<IUnityContainer, ScreenSubject, ScreenState, ScreenContentControl>)CustomModelsEditor.CreateScreen));
			ScreenFactoryCatalog.RegisterScreenFactory((IScreenFactory)new DelegateScreenFactory(ScreenFactoryKeys.CustomModelsObjectManagerScreenFactory, (Func<IUnityContainer, ScreenSubject, ScreenState, ScreenContentControl>)CustomModelsObjectManager.CreateScreen));
		}
	}
}

	public enum ACMAttribute
	{
		None,
		Name,
		Value,
		Spec,
		Derivative,
		Scale,
		Tag,
		Type,
		Record,
		Units,
		Description,
		Upper,
		Lower
	}
	public class ACMChart : ACMObject
	{
		public override ACMObjectTypes Type => ACMObjectTypes.ACMChart;

		public ACMChart(string name)
		{
			base.Name = name;
		}
	}
	public class ACMHelp : ACMObject
	{
		public string FileName { get; set; }

		public string sTempDir { get; set; }

		public override ACMObjectTypes Type => ACMObjectTypes.ACMHelp;

		public ACMHelp(ACMModel parentModel, string name)
		{
			base.Name = name;
			base.ParentModel = parentModel;
		}
	}
	public class ACMIcon : ACMObject
	{
		public string ModelName { get; private set; }

		public IAModelIcon TargetModelIcon { get; set; }

		public override ACMObjectTypes Type => ACMObjectTypes.ACMIcon;

		public ACMIcon(string iconName, string modelName, IAModelIcon modelIcon = null)
		{
			base.Name = iconName;
			ModelName = modelName;
			TargetModelIcon = modelIcon;
		}

		public override void Rename(string newName)
		{
			base.ParentModel.ACMDoc.RenameIcon(base.ParentModel.Name, TargetModelIcon, newName);
		}
	}
	public class ACMModel
	{
		public const string AllVariablesTable = "All Variables";

		public List<string> submodelList = new List<string>();

		public Dictionary<string, string> dicSubModNameCompModText = new Dictionary<string, string>();

		public Dictionary<string, bool> dicSubModNameCompFlag = new Dictionary<string, bool>();

		private FileStream _fsLock;

		private bool _modelNotEditable;

		public string Name { get; set; }

		public string ModelTypeName { get; set; }

		public ACMHelp Help { get; set; }

		public Collection<ACMChart> Charts { get; } = new Collection<ACMChart>();

		public Collection<ACMPlot> Plots { get; } = new Collection<ACMPlot>();

		public Collection<ACMScript> Scripts { get; } = new Collection<ACMScript>();

		public Collection<ACMTask> Tasks { get; } = new Collection<ACMTask>();

		public Collection<ACMIcon> Icons { get; } = new Collection<ACMIcon>();

		public Collection<ACMTable> Tables { get; } = new Collection<ACMTable>();

		public Collection<ACMCustomVariableType> CustomVariableTypes { get; } = new Collection<ACMCustomVariableType>();

		public Collection<ACMCustomParameterType> CustomParameterTypes { get; } = new Collection<ACMCustomParameterType>();

		public Collection<ACMProcedure> Procedures { get; } = new Collection<ACMProcedure>();

		public Collection<ACMSubmodel> SubModels { get; } = new Collection<ACMSubmodel>();

		internal ACMDocomentWrapper ACMDoc { get; private set; }

		public string ModelText { get; set; }

		public string CompiledModelText { get; set; }

		public string sCompiled { get; set; }

		public string sDescription { get; set; }

		public string ModelPath { get; private set; }

		public bool IsModified { get; set; }

		public string ModelTempFolder { get; set; }

		private object ACMExpModelInfoObject { get; set; }

		public string ModelAtmlzPath { get; set; }

		public ModelType ModelType { get; set; }

		public bool Locked
		{
			get
			{
				return _fsLock != null;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value)
				{
					if (_fsLock == null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(ModelAtmlzPath))
					{
						_fsLock = File.Open(ModelAtmlzPath, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read, FileShare.Read);
					}
				}
				else if (_fsLock != null)
				{
					_fsLock.Dispose();
					_fsLock = null;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool NotEditable
		{
			get
			{
				return _modelNotEditable;
			}
			set
			{
				_modelNotEditable = value;
			}
		}

		public ACMModel(ACMModelInfo modelInfo)
		{
			//IL_0123: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_012e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0134: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0137: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0179: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_017f: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_013f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0145: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_01a8: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01ae: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0264: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ACMModel aCMModel = this;
			Name = modelInfo.ModelName;
			ModelTypeName = modelInfo.ModelName;
			sCompiled = modelInfo.Compiled;
			sDescription = modelInfo.Description;
			ModelTempFolder = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), Path.GetRandomFileName());
			if (!Directory.Exists(ModelTempFolder))
			{
				Directory.CreateDirectory(ModelTempFolder);
			}
			ModelPath = Path.Combine(ModelTempFolder, Name + ".acmf");
			ModelType = modelInfo.ModelType;
			if ((int)modelInfo.ModelType == 1 || (int)modelInfo.ModelType == 0 || (int)modelInfo.ModelType == 3)
			{
				ACMExpModelInfoObject = modelInfo.ExpModelInfoObject;
				if (ACMExpModelInfoObject == null)
				{
					ACMExpModelInfoObject = ACMModelLibraryHelper.GetModelFileInfo(modelInfo.AtmlzFilePath);
				}
				ModelAtmlzPath = modelInfo.AtmlzFilePath;
				if ((int)modelInfo.ModelType == 1)
				{
					Locked = true;
				}
			}
			ACMDoc = (ACMDocomentWrapper)Activator.CreateInstance(Marshal.GetTypeFromCLSID(new Guid("4C4883CF-8401-42B1-B410-782C5E8A4708")));
			if ((int)modelInfo.ModelType == 2)
			{
				ACMDoc.NewDocument();
				ACMDoc.CreateModel(Name);
				Tables.Add(new ACMTable(this, "All Variables")
				{
					IsBuiltIn = true,
					IsAllVariables = true
				});
				ModelText = ACMDoc.GetModelText(Name);
				Help = new ACMHelp(this, Name + " Help");
				return;
			}
			object obj = ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_OpenModelFile(ModelAtmlzPath);
			if (!ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_GetModelCustomModelingFile(obj, ModelPath))
			{
				string text = string.Format(CustomModelsResources.ModelNotEditable, Name);
				string modelNotEditableTitle = CustomModelsResources.ModelNotEditableTitle;
				MMMessageBox.Show(text, modelNotEditableTitle, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
				NotEditable = true;
				return;
			}
			ACMModelHelper.GetModelProcedures(obj, ModelTempFolder);
			ACMModelHelper.GetModelHelpFile(obj, Path.Combine(ModelTempFolder, "AM_" + Name));
			if (!ACMDoc.OpenDocument(ModelPath))
			{
				NotEditable = true;
				return;
			}
			StringComparison comp = StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase;
			string text2 = "";
			string text3 = File.ReadAllText(ModelPath);
			string[] separator = new string[1] { "\r\n" };
			string[] array = text3.Split(separator, StringSplitOptions.None);
			string text4 = Array.Find(array, (string element) => element.IndexOf(aCMModel.Name, comp) >= 0);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
			{
				if (text4.IndexOf("Model", comp) >= 0 && text4.IndexOf(Name, comp) >= 0)
				{
					ModelText = ACMDoc.GetModelText(Name);
				}
				else
				{
					string text5 = Array.Find(array, (string element) => element.IndexOf("Model", comp) == 0);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text5))
					{
						string text6 = text5.Substring(5);
						text6 = text6.Trim();
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text6))
						{
							string modelText = ACMDoc.GetModelText(Name);
							if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(modelText))
							{
								ModelText = ACMDoc.GetModelText(text6);
								ModelTypeName = text6;
							}
							else
							{
								ModelText = modelText;
							}
						}
						else
						{
							string badNameNoNameOfModel = CustomModelsResources.BadNameNoNameOfModel;
							string badNameNoNameOfModel2 = CustomModelsResources.BadNameNoNameOfModel;
							System.Windows.MessageBox.Show(badNameNoNameOfModel, badNameNoNameOfModel2, MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Exclamation);
							ModelText = ACMDoc.GetModelText(Name);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			int num = array.Count();
			for (int num2 = 0; num2 < num; num2++)
			{
				array[num2] = array[num2].Trim();
				if (array[num2].StartsWith("Model ", comp))
				{
					text2 = array[num2].Substring(6);
					text2 = text2.Trim();
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(ACMDoc.GetModelText(text2)) && !text2.Equals(ModelTypeName, comp) && !submodelList.Contains(text2))
					{
						submodelList.Add(text2);
					}
				}
			}
			ReadAllProperties(obj);
			if (obj != null)
			{
				ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_CloseModelFile(obj);
			}
		}

		~ACMModel()
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(ModelTempFolder))
			{
				try
				{
					Directory.Delete(ModelTempFolder, recursive: true);
					return;
				}
				catch
				{
					return;
				}
			}
		}

		internal void ReadVariableTypeProperties(ACMCustomVariableType variableType)
		{
			string inheritedType = "RealVariable";
			string[] array = ACMDoc.GetModelText(variableType.Name).Split(new char[2] { ' ', '\n' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
			if (array.Count() >= 4 && string.Equals(array[2], "Uses", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				inheritedType = array[3];
			}
			variableType.InheritedType = inheritedType;
			ACMDoc.GetTypedAssignments(0, variableType.Name, out var _, out var value, out var sPhysicalQuantity, out var sUnits, out var lower, out var upper, out var _);
			variableType.Value = value;
			variableType.Lower = lower;
			variableType.Upper = upper;
			variableType.PhyscialType = sPhysicalQuantity;
			variableType.Units = sUnits;
		}

		internal void ReadParameterTypeProperties(ACMCustomParameterType parameter)
		{
			string text = "IntegerParameter";
			ParameterTypes parameterTypes = ParameterTypes.Integer;
			string[] array = ACMDoc.GetModelText(parameter.Name).Split(new char[2] { ' ', '\n' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
			if (array.Count() >= 4 && string.Equals(array[2], "Uses", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				text = array[3];
			}
			string text2 = text;
			while (true)
			{
				if (string.Equals(text2, "IntegerParameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					parameterTypes = ParameterTypes.Integer;
					break;
				}
				if (string.Equals(text2, "LogicalParameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					parameterTypes = ParameterTypes.Logical;
					break;
				}
				if (string.Equals(text2, "StringParameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					parameterTypes = ParameterTypes.String;
					break;
				}
				if (string.Equals(text2, "RealParameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					parameterTypes = ParameterTypes.Real;
					break;
				}
				array = ACMDoc.GetModelText(text2).Split(new char[2] { ' ', '\n' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
				if (array.Count() >= 4 && string.Equals(array[2], "Uses", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					text2 = array[3];
				}
			}
			parameter.InheritedType = text;
			parameter.ParameterType = parameterTypes;
			ACMDoc.GetParameterValues(parameter.Name, out var _, out var _, out var sUnits, out var sPhysicalQuantity, out var sValue, out var sUpper, out var sLower);
			parameter.Value = sValue;
			if (parameterTypes == ParameterTypes.Integer || parameterTypes == ParameterTypes.Real)
			{
				parameter.Lower = sLower;
				parameter.Upper = sUpper;
			}
			parameter.Units = sPhysicalQuantity;
			parameter.PhyscialType = sUnits;
		}

		private void ReadAllProperties(object oExpModelObject)
		{
			//IL_02eb: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_02f2: Expected O, but got Unknown
			string text = ACMModelLibraryHelper.ACM_GetModelProperty(oExpModelObject, "Plots");
			Tables.Add(new ACMTable(this, "All Variables")
			{
				IsBuiltIn = true,
				IsAllVariables = true
			});
			try
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
					xmlDocument.LoadXml(text);
					foreach (XmlNode item2 in xmlDocument.GetElementsByTagName("FORM"))
					{
						string value = item2.Attributes["NAME"].Value;
						string value2 = item2.Attributes["CLSID"].Value;
						string innerText = item2.InnerText;
						ACMObject aCMObject = CreateACMObject(value, value2, innerText);
						if (aCMObject != null)
						{
							if (aCMObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMTable)
							{
								ACMTable item = (aCMObject as ACMTable) ?? new ACMTable(this, value);
								Tables.Add(item);
							}
							else
							{
								Add(aCMObject.Type, aCMObject.Name, aCMObject);
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			UpdateIcons();
			string[] scriptList = ACMDoc.GetScriptList(ModelTypeName);
			foreach (string name in scriptList)
			{
				Scripts.Add(new ACMScript(this, name));
			}
			foreach (string submodel in submodelList)
			{
				SubModels.Add(new ACMSubmodel(this, submodel));
			}
			scriptList = ACMDoc.GetProcedureList(ModelTypeName);
			foreach (string name2 in scriptList)
			{
				Procedures.Add(new ACMProcedure(this, name2));
			}
			scriptList = ACMDoc.GetVariableTypesList();
			foreach (string name3 in scriptList)
			{
				ACMCustomVariableType aCMCustomVariableType = new ACMCustomVariableType(this, name3);
				ReadVariableTypeProperties(aCMCustomVariableType);
				CustomVariableTypes.Add(aCMCustomVariableType);
			}
			scriptList = ACMDoc.GetParameterTypesList();
			foreach (string name4 in scriptList)
			{
				ACMCustomParameterType aCMCustomParameterType = new ACMCustomParameterType(this, name4);
				ReadParameterTypeProperties(aCMCustomParameterType);
				CustomParameterTypes.Add(aCMCustomParameterType);
			}
			Help = new ACMHelp(this, Name + " Help");
			Help.FileName = ACMModelHelper.GetModelHelpFile(oExpModelObject, Path.Combine(ModelTempFolder, "AM_" + Name));
			Help.sTempDir = Path.Combine(ModelTempFolder, "AM_" + Name);
			if (Help.FileName == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			AspenPackage val = new AspenPackage();
			if (val.Open(Help.FileName, FileMode.Open))
			{
				string name5 = val.RootPart.Name;
				string empty = string.Empty;
				empty = Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(Help.FileName), name5);
				if (val.RootPart.SaveToFile(empty))
				{
					Help.FileName = empty;
				}
			}
			val.Close();
		}

		public void UpdateIcons()
		{
			Icons.Clear();
			object[] modelIcons = ACMDoc.GetModelIcons(ModelTypeName);
			string defaultIconName = ACMDoc.GetDefaultIconName(ModelTypeName);
			object[] array = modelIcons;
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				if (array[i] is IAModelIcon iAModelIcon)
				{
					byte[] array2 = new byte[64];
					iAModelIcon.GetName(ref array2[0], array2.Length);
					string stringFromBytes = GetStringFromBytes(array2);
					Icons.Add(new ACMIcon(stringFromBytes, Name, iAModelIcon)
					{
						IsBuiltIn = stringFromBytes.Equals("System"),
						IsDefault = stringFromBytes.Equals(defaultIconName)
					});
				}
			}
		}

		private static string GetStringFromBytes(byte[] bytes)
		{
			return Encoding.Default.GetString(bytes).TrimEnd(default(char));
		}

		private ACMObject CreateACMObject(string name, string clsid, string content)
		{
			return clsid switch
			{
				"{6BA76840-806B-11D0-BE51-0000C09984EF}" => new ACMTable(this, name), 
				"{40C692F8-9541-11D4-8062-00C04F89DD44}" => new ACMPlot(this, name)
				{
					Content = content
				}, 
				"{07946F1C-D69D-4927-A221-1D2EAC51B8D9}" => new ACMHelp(this, name), 
				_ => null, 
			};
		}

		public bool RemoveSubModel(ACMObject objectToBeRemoved, bool canDelete, ref string sModelLineName, ref string sLine)
		{
			bool result = false;
			if (objectToBeRemoved is ACMSubmodel)
			{
				string name = ((ACMSubmodel)objectToBeRemoved).Name;
				if (canDelete)
				{
					IsModified = true;
					result = true;
					string modelText = "Model " + name + "\nEnd\n";
					ACMDoc.SaveSubModelText(name, modelText);
					SubModels.Remove((ACMSubmodel)objectToBeRemoved);
					if ((long)ACMDoc.RemoveSubModel(name) == 1 && submodelList.Contains(name))
					{
						submodelList.Remove(name);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					result = false;
					if (!canDelete)
					{
						string editor_SubModelInUse = CustomModelsResources.Editor_SubModelInUse;
						editor_SubModelInUse = name + " " + editor_SubModelInUse;
						editor_SubModelInUse = editor_SubModelInUse + "\n\n" + sModelLineName + ":\n" + sLine;
						string editor_DeleteSubModelTitle = CustomModelsResources.Editor_DeleteSubModelTitle;
						System.Windows.MessageBox.Show(editor_SubModelInUse, editor_DeleteSubModelTitle, MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Exclamation);
					}
					else
					{
						string editor_SubModelInBadState = CustomModelsResources.Editor_SubModelInBadState;
						string editor_DeleteSubModelTitle2 = CustomModelsResources.Editor_DeleteSubModelTitle;
						System.Windows.MessageBox.Show(editor_SubModelInBadState, editor_DeleteSubModelTitle2, MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Exclamation);
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public void Remove(ACMObject objectToBeRemoved)
		{
			if (objectToBeRemoved.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMChart)
			{
				Charts.Remove((ACMChart)objectToBeRemoved);
			}
			else if (objectToBeRemoved.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMPlot)
			{
				Plots.Remove((ACMPlot)objectToBeRemoved);
				ACMDoc.DeleteCustomTable(ModelTypeName, objectToBeRemoved.Name);
			}
			else if (objectToBeRemoved.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMTable)
			{
				Tables.Remove((ACMTable)objectToBeRemoved);
				ACMDoc.DeleteCustomTable(ModelTypeName, objectToBeRemoved.Name);
			}
			else if (objectToBeRemoved.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMScript)
			{
				Scripts.Remove((ACMScript)objectToBeRemoved);
				ACMDoc.DeleteScript(ModelTypeName, objectToBeRemoved.Name);
			}
			else if (objectToBeRemoved.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMTask)
			{
				Tasks.Remove((ACMTask)objectToBeRemoved);
			}
			else if (objectToBeRemoved.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMIcon)
			{
				Icons.Remove((ACMIcon)objectToBeRemoved);
			}
			else if (objectToBeRemoved.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMProcedure)
			{
				Procedures.Remove((ACMProcedure)objectToBeRemoved);
			}
			else if (objectToBeRemoved.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomVariableType)
			{
				CustomVariableTypes.Remove((ACMCustomVariableType)objectToBeRemoved);
			}
			else if (objectToBeRemoved.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomParameterType)
			{
				CustomParameterTypes.Remove((ACMCustomParameterType)objectToBeRemoved);
			}
			IsModified = true;
		}

		public ACMObject Add(ACMObjectTypes objectType, string name, ACMObject objectToAdd = null)
		{
			ACMObject result = null;
			bool flag = false;
			Collection<string> collection = new Collection<string>();
			foreach (ACMSubmodel subModel in SubModels)
			{
				collection.Add(subModel.Name);
			}
			foreach (ACMCustomParameterType customParameterType in CustomParameterTypes)
			{
				collection.Add(customParameterType.Name);
			}
			foreach (ACMCustomVariableType customVariableType in CustomVariableTypes)
			{
				collection.Add(customVariableType.Name);
			}
			foreach (ACMProcedure procedure in Procedures)
			{
				collection.Add(procedure.Name);
			}
			switch (objectType)
			{
			case ACMObjectTypes.ACMHelp:
			{
				ACMHelp aCMHelp = (Help = (objectToAdd as ACMHelp) ?? new ACMHelp(this, name));
				result = aCMHelp;
				break;
			}
			case ACMObjectTypes.ACMChart:
			{
				ACMChart aCMChart = (objectToAdd as ACMChart) ?? new ACMChart(name);
				Charts.Add(aCMChart);
				result = aCMChart;
				break;
			}
			case ACMObjectTypes.ACMPlot:
			{
				ACMPlot aCMPlot = objectToAdd as ACMPlot;
				if (aCMPlot == null)
				{
					ACMDoc.CreateProfilePlot(ModelTypeName, name);
					aCMPlot = new ACMPlot(this, name);
				}
				else if (aCMPlot.IsADuplicate)
				{
					ACMDoc.CreateProfilePlot(ModelTypeName, name);
				}
				Plots.Add(aCMPlot);
				result = aCMPlot;
				break;
			}
			case ACMObjectTypes.ACMTable:
			{
				ACMTable aCMTable = (objectToAdd as ACMTable) ?? new ACMTable(this, name);
				ACMDoc.CreateCustomTable(ModelTypeName, name);
				Tables.Add(aCMTable);
				result = aCMTable;
				break;
			}
			case ACMObjectTypes.ACMScript:
				foreach (ACMScript script in Scripts)
				{
					if (script.Name.Equals(name, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
					{
						flag = true;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					string[] procedureList = ACMDoc.GetScriptList(ModelTypeName);
					for (int num = 0; num < procedureList.Length; num++)
					{
						if (procedureList[num].Equals(name, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
						{
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				if (!flag && name.Equals(CustomModelsResources.NavigationPanel_Scripts, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					string messageBoxText5 = string.Format(CustomModelsResources.TypeDefinitionAlreadyExists, name);
					string editor_ScriptMessageTitle = CustomModelsResources.Editor_ScriptMessageTitle;
					System.Windows.MessageBox.Show(messageBoxText5, editor_ScriptMessageTitle, MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Exclamation);
				}
				else
				{
					ACMScript aCMScript = (objectToAdd as ACMScript) ?? new ACMScript(this, name);
					ACMDoc.CreateScript(ModelTypeName, name);
					Scripts.Add(aCMScript);
					result = aCMScript;
				}
				break;
			case ACMObjectTypes.ACMSubmodel:
			{
				foreach (string item in collection)
				{
					if (item.Equals(name, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
					{
						flag = true;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					string messageBoxText3 = string.Format(CustomModelsResources.TypeDefinitionAlreadyExists, name);
					string editor_CreateSubModelTitle = CustomModelsResources.Editor_CreateSubModelTitle;
					System.Windows.MessageBox.Show(messageBoxText3, editor_CreateSubModelTitle, MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Exclamation);
					break;
				}
				ACMSubmodel aCMSubmodel = (objectToAdd as ACMSubmodel) ?? new ACMSubmodel(this, name);
				ACMDoc.CreateModel(name);
				if (!submodelList.Contains(name))
				{
					submodelList.Add(name);
				}
				SubModels.Add(aCMSubmodel);
				result = aCMSubmodel;
				break;
			}
			case ACMObjectTypes.ACMTask:
			{
				ACMTask aCMTask = (objectToAdd as ACMTask) ?? new ACMTask(name);
				Tasks.Add(aCMTask);
				result = aCMTask;
				break;
			}
			case ACMObjectTypes.ACMIcon:
				try
				{
					IAModelIcon pIconToCopy = null;
					if (objectToAdd is ACMIcon aCMIcon)
					{
						pIconToCopy = aCMIcon.TargetModelIcon;
					}
					ACMDoc.CreateIcon(ModelTypeName, name, pIconToCopy);
					UpdateIcons();
					result = Icons.FirstOrDefault((ACMIcon o) => string.Compare(name, o.Name, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) == 0);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
				break;
			case ACMObjectTypes.ACMProcedure:
			{
				foreach (string item2 in collection)
				{
					if (item2.Equals(name, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
					{
						flag = true;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					string[] procedureList = ACMDoc.GetProcedureList(ModelTypeName);
					for (int num = 0; num < procedureList.Length; num++)
					{
						if (procedureList[num].Equals(name, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
						{
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					string messageBoxText2 = string.Format(CustomModelsResources.TypeDefinitionAlreadyExists, name);
					string createProcedureTitle = CustomModelsResources.CreateProcedureTitle;
					System.Windows.MessageBox.Show(messageBoxText2, createProcedureTitle, MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Exclamation);
					break;
				}
				ACMProcedure aCMProcedure = (objectToAdd as ACMProcedure) ?? new ACMProcedure(this, name);
				string text2 = aCMProcedure.Text;
				if (text2 != "")
				{
					int count2 = text2.IndexOf('\n');
					text2 = text2.Remove(0, count2);
					int startIndex2 = text2.LastIndexOf('E');
					text2 = text2.Remove(startIndex2);
				}
				ACMDoc.CreateProcedure(ModelTypeName, name, text2);
				Procedures.Add(aCMProcedure);
				result = aCMProcedure;
				break;
			}
			case ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomVariableType:
			{
				foreach (string item3 in collection)
				{
					if (item3.Equals(name, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
					{
						flag = true;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					string[] procedureList = ACMDoc.GetVariableTypesList();
					for (int num = 0; num < procedureList.Length; num++)
					{
						if (procedureList[num].Equals(name, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
						{
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					string messageBoxText4 = string.Format(CustomModelsResources.TypeDefinitionAlreadyExists, name);
					string createVariableTypeTitle = CustomModelsResources.CreateVariableTypeTitle;
					System.Windows.MessageBox.Show(messageBoxText4, createVariableTypeTitle, MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Exclamation);
					break;
				}
				ACMCustomVariableType aCMCustomVariableType = (objectToAdd as ACMCustomVariableType) ?? new ACMCustomVariableType(this, name);
				string text3 = aCMCustomVariableType.Text;
				if (text3 != "")
				{
					int count3 = text3.IndexOf('\n');
					text3 = text3.Remove(0, count3);
					int startIndex3 = text3.LastIndexOf('E');
					text3 = text3.Remove(startIndex3);
				}
				ACMDoc.CreateVariableType(name, aCMCustomVariableType.InheritedType, text3);
				CustomVariableTypes.Add(aCMCustomVariableType);
				result = aCMCustomVariableType;
				break;
			}
			case ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomParameterType:
			{
				foreach (string item4 in collection)
				{
					if (item4.Equals(name, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
					{
						flag = true;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					string messageBoxText = string.Format(CustomModelsResources.TypeDefinitionAlreadyExists, name);
					string createParameterTypeTitle = CustomModelsResources.CreateParameterTypeTitle;
					System.Windows.MessageBox.Show(messageBoxText, createParameterTypeTitle, MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Exclamation);
					break;
				}
				ACMCustomParameterType aCMCustomParameterType = (objectToAdd as ACMCustomParameterType) ?? new ACMCustomParameterType(this, name);
				string text = aCMCustomParameterType.Text;
				if (text != "")
				{
					int count = text.IndexOf('\n');
					text = text.Remove(0, count);
					int startIndex = text.LastIndexOf('E');
					text = text.Remove(startIndex);
				}
				ACMDoc.CreateParameterType(name, (int)aCMCustomParameterType.ParameterType, aCMCustomParameterType.InheritedType, text);
				CustomParameterTypes.Add(aCMCustomParameterType);
				result = aCMCustomParameterType;
				break;
			}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public ACMObject GenerateProcedureCode(Type objectType, string name, ACMObject objectToAdd = null)
		{
			if (objectType == typeof(ACMProcedure) && !(objectToAdd is ACMProcedure))
			{
				new ACMProcedure(this, name);
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void Close()
		{
			ACMDoc.CloseDocument();
			Locked = false;
		}

		public bool SaveModelText(string modelText)
		{
			bool num = ACMDoc.SaveSubModelText(ModelTypeName, modelText);
			if (num)
			{
				ModelText = modelText;
			}
			return num;
		}

		public bool CompileModel(string modelText)
		{
			return ACMDoc.CompileModel(ModelTypeName, modelText);
		}

		public bool CompileSubModel(string subModelName, string subModelText)
		{
			return ACMDoc.CompileModel(subModelName, subModelText);
		}

		public bool ExportModel(string modelTypeName, string fileNameATMLZ, string fileNameACMF)
		{
			return ACMDoc.ExportModel(modelTypeName, fileNameATMLZ, fileNameACMF);
		}

		public bool SaveDocument(string filePath)
		{
			bool num = ACMDoc.SaveDocument(filePath);
			if (num)
			{
				IsModified = false;
			}
			return num;
		}

		public void SetActiveModel()
		{
			ACMDoc.SetActiveModel(Name);
		}
	}
	public abstract class ACMObject
	{
		public string Name { get; set; }

		public bool IsBuiltIn { get; set; }

		public bool IsDefault { get; set; }

		public ACMModel ParentModel { get; protected set; }

		public string Description { get; set; }

		public bool IsADuplicate { get; set; }

		public abstract ACMObjectTypes Type { get; }

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (obj is ACMObject aCMObject)
			{
				return aCMObject.Name.Equals(Name);
			}
			return false;
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			if (Name == null)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			return Name.GetHashCode();
		}

		public virtual void Rename(string newName)
		{
		}
	}
	public enum ACMObjectTypes
	{
		ACMChart,
		ACMCustomParameterType,
		ACMCustomVariableType,
		ACMHelp,
		ACMIcon,
		ACMPlot,
		ACMProcedure,
		ACMScript,
		ACMSubmodel,
		ACMTable,
		ACMTask,
		ACMText
	}
	public class ACMPlot : ACMObject
	{
		public bool Is3D { get; set; }

		public string XProfile { get; set; }

		public string YProfile { get; set; }

		public string[] XVariables { get; set; }

		public string[] YVariables { get; set; }

		public string[] Profiles { get; set; }

		public uint[] ProfileDimensions { get; set; }

		public uint[] VariableNumbers { get; set; }

		public string[] ProfileVariables { get; set; }

		public string Content { get; set; }

		public string PlotInputFile { get; set; }

		public override ACMObjectTypes Type => ACMObjectTypes.ACMPlot;

		public ACMPlot(ACMModel parentModel, string name)
		{
			base.ParentModel = parentModel;
			base.Name = name;
		}

		public void LoadDefinition()
		{
			base.ParentModel.ACMDoc.GetProfilePlotDefinition(base.ParentModel.ModelTypeName, base.Name, out var b3D, out var xProfile, out var yProfile, out var aProfiles, out var aProfileDims, out var aXVars, out var aYVars, out var aNumVars, out var aProfileVars, out var _, out var _, out var _, out var sPlotInputFile);
			Is3D = b3D;
			XProfile = xProfile;
			YProfile = yProfile;
			XVariables = aXVars;
			YVariables = aYVars;
			Profiles = aProfiles;
			ProfileDimensions = aProfileDims;
			VariableNumbers = aNumVars;
			ProfileVariables = aProfileVars;
			PlotInputFile = sPlotInputFile;
		}

		public void SaveDefinition()
		{
			base.ParentModel.ACMDoc.SetProfilePlotDefinition(base.ParentModel.ModelTypeName, base.Name, Is3D, XProfile, YProfile, Profiles, ProfileDimensions, XVariables, YVariables, VariableNumbers, ProfileVariables, PlotInputFile);
		}
	}

		public ACMText(ACMModel parentModel, string name, string text = null)

			Text = ((text == null) ? string.Empty : text);
		}

	public class ACMVariable
	{
		public string Name { get; set; }

		public string Value { get; set; }

		public string Spec { get; set; }

		public string Lower { get; set; }

		public string Upper { get; set; }

		public string Type { get; set; }

		public string Description { get; set; }

		public double Scale { get; set; }

		public string Units { get; set; }

		public double Derivative { get; set; }

		public string Record { get; set; }

		public string Tag { get; set; }

		public bool IsParameter { get; set; }

		public bool IsArray { get; set; }

		public ACMVariable(string name)
		{
			Name = name;
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (obj is ACMVariable aCMVariable)
			{
				return aCMVariable.Name.Equals(Name);
			}
			return false;
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			if (Name == null)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			return Name.GetHashCode();
		}
	}

namespace AspenTech.AspenPlus.CustomModels.Images
{
	internal static class IconHelper
	{
		[DllImport("gdi32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
		private static extern bool DeleteObject(IntPtr hObject);

		public static ImageSource ToImageSource(this Icon icon)
		{
			IntPtr hbitmap = icon.ToBitmap().GetHbitmap();
			ImageSource result = Imaging.CreateBitmapSourceFromHBitmap(hbitmap, IntPtr.Zero, Int32Rect.Empty, BitmapSizeOptions.FromEmptyOptions());
			if (!DeleteObject(hbitmap))
			{
				throw new Win32Exception();
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	public static class ImageResources
	{
		private static readonly Dictionary<string, ImageSource> _imageSources = new Dictionary<string, ImageSource>();

		public static ImageSource EditUndo => GetImageSource("EditUndo.png");

		public static ImageSource EditRedo => GetImageSource("EditRedo.png");

		public static ImageSource Icon16 => GetImageSource("Icons.png");

		public static ImageSource ModelHelp16 => GetImageSource("ModelHelp.ico", 16, 16);

		public static ImageSource Task16 => GetImageSource("Task.ico", 16, 16);

		public static ImageSource Script16 => GetImageSource("Script.ico", 16, 16);

		public static ImageSource FormPlot16 => GetImageSource("FormPlot.ico", 16, 16);

		public static ImageSource Procedures16 => GetImageSource("Procedures.ico", 16, 16);

		public static ImageSource FormInstance16 => GetImageSource("FormInstance.ico", 16, 16);

		public static ImageSource ModelText16 => GetImageSource("ModelText.ico", 16, 16);

		public static ImageSource Model16 => GetImageSource("Model.ico", 16, 16);

		public static ImageSource Checkmark => GetImageSource("Checkmark.png");

		public static ImageSource VariableType16 => GetImageSource("VariableType.ico", 16, 16);

		public static ImageSource ParameterType16 => GetImageSource("ParameterType.ico", 16, 16);

		private static ImageSource GetImageSource(string imageFileName)
		{
			if (!_imageSources.ContainsKey(imageFileName))
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				stringBuilder.Append("pack://application:,,,/");
				stringBuilder.Append(typeof(ImageResources).Assembly.FullName);
				stringBuilder.Append(";component/Images/");
				stringBuilder.Append(imageFileName);
				_imageSources.Add(imageFileName, new BitmapImage(new Uri(stringBuilder.ToString(), UriKind.RelativeOrAbsolute)));
			}
			return _imageSources[imageFileName];
		}

		private static ImageSource GetImageSource(string imageFileName, int width, int height)
		{
			string key = imageFileName + width;
			if (!_imageSources.ContainsKey(key))
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				stringBuilder.Append("pack://application:,,,/");
				stringBuilder.Append(typeof(ImageResources).Assembly.FullName);
				stringBuilder.Append(";component/Images/");
				stringBuilder.Append(imageFileName);
				Icon icon = new Icon(System.Windows.Application.GetResourceStream(new Uri(stringBuilder.ToString())).Stream, new System.Drawing.Size(width, height));
				_imageSources.Add(key, icon.ToImageSource());
			}
			return _imageSources[key];
		}
	}
}

	public class DuplicateDlg : Window, IComponentConnector
	{
		private string _sRetName;

		private CustomModelsObjectManager _cmom;

		private List<string> _slModelNameList;

		private bool bf;

		private int ititle;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.TextBox tbValue;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Button bthOK;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Button bthCancel;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public DuplicateDlg(ref CustomModelsObjectManager cmom, ref List<string> slModelNameList, string sModelName, string sTitle, int iT)
		{
			bf = false;
			InitializeComponent();
			cmom._sRetDup = "";
			_cmom = cmom;
			tbValue.Text = ((sModelName.Length > 26) ? sModelName.Substring(0, 26) : sModelName) + "_";
			_sRetName = tbValue.Text;
			tbValue.Focus();
			tbValue.Select(tbValue.Text.Length, 0);
			base.Title = sTitle;
			ititle = iT;
			_slModelNameList = new List<string>(slModelNameList);
			bf = true;
		}

		private void bthOK_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			string sRetNameL = _sRetName.ToLower();
			_slModelNameList = _slModelNameList.ConvertAll((string low) => low.ToLowerInvariant());
			if (_slModelNameList.FindIndex((string x) => x.Equals(sRetNameL)) != -1)
			{
				string modelNameInUse_Msg = CustomModelsResources.ModelNameInUse_Msg;
				string caption = "";
				if (ititle == 0)
				{
					caption = CustomModelsResources.DuplicateModel_Msg;
				}
				if (ititle == 1)
				{
					caption = CustomModelsResources.RenameModel_Msg;
				}
				System.Windows.MessageBox.Show(modelNameInUse_Msg, caption);
			}
			else
			{
				_cmom._sRetDup = _sRetName;
				Close();
			}
		}

		private void bthCancel_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			_sRetName = "";
			_cmom._sRetDup = _sRetName;
			Close();
		}

		private void tbValue_TextChanged(object sender, TextChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(tbValue.Text))
			{
				bthOK.IsEnabled = false;
			}
			else
			{
				bthOK.IsEnabled = true;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(tbValue.Text) && !char.IsLetter(tbValue.Text[0]))
			{
				tbValue.Text = "";
			}
			else if (bf)
			{
				if (Regex.IsMatch(tbValue.Text, "[^a-zA-Z0-9_]"))
				{
					tbValue.Text = _sRetName;
				}
				_sRetName = tbValue.Text;
				tbValue.Select(tbValue.Text.Length, 0);
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}
