	public enum SearchMode
	{
		Find,
		Replace
	}
	public class FindAndReplace : Window, INotifyPropertyChanged, IComponentConnector
	{
		private const int SCFIND_WHOLEWORD = 2;

		private const int SCFIND_MATCHCASE = 4;

		private AxEditorCtrl _editorControl;

		private bool _bForwardSearch = true;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty SearchModeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SearchMode", typeof(SearchMode), typeof(FindAndReplace), new UIPropertyMetadata(OnSearchModePropertyChanged));

		private string _searchText;

		internal static readonly ObservableCollection<string> _searchStrings = new ObservableCollection<string>();

		private string _replaceText = string.Empty;

		internal static readonly ObservableCollection<string> _replaceStrings = new ObservableCollection<string>();

		private string _statusMessage;

		private ICommand _findNextCommand;

		private ICommand _findPreviousCommand;

		private ICommand _closeCommand;

		private ICommand _replaceCommand;

		private ICommand _replaceAllCommand;

		internal TabItem tabFind;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox cboSearchText;

		internal TabItem tabReplace;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox cboSearchTextReplace;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Button btnReplace;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Button btnReplaceAll;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Button btnReplaceSelection;

		internal TextBlock statusBar;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.CheckBox chkMatchCase;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.CheckBox chkWholeWords;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Button btnFindNext;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Button btnFindPrevious;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Button btnClose;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public bool IsMatchCase { get; set; }

		public bool IsMatchWords { get; set; }

		public SearchMode SearchMode
		{
			get
			{
				return (SearchMode)GetValue(SearchModeProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(SearchModeProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public string SearchText
		{
			get
			{
				return _searchText;
			}
			set
			{
				_searchText = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("SearchText");
			}
		}

		public ObservableCollection<string> SearchStrings => _searchStrings;

		public string ReplaceText
		{
			get
			{
				return _replaceText;
			}
			set
			{
				_replaceText = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("ReplaceText");
			}
		}

		public ObservableCollection<string> ReplaceStrings => _replaceStrings;

		public string StatusMessage
		{
			get
			{
				return _statusMessage;
			}
			set
			{
				_statusMessage = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("StatusMessage");
			}
		}

		public int SearchFlags
		{
			get
			{
				int num = 0;
				if (IsMatchWords)
				{
					num |= 2;
				}
				if (IsMatchCase)
				{
					num |= 4;
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		public ICommand FindNextCommand
		{
			get
			{
				if (_findNextCommand == null)
				{
					_findNextCommand = (ICommand)new RelayActionCommand<object>((Action<object>)FindNextCommand_Executed, (Func<object, bool>)FindNextCommand_CanExecute);
				}
				return _findNextCommand;
			}
		}

		public ICommand FindPreviousCommand
		{
			get
			{
				if (_findPreviousCommand == null)
				{
					_findPreviousCommand = (ICommand)new RelayActionCommand<object>((Action<object>)FindPreviousCommand_Executed, (Func<object, bool>)FindPreviousCommand_CanExecute);
				}
				return _findPreviousCommand;
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public FindAndReplace(AxEditorCtrl editorCtrl)
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			_editorControl = editorCtrl;
			base.DataContext = this;
			base.Loaded += FindAndReplace_Loaded;
		}

		private void FindAndReplace_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Input, (Action)delegate
			{
				if (SearchMode == SearchMode.Find)
				{
					cboSearchText.Focus();
				}
				else
				{
					cboSearchTextReplace.Focus();
				}
			});
		}

		private static void OnSearchModePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as FindAndReplace).OnSearchModeChanged((SearchMode)e.OldValue, (SearchMode)e.NewValue);
		}

		private void OnSearchModeChanged(SearchMode oldValue, SearchMode newValue)
		{
			if (newValue == SearchMode.Find)
			{
				tabFind.IsSelected = true;
			}
			else
			{
				tabReplace.IsSelected = true;
			}
		}

		protected void OnPropertyChanged(string propName)
		{
			this.PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
		}

		private bool FindNextCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(SearchText);
		}

		private void FindNextCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			StatusMessage = string.Empty;
			_bForwardSearch = true;
			_editorControl.SetSearchFlags(SearchFlags);
			StoreSearchString(SearchText);
			if (!_editorControl.SearchNext(SearchText))
			{
				StatusMessage = CustomModelsResources.FindAndReplaceDialog_NotFound;
			}
		}

		private bool FindPreviousCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(SearchText);
		}

		private void FindPreviousCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			StatusMessage = string.Empty;
			_bForwardSearch = false;
			_editorControl.SetSearchFlags(SearchFlags);
			StoreSearchString(SearchText);
			if (!_editorControl.SearchPrevious(SearchText))
			{
				StatusMessage = CustomModelsResources.FindAndReplaceDialog_NotFound;
			}
		}

		private void CloseCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			Close();
		}

		private bool ReplaceCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(SearchText);
		}

		private void ReplaceCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			StatusMessage = string.Empty;
			_editorControl.SetSearchFlags(SearchFlags);
			bool flag = false;
			string selectedText = _editorControl.GetSelectedText();
			flag = ((!IsMatchCase) ? SearchText.Equals(selectedText, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) : SearchText.Equals(selectedText, StringComparison.InvariantCulture));
			int selectionStart = _editorControl.GetSelectionStart();
			int pos = _editorControl.GetSelectionEnd();
			if (flag)
			{
				_editorControl.ReplaceSelectedText(ReplaceText);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(ReplaceText))
				{
					StoreReplaceString(ReplaceText);
				}
				pos = selectionStart + ReplaceText.Length;
			}
			if (_bForwardSearch)
			{
				_editorControl.GotoPosition(pos);
				FindNextCommand.Execute(null);
			}
			else
			{
				_editorControl.GotoPosition(selectionStart);
				FindPreviousCommand.Execute(null);
			}
		}

		private bool ReplaceAllCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(SearchText);
		}

		private void ReplaceAllCommand_Executed(object args)
		{
			_editorControl.SetSearchFlags(SearchFlags);
			bool bUseSelection = args as string == "1";
			int num = _editorControl.ReplaceAll(SearchText, ReplaceText, bUseSelection);
			if (num <= 0)
			{
				StatusMessage = CustomModelsResources.FindAndReplaceDialog_NotFound;
			}
			else
			{
				StatusMessage = string.Format(CustomModelsResources.FindAndReplaceDialog_Replacements, num);
			}
			StoreSearchString(SearchText);
			StoreReplaceString(ReplaceText);
		}

		private void StoreSearchString(string szSearch)
		{
			if (!SearchStrings.Contains(szSearch, StringComparer.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				SearchStrings.Add(szSearch);
			}
		}

		private void StoreReplaceString(string szReplace)
		{
			if (!ReplaceStrings.Contains(szReplace, StringComparer.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				ReplaceStrings.Add(szReplace);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/AspenTech.AspenPlus.CustomModels;component/dialogs/findandreplace.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				System.Windows.Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		}

	public class FindVariablesDialog : Window, IComponentConnector
	{
		private ACMModel _model;

		private ICommand _okCommand;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Button btnOK;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Button btnCancel;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public ObservableCollection<ACMVariable> AvailableVariables { get; private set; }

		public ObservableCollection<ACMVariable> SelectedVariables { get; private set; }

		public ICommand OKCommand
		{
			get
			{
				if (_okCommand == null)
				{
					_okCommand = (ICommand)new RelayActionCommand<object>((Action<object>)OKCommand_Executed, (Func<object, bool>)OKCommand_CanExecute);
				}
				return _okCommand;
			}
		}

		public FindVariablesDialog(ACMModel model, IEnumerable<string> selectedVariables, bool allowMultipleSelection = false)
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			_model = model;
			AvailableVariables = new ObservableCollection<ACMVariable>(GetAllVariables());
			SelectedVariables = new ObservableCollection<ACMVariable>();
			foreach (string variableName in selectedVariables)
			{
				ACMVariable aCMVariable = AvailableVariables.FirstOrDefault((ACMVariable v) => v.Name.Equals(variableName, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase));
				if (aCMVariable == null)
				{
					aCMVariable = new ACMVariable(variableName);
				}
				SelectedVariables.Add(aCMVariable);
			}
		}

		private IEnumerable<ACMVariable> GetAllVariables()
		{
			string[] modelVariables = _model.ACMDoc.GetModelVariables(_model.ModelTypeName);
			_model.ACMDoc.GetModelVariablesAttributes(_model.ModelTypeName, out var specs, out var descriptions, out var uppers, out var lowers, out var values, out var types);
			string[] modelParameters = _model.ACMDoc.GetModelParameters(_model.ModelTypeName);
			for (int i = 0; i < modelVariables.Length; i++)
			{
				ACMVariable aCMVariable = new ACMVariable(modelVariables[i]);
				aCMVariable.Spec = specs[i];
				aCMVariable.Description = descriptions[i];
				aCMVariable.Upper = uppers[i];
				aCMVariable.Lower = lowers[i];
				aCMVariable.Value = values[i];
				aCMVariable.Type = types[i];
				if (aCMVariable.Name.Contains("(*)"))
				{
					aCMVariable.IsArray = true;
				}
				else if (modelParameters.Contains(aCMVariable.Name))
				{
					aCMVariable.IsParameter = true;
				}
				yield return aCMVariable;
			}
		}

		private void btnCancel_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.DialogResult = false;
		}

		private bool OKCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			return SelectedVariables.Count > 0;
		}

		private void OKCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			base.DialogResult = true;
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/AspenTech.AspenPlus.CustomModels;component/dialogs/findvariablesdialog.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				System.Windows.Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		internal Delegate _CreateDelegate(Type delegateType, string handler)
		{
			return Delegate.CreateDelegate(delegateType, this, handler);
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]

	public class FontDialog : Window, IComponentConnector
	{
		private static InstalledFontCollection FontNameList = new InstalledFontCollection();

		private static EncodingInfo[] Encodings = Encoding.GetEncodings();

		private static int[] sizeIntArray = new int[16]
		{
			8, 9, 10, 11, 12, 14, 16, 18, 20, 22,
			24, 26, 28, 36, 48, 72
		};

		private static string[] styleFontArray = new string[4] { "Regular", "Italic", "Bold", "Bold Italic" };

		private static string[] scriptFontArray = new string[9] { "Western", "Hebrew", "Arabic", "Greek", "Turkish", "Baltic", "Central European", "Cyrillic", "Vietnamese" };

		private static string sDefaultFont = "Courier New";

		private static string sDefaultFontStyle = "Regular";

		private static string sDefaultSize = "9";

		public int _iVisual;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.TextBox tbSelFont;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.TextBox tbSelFontStyle;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.TextBox tbSelSize;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.ListBox lstbFonts;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.ListBox lstbStyles;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.ListBox lstbSizes;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox cbScripts;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.TextBox tbSampleN;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.TextBox tbSampleB;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.TextBox tbSampleI;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.TextBox tbSampleIB;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Button btnOK;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Button btnCancel;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		private List<string> fontList { get; set; }

		private List<string> sizeFontList { get; set; }

		private List<string> styleFontList { get; set; }

		public AxEditorCtrl _EditorCtrl { get; private set; }

		public FontDialog(ref AxEditorCtrl editorCtrl)
		{
			_EditorCtrl = editorCtrl;
			InitializeComponent();
			InitializeFontList();
			InitializeFontListBox();
			InitializeFontStyleListBox();
			InitializeFontSizeListBox();
			InitializeFontScriptCombotBox();
			string sFontName = "";
			string sFontStyle = "";
			string sFontSize = "";
			bool fontFromRegestry = GetFontFromRegestry(ref sFontName, ref sFontStyle, ref sFontSize);
			string text = "";
			text = ((!fontFromRegestry) ? _EditorCtrl.GetFontName() : sFontName);
			bool flag = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && fontList.Contains(text);
			bool flag2 = false;
			if (flag)
			{
				flag2 = true;
			}
			tbSelFont.Clear();
			if (flag2)
			{
				lstbFonts.SelectedItem = text;
				int selectedIndex = lstbFonts.SelectedIndex;
				lstbFonts.ScrollIntoView(lstbFonts.Items.GetItemAt(selectedIndex));
				lstbFonts.SelectedIndex = selectedIndex;
			}
			else
			{
				lstbFonts.SelectedItem = sDefaultFont;
				int selectedIndex2 = lstbFonts.SelectedIndex;
				lstbFonts.ScrollIntoView(lstbFonts.Items.GetItemAt(selectedIndex2));
				lstbFonts.SelectedIndex = selectedIndex2;
			}
			bool flag3 = false;
			string sFontStyle2 = "";
			if (fontFromRegestry)
			{
				sFontStyle2 = sFontStyle;
				flag3 = Array.Exists(styleFontArray, (string x) => x.Equals(sFontStyle2));
			}
			else
			{
				bool fontBold = _EditorCtrl.GetFontBold();
				bool fontItalic = _EditorCtrl.GetFontItalic();
				if (!fontBold && !fontItalic)
				{
					sFontStyle2 = styleFontArray[0];
					flag3 = true;
				}
				if (!fontBold && fontItalic)
				{
					sFontStyle2 = styleFontArray[1];
					flag3 = true;
				}
				if (fontBold && !fontItalic)
				{
					sFontStyle2 = styleFontArray[2];
					flag3 = true;
				}
				if (fontBold && fontItalic)
				{
					sFontStyle2 = styleFontArray[3];
					flag3 = true;
				}
			}
			tbSelFontStyle.Clear();
			if (flag3)
			{
				lstbStyles.SelectedItem = sFontStyle2;
				int selectedIndex3 = lstbStyles.SelectedIndex;
				lstbStyles.ScrollIntoView(lstbStyles.Items.GetItemAt(selectedIndex3));
				lstbStyles.SelectedIndex = selectedIndex3;
			}
			else
			{
				lstbStyles.SelectedItem = sDefaultFontStyle;
				int selectedIndex4 = lstbStyles.SelectedIndex;
				lstbStyles.ScrollIntoView(lstbStyles.Items.GetItemAt(selectedIndex4));
				lstbStyles.SelectedIndex = selectedIndex4;
			}
			int iFontSize;
			if (fontFromRegestry)
			{
				int result = 0;
				if (int.TryParse(sFontSize, out result))
				{
					iFontSize = result;
				}
				else
				{
					iFontSize = 0;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				iFontSize = _EditorCtrl.GetFontSize();
			}
			bool flag4 = false;
			if (iFontSize >= 8 && iFontSize <= 72 && Array.Exists(sizeIntArray, (int x) => x.Equals(iFontSize)))
			{
				flag4 = true;
			}
			tbSelSize.Clear();
			if (flag4)
			{
				lstbSizes.SelectedItem = iFontSize.ToString();
				int selectedIndex5 = lstbSizes.SelectedIndex;
				lstbSizes.ScrollIntoView(lstbSizes.Items.GetItemAt(selectedIndex5));
				lstbSizes.SelectedIndex = selectedIndex5;
			}
			else
			{
				lstbSizes.SelectedItem = sDefaultSize;
				int selectedIndex6 = lstbSizes.SelectedIndex;
				lstbSizes.ScrollIntoView(lstbSizes.Items.GetItemAt(selectedIndex6));
				lstbSizes.SelectedIndex = selectedIndex6;
			}
			SetSample();
			lstbFonts.Focus();
			_iVisual = 1;
		}

		private void SetSample()
		{
			string familyName = lstbFonts.SelectedItem.ToString();
			string text = lstbStyles.SelectedItem.ToString();
			float num = float.Parse(lstbSizes.SelectedItem.ToString());
			System.Windows.Media.FontFamily fontFamily = new System.Windows.Media.FontFamily(familyName);
			if (text == styleFontArray[0])
			{
				tbSampleN.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				tbSampleB.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				tbSampleI.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				tbSampleIB.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				tbSampleN.FontFamily = fontFamily;
				tbSampleN.FontSize = num;
			}
			if (text == styleFontArray[1])
			{
				tbSampleN.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				tbSampleB.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				tbSampleI.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				tbSampleIB.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				tbSampleI.FontFamily = fontFamily;
				tbSampleI.FontSize = num;
			}
			if (text == styleFontArray[2])
			{
				tbSampleN.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				tbSampleB.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				tbSampleI.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				tbSampleIB.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				tbSampleB.FontFamily = fontFamily;
				tbSampleB.FontSize = num;
			}
			if (text == styleFontArray[3])
			{
				tbSampleN.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				tbSampleB.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				tbSampleI.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				tbSampleIB.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				tbSampleIB.FontFamily = fontFamily;
				tbSampleIB.FontSize = num;
			}
		}

		private void InitializeFontList()
		{
			fontList = new List<string>();
			System.Drawing.FontFamily[] families = FontNameList.Families;
			foreach (System.Drawing.FontFamily fontFamily in families)
			{
				fontList.Add(fontFamily.Name);
			}
		}

		private void InitializeFontListBox()
		{
			foreach (string font in fontList)
			{
				lstbFonts.Items.Add(font);
			}
		}

		private void InitializeFontSizeListBox()
		{
			int[] array = sizeIntArray;
			foreach (int num in array)
			{
				lstbSizes.Items.Add(num.ToString());
			}
		}

		private void InitializeFontStyleListBox()
		{
			string[] array = styleFontArray;
			foreach (string newItem in array)
			{
				lstbStyles.Items.Add(newItem);
			}
		}

		private void InitializeFontScriptCombotBox()
		{
			string[] array = scriptFontArray;
			foreach (string newItem in array)
			{
				cbScripts.Items.Add(newItem);
			}
			cbScripts.SelectedIndex = 0;
		}

		private void LstbFonts_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			_ = lstbFonts.SelectedIndex;
			string textData = lstbFonts.SelectedItem.ToString();
			tbSelFont.Clear();
			tbSelFont.AppendText(textData);
			if (_iVisual == 1)
			{
				SetSample();
			}
		}

			if (_iVisual == 1)
			{
				SetSample();
			}
		}

		private void LstbSizes_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			_ = lstbSizes.SelectedIndex;
			string textData = lstbSizes.SelectedItem.ToString();

			}
		}

		private void BtnOK_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			string sFontName = lstbFonts.SelectedItem.ToString();
			string text = lstbSizes.SelectedItem.ToString();
			int nFontSize = int.Parse(text);
			bool bBold = false;
			bool bItalic = false;
			string text2 = lstbStyles.SelectedItem.ToString();
			if (text2 == styleFontArray[0])
			{
				bBold = false;
				bItalic = false;
			}
			if (text2 == styleFontArray[1])
			{
				bBold = false;
				bItalic = true;
			}
			if (text2 == styleFontArray[2])
			{
				bBold = true;
				bItalic = false;
			}
			if (text2 == styleFontArray[3])
			{
				bBold = true;
				bItalic = true;
			}
			_EditorCtrl.SetFont(sFontName, nFontSize, bBold, bItalic);
			SaveFontInRegestry(sFontName, text2, text);
			Close();
		}

		private void SaveFontInRegestry(string sFontName, string sFontStyle, string sFontSize)
		{
			string text = "";
			text = SystemEnvironment.InternalVersionString;
			string text2 = "Software\\AspenTech\\Aspen Plus\\" + text + "\\Text Editor\\Font";
			RegistryKey registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey(text2, writable: true);
			if (registryKey == null)
			{
				registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.CreateSubKey(text2, writable: true);
			}
			if (registryKey != null)
			{
				registryKey.SetValue("FontName", sFontName);
				registryKey.SetValue("FontType", sFontStyle);
				registryKey.SetValue("FontSize", sFontSize);
			}
		}

		private bool GetFontFromRegestry(ref string sFontName, ref string sFontStyle, ref string sFontSize)
		{
			bool result = false;
			sFontName = "";
			sFontStyle = "";
			sFontSize = "";
			string text = "";
			text = SystemEnvironment.InternalVersionString;
			string name = "Software\\AspenTech\\Aspen Plus\\" + text + "\\Text Editor\\Font";
			RegistryKey registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey(name, writable: true);
			if (registryKey != null)
			{
				sFontName = (string)registryKey.GetValue("FontName");
				sFontStyle = (string)registryKey.GetValue("FontType");
				sFontSize = (string)registryKey.GetValue("FontSize");
				result = true;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void BtnCancel_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			Close();
		}

		}

		{
			//IL_0060: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			while (true)
			{
				FolderBrowserDialog folderBrowserDialog = new FolderBrowserDialog();
				folderBrowserDialog.Description = ApwnResources.SetWorkingDirectory;
				folderBrowserDialog.SelectedPath = WorkingDir;
				if (folderBrowserDialog.ShowDialog().Equals(System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult.OK))
				{
					if (folderBrowserDialog.SelectedPath.Length > 216)
					{
						MMMessageBox.Show(ApwnResources.PathTooLong, SystemEnvironment.ApplicationName, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)16, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
						continue;
					}
					WorkingDir = folderBrowserDialog.SelectedPath;
					break;
				}
				break;
			}
		}

	public class GotoLine : Window, IDataErrorInfo, IComponentConnector
	{
		private ICommand _okCommand;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Label lblDescription;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.TextBox txtLineNumber;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Button btnOK;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Button btnCancel;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public int LineNumber { get; set; }

		public int LineCount { get; private set; }

		public string this[string columnName]
		{
			get
			{
				if (columnName == "LineNumber" && (LineNumber < 1 || LineNumber > LineCount))
				{
					return string.Format(CustomModelsResources.GotoLineDialog_ErrorMessage, LineCount);
				}
				return string.Empty;
			}
		}

		public string Error => null;

		public ICommand OKCommand
		{
			get
			{
				if (_okCommand == null)
				{
					_okCommand = (ICommand)new RelayActionCommand<object>((Action<object>)OKCommand_Executed, (Func<object, bool>)OKCommand_CanExecute);
				}
				return _okCommand;
			}
		}

		public GotoLine(int lineNumber, int lineCount)
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			LineNumber = lineNumber;
			LineCount = lineCount;
			lblDescription.Content = string.Format(CustomModelsResources.GotoLineDialog_Description, lineCount);
			base.DataContext = this;
			base.Loaded += GotoLine_Loaded;
		}

		private void GotoLine_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Input, (Action)delegate
			{
				txtLineNumber.Focus();
				txtLineNumber.SelectAll();
			});
		}

		private bool OKCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			return string.IsNullOrEmpty(this["LineNumber"]);
		}

		private void OKCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			base.DialogResult = true;
			Close();
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/AspenTech.AspenPlus.CustomModels;component/dialogs/gotoline.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				System.Windows.Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		{
			switch (connectionId)

	public class IndentationSettings : Window, IComponentConnector
	{
		internal System.Windows.Controls.Button bthOK;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Button bthCancel;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.TextBox tbValue;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public AxEditorCtrl _EditorCtrl { get; private set; }

		public IndentationSettings(ref AxEditorCtrl editorCtrl)
		{
			_EditorCtrl = editorCtrl;
			InitializeComponent();
			int indent = _EditorCtrl.GetIndent();
			tbValue.Text = indent.ToString();
		}

		private void BthOK_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			string text = tbValue.Text;
			text = text.Trim();
			text = text.TrimStart('0');
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				text = "0";
			}
			int indent = Convert.ToInt32(text);
			_EditorCtrl.SetIndent(indent);
			Close();
		}

		private void BthCancel_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			Close();
		}

		private void TbValue_TextChanged(object sender, TextChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (Regex.IsMatch(tbValue.Text, "[^0-9]"))
			{
				tbValue.Text = tbValue.Text.Remove(tbValue.Text.Length - 1);
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(tbValue.Text.Trim()))
			{
				tbValue.Text = "0";
			}
			tbValue.Select(tbValue.Text.Length, 0);
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/AspenTech.AspenPlus.CustomModels;component/dialogs/indentationsettings.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				System.Windows.Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				bthOK = (System.Windows.Controls.Button)target;
				bthOK.Click += BthOK_Click;
				break;
			case 2:
				bthCancel = (System.Windows.Controls.Button)target;
				bthCancel.Click += BthCancel_Click;
				break;
			case 3:
				tbValue = (System.Windows.Controls.TextBox)target;
				tbValue.TextChanged += TbValue_TextChanged;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	public class ProfilePlotEditorWindow : System.Windows.Controls.UserControl, INotifyPropertyChanged, IComponentConnector

					{

						Variables = new ObservableCollection<VariableItem>
						{

		public bool IsMultipleY
		{
			get
			{
				return _isMultipleY;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_isMultipleY == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				_isMultipleY = value;
				if (_isMultipleY)
				{
					if (ProfileGroups.Count > 0 && ProfileGroups[0].Profiles.Count > 1)
					{
						List<ProfileGroup> list = new List<ProfileGroup>();
						foreach (ProfileItem profile in ProfileGroups[0].Profiles)
						{
							list.Add(new ProfileGroup
							{
								Name = profile.Name,
								Profiles = new ObservableCollection<ProfileItem> { profile }
							});
						}
						ProfileGroups.Clear();
						foreach (ProfileGroup item in list)
						{
							ProfileGroups.Add(item);
						}
					}
				}
				else if (ProfileGroups.Count > 1)
				{
					ProfileGroup profileGroup = new ProfileGroup
					{
						Name = ProfileGroups[0].Name
					};
					List<ProfileItem> list2 = new List<ProfileItem>();
					foreach (ProfileGroup profileGroup2 in ProfileGroups)
					{
						list2.AddRange(profileGroup2.Profiles);
					}
					profileGroup.Profiles = new ObservableCollection<ProfileItem>(list2);
					ProfileGroups.Clear();
					ProfileGroups.Add(profileGroup);
				}
				NotifyPropertyChanged("IsMultipleY");
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public ProfilePlotEditorWindow(ACMPlot plotObject)
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			PlotObject = plotObject;
			Is3D = PlotObject.Is3D;
			if (Is3D)
			{
				_plotSettings = Load3DPlotSettings(PlotObject.PlotInputFile);
			}
			else
			{
				_plotSettings = Load2DPlotSettings(PlotObject.PlotInputFile);
			}
			_xLabel = _plotSettings.XLabel;
			_isMultipleY = _plotSettings.IsMultipleY;
			_xProfile = PlotObject.XProfile;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_xProfile))
			{
				_xProfile = "XProfile";
			}
			_xVariables = new ObservableCollection<VariableItem>();
			string[] xVariables = PlotObject.XVariables;
			foreach (string name in xVariables)
			{
				_xVariables.Add(new VariableItem(name));
			}
			if (Is3D)
			{
				xVariables = PlotObject.YVariables;
				foreach (string name2 in xVariables)
				{
					YAxisVariables3D.Add(new VariableItem(name2));
				}
				xVariables = PlotObject.ProfileVariables;
				foreach (string name3 in xVariables)
				{
					ZAxisVariables3D.Add(new VariableItem(name3));
				}
				YLabel3D = _plotSettings.YLabel3D;
				ZLabel3D = _plotSettings.ZLabel3D;
			}
			else
			{
				ProfileItem profileItem = new ProfileItem
				{
					Name = PlotObject.YProfile,
					Variables = new ObservableCollection<VariableItem>()
				};
				xVariables = PlotObject.YVariables;
				foreach (string name4 in xVariables)
				{
					profileItem.Variables.Add(new VariableItem(name4));
				}
				_profileGroups = new ObservableCollection<ProfileGroup>();
				if (!IsMultipleY)
				{
					if (_plotSettings.YAxes != null && _plotSettings.YAxes.Length != 0)
					{
						ProfileGroup profileGroup = new ProfileGroup
						{
							Name = _plotSettings.YAxes[0].Label,
							Profiles = new ObservableCollection<ProfileItem>()
						};
						_profileGroups.Add(profileGroup);
						int num = 0;
						for (int j = 0; j < PlotObject.Profiles.Length; j++)
						{
							string text = PlotObject.Profiles[j];
							if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(PlotObject.YProfile) || !text.Equals(PlotObject.YProfile))
							{
								ObservableCollection<VariableItem> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<VariableItem>();
								for (int k = 0; k < PlotObject.VariableNumbers[j]; k++)
								{
									observableCollection.Add(new VariableItem(PlotObject.ProfileVariables[num++]));
								}
								profileGroup.Profiles.Add(new ProfileItem
								{
									Name = text,
									Variables = observableCollection
								});
							}
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					int num2 = 0;
					ProfileGroup[] array = null;
					if (_plotSettings.YAxes != null && _plotSettings.YAxes.Length != 0)
					{
						int num3 = _plotSettings.YAxes.Select((AxisInfo p) => p.AxisMap).Max();
						if (num3 > 0)
						{
							array = new ProfileGroup[num3];
						}
					}
					for (int num4 = 0; num4 < PlotObject.Profiles.Length; num4++)
					{
						string text2 = PlotObject.Profiles[num4];
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(PlotObject.YProfile) || !text2.Equals(PlotObject.YProfile))
						{
							ObservableCollection<VariableItem> observableCollection2 = new ObservableCollection<VariableItem>();
							for (int num5 = 0; num5 < PlotObject.VariableNumbers[num4]; num5++)
							{
								observableCollection2.Add(new VariableItem(PlotObject.ProfileVariables[num2++]));
							}
							int num6 = num4;
							if (array.Length <= num4)
							{
								num6 = array.Length - 1;
							}
							if (array[_plotSettings.YAxes[num6].AxisMap - 1] == null)
							{
								array[_plotSettings.YAxes[num6].AxisMap - 1] = new ProfileGroup
								{
									Name = _plotSettings.YAxes[num6].Label,
									Profiles = new ObservableCollection<ProfileItem>()
								};
							}
							array[_plotSettings.YAxes[num6].AxisMap - 1].Profiles.Add(new ProfileItem
							{
								Name = text2,
								Variables = observableCollection2
							});
						}
					}
					_profileGroups = new ObservableCollection<ProfileGroup>();
					if (array != null)
					{
						ProfileGroup[] array2 = array;
						foreach (ProfileGroup profileGroup2 in array2)
						{
							if (profileGroup2 != null && profileGroup2.Profiles != null && profileGroup2.Profiles.Count > 0)
							{
								_profileGroups.Add(profileGroup2);
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if (_profileGroups.Count == 0)
				{
					_profileGroups.Add(new ProfileGroup());
				}
			}
			XVariables.CollectionChanged += ProfileGroups_CollectionChanged;
			ProfileGroups.CollectionChanged += ProfileGroups_CollectionChanged;
			YAxisVariables3D.CollectionChanged += ProfileGroups_CollectionChanged;
			ZAxisVariables3D.CollectionChanged += ProfileGroups_CollectionChanged;
			base.DataContext = this;
		}

		private void ProfileGroups_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			IsModified = true;
		}

		public void MarkEditorAsADuplicate()
		{
			IsModified = true;
		}

		private bool AddUnitsCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			switch (arg as string)
			{
			case "X":
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(XLabel))
				{
					return !XLabel.EndsWith("$UOM", StringComparison.InvariantCulture);
				}
				return false;
			case "Y3D":
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(YLabel3D))
				{
					return !YLabel3D.EndsWith("$UOM", StringComparison.InvariantCulture);
				}
				return false;
			case "Z3D":
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(ZLabel3D))
				{
					return !ZLabel3D.EndsWith("$UOM", StringComparison.InvariantCulture);
				}
				return false;
			default:
				return false;
			}
		}

		private void AddUnitsCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			switch (obj as string)
			{
			case "X":
				XLabel = XLabel.Trim() + "$UOM";
				break;
			case "Y3D":
				YLabel3D = YLabel3D.Trim() + "$UOM";
				break;
			case "Z3D":
				ZLabel3D = ZLabel3D.Trim() + "$UOM";
				break;
			}
		}

		private bool AddYAxisCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			return true;
		}

		private void AddYAxisCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			ProfileGroups.Add(new ProfileGroup());
			tabSeparateYAxis.SelectedIndex = tabSeparateYAxis.Items.Count - 1;
		}

		private bool RemoveYAxisCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (tabSeparateYAxis.SelectedIndex >= 0)
			{
				return tabSeparateYAxis.Items.Count > 1;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void RemoveYAxisCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			ProfileGroups.RemoveAt(tabSeparateYAxis.SelectedIndex);
		}

		private bool SaveCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(XProfile) && ProfileGroups != null && ProfileGroups.Count > 0)
			{
				return ProfileGroups[0].Profiles.Count > 0;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void SaveCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			Save();
		}

		public void Save()
		{
			PlotObject.Is3D = Is3D;
			PlotObject.XProfile = XLabel;
			PlotObject.XVariables = XVariables.Select((VariableItem v) => v.Name).ToArray();
			if (Is3D)
			{
				if (IsYAxisTime)
				{
					PlotObject.YProfile = YLabel3D;
					PlotObject.YVariables = new string[0];
				}
				else
				{
					PlotObject.YVariables = YAxisVariables3D.Select((VariableItem v) => v.Name).ToArray();
					PlotObject.YProfile = YLabel3D;
				}
				PlotObject.ProfileVariables = ZAxisVariables3D.Select((VariableItem v) => v.Name).ToArray();
				PlotObject.Profiles = new string[1] { ZLabel3D };
				PlotObject.ProfileDimensions = new uint[1] { 2u };
				PlotObject.VariableNumbers = new uint[1] { (uint)PlotObject.ProfileVariables.Length };
			}
			else
			{
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				List<uint> list2 = new List<uint>();
				List<string> list3 = new List<string>();
				List<uint> list4 = new List<uint>();
				foreach (ProfileGroup profileGroup in ProfileGroups)
				{
					foreach (ProfileItem profile in profileGroup.Profiles)
					{
						if (!list.Contains(profile.Name))
						{
							list.Add(profile.Name);
							list2.Add(1u);
							IEnumerable<string> enumerable = profile.Variables.Select((VariableItem p) => p.Name);
							list3.AddRange(enumerable);
							list4.Add((uint)enumerable.Count());
						}
					}
				}
				PlotObject.Profiles = list.ToArray();
				PlotObject.ProfileDimensions = list2.ToArray();
				PlotObject.ProfileVariables = list3.ToArray();
				PlotObject.VariableNumbers = list4.ToArray();
			}
			UpdatePlotInputFile();
			PlotObject.SaveDefinition();
			IsModified = false;
		}

		protected void NotifyPropertyChanged(string propName)
		{
			PropertyChangedEventHandler propertyChangedEventHandler = this.PropertyChanged;
			if (propertyChangedEventHandler != null)
			{
				propertyChangedEventHandler(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
				IsModified = true;
			}
		}

		private ProfilePlotSettings Load2DPlotSettings(string settings)
		{
			ProfilePlotSettings profilePlotSettings = new ProfilePlotSettings();
			string[] array = settings.Split('\n');
			int num = 0;
			string[] array2 = array;
			for (int i = 0; i < array2.Length; i++)
			{
				string[] array3 = array2[i].Split(':');
				if (array3 == null || array3.Length != 2)
				{
					continue;
				}
				string text = array3[0];
				string text2 = array3[1];
				switch (text)
				{
				case "Title":
					profilePlotSettings.Title = text2;
					break;
				case "Xlab":
					profilePlotSettings.XLabel = text2;
					break;
				case "MultiY":
					profilePlotSettings.IsMultipleY = text2 == "0";
					break;
				case "Nvars":
				{
					profilePlotSettings.NVars = int.Parse(text2, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
					if (PlotObject.Is3D)
					{
						profilePlotSettings.YAxes = new AxisInfo[2];
					}
					else
					{
						profilePlotSettings.YAxes = new AxisInfo[profilePlotSettings.NVars];
					}
					for (int j = 0; j < profilePlotSettings.YAxes.Length; j++)
					{
						profilePlotSettings.YAxes[j] = new AxisInfo();
					}
					break;
				}
				case "Var":
					num = int.Parse(text2, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
					break;
				case "Label":
					if (num > 0)
					{
						profilePlotSettings.YAxes[num - 1].Label = text2;
					}
					break;
				case "AxisMap":
					if (num > 0)
					{
						profilePlotSettings.YAxes[num - 1].AxisMap = int.Parse(text2, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
					}
					break;
				case "SubstValue":
					if (num > 0)
					{
						profilePlotSettings.YAxes[num - 1].SubstValue = text2;
					}
					break;
				case "Legend":
					if (num > 0)
					{
						profilePlotSettings.YAxes[num - 1].Legend = text2;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			return profilePlotSettings;
		}

		private ProfilePlotSettings Load3DPlotSettings(string settings)
		{
			ProfilePlotSettings profilePlotSettings = new ProfilePlotSettings();
			string[] array = settings.Split('\n');
			int num = 0;
			string[] array2 = array;
			for (int i = 0; i < array2.Length; i++)
			{
				string[] array3 = array2[i].Split(':');
				if (array3 == null || array3.Length != 2)
				{
					continue;
				}
				string text = array3[0];
				string text2 = array3[1];
				switch (text)
				{
				case "Title":
					profilePlotSettings.Title = text2;
					break;
				case "Nvars":
					profilePlotSettings.NVars = int.Parse(text2, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
					break;
				case "Var":
					num = int.Parse(text2, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
					break;
				case "Label":
					switch (num)
					{
					case 0:
						profilePlotSettings.XLabel = text2;
						break;
					case 2:
						profilePlotSettings.YLabel3D = text2;
						break;
					case 1:
						profilePlotSettings.ZLabel3D = text2;
						break;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			return profilePlotSettings;
		}

		private void UpdatePlotInputFile()
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append("Type:" + BoolToStr(Is3D) + "\n");
			stringBuilder.Append("Title:" + _plotSettings.Title + "\n");
			if (Is3D)
			{
				stringBuilder.Append("Nvars:1\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("Var:0\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("SubstValue:\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("Label:" + XLabel + "\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("Var:1\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("SubstValue:\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("Label:" + ZLabel3D + "\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("Var:2\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("SubstValue:\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("Label:" + YLabel3D + "\n");
			}
			else
			{
				stringBuilder.Append("Xlab:" + XLabel + "\n");
				string text = ((ProfileGroups.Count > 0) ? ProfileGroups[0].Name : string.Empty);
				stringBuilder.Append("Ylab:" + text + "\n");
				int num = 0;
				foreach (ProfileGroup profileGroup in ProfileGroups)
				{
					num += profileGroup.Profiles.Count;
				}
				stringBuilder.Append($"Nvars:{num}\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("MultiY:" + BoolToStr(!IsMultipleY) + "\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("Subst:UOM\n");
				stringBuilder.Append($"NYaxes:{ProfileGroups.Count}\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("RunPlot:0\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("PhasePlot:0\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("SizeToData:0\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("CommunicationIntervals:1\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("UseVar:0\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("IgnoreLegend:0\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("TimeWin:1\nTimeScroll: 0.2\nAlwaysShow: 0\nPhaseX:\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("Var:0\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("AxisMap:0\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("Label:" + XLabel + "\n");
				int num2 = 1;
				int num3 = 1;
				foreach (ProfileGroup profileGroup2 in ProfileGroups)
				{
					foreach (ProfileItem profile in profileGroup2.Profiles)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append($"Var:{num3}\n");
						stringBuilder.Append($"AxisMap:{num2}\n");
						stringBuilder.Append("Label:" + profileGroup2.Name + "\n");
						stringBuilder.Append("Legend:" + profile.Name + " $UOM\n");
						stringBuilder.Append("SubstValue:\n");
						num3++;
					}
					num2++;
				}
				stringBuilder.Append("LegendBox:0 1.000000 0.030000\nLegendBox2: 90 1.250000 5\n");
			}
			PlotObject.PlotInputFile = stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private string BoolToStr(bool b)
		{
			if (!b)
			{
				return "0";
			}
			return "1";
		}

	public enum TypeMode
	{
		Keyword,
		Parameter,
		Variable,
		Model,
		Port,
		Procedure
	}
	public class TypeDefinition : Window, IComponentConnector
	{
		public class Data
		{
			public string Word { get; set; }
		}

		private string sModelKeywords = "abs acos after always and as asin at atan before call componentlist connect convertto cos cosh create delay description difference do elif else elseif end endfor endif endparallel endstate endswitch endwhile endwith endwithin exp external fixed for foreach free global hidden if in initial input integer integerset intersection invoke link log10 loge max min model multiport not notype once or output parallel pause print private product ramp random rateinitial repeat restart retain return round runs sigma sin sinh size snapshot sqr sqrt sramp state string stringset switch symdiff tan tanh task tear then time truncate union until uses useuomof virtual wait when while with within xor";

		private string sScriptKeywords = "and byref byval call case class const dim do each else elseif empty end endif erase error execute exit explicit false for function get goto if in let loop next not nothing null on or option private property public randomize redim rem resume select set step stop sub then to true until wend while with xor";

		private TypeMode _typeMode;

		public string _sText;

		public string[] allWords;

		public int _iVisual;

		private string _sStart = "";

		internal System.Windows.Controls.DataGrid dataTypeList;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public AxEditorCtrl _EditorCtrl { get; private set; }

		public List<Data> GetWordList(string sWordSring)
		{
			List<Data> list = new List<Data>();
			allWords = sWordSring.Split(' ');
			string[] array = allWords;
			foreach (string word in array)
			{
				list.Add(new Data
				{
					Word = word
				});
			}
			return list;
		}

		public TypeDefinition(TypeMode mode, string text, ref AxEditorCtrl editorCtrl)
		{
			_EditorCtrl = editorCtrl;
			_typeMode = mode;
			_sText = text;
			InitializeComponent();
			SetTitle();
			int num = _EditorCtrl.IsModelEditor();
			if (_typeMode == TypeMode.Keyword && num == 1)
			{
				dataTypeList.ItemsSource = GetWordList(sModelKeywords);
			}
			if (_typeMode == TypeMode.Keyword && num == 0)
			{
				dataTypeList.ItemsSource = GetWordList(sScriptKeywords);
			}
			int currentLine = _EditorCtrl.GetCurrentLine();
			int startLinePos = _EditorCtrl.GetStartLinePos(currentLine);
			_EditorCtrl.GetEndLinePos(currentLine);
			int selectionStart = _EditorCtrl.GetSelectionStart();
			string lineText = _EditorCtrl.GetLineText((uint)currentLine);
			_ = lineText.Length;
			int num2 = selectionStart - startLinePos;
			int num3 = 0;
			if (num2 == 0)
			{
				num3 = 0;
			}
			string sStart = "";
			if (num2 > 0)
			{
				string text2 = lineText.Substring(0, num2);
				int length = text2.Length;
				if (text2[length - 1] == ' ')
				{
					num3 = 0;
				}
				else
				{
					if (text2.Contains(" "))
					{
						int num4 = text2.LastIndexOf(' ');
						sStart = text2.Substring(num4 + 1);
						sStart = sStart.Trim();
					}
					else
					{
						sStart = text2;
						sStart = sStart.Trim();
					}
					num3 = FindIndex(sStart);
				}
			}
			if (num3 == -1)
			{
				num3 = 0;
			}
			if (num3 >= 0)
			{
				_iVisual = 1;
				if (num3 == 0)
				{
					_sStart = "";
				}
				else
				{
					_sStart = sStart;
				}
			}
			if (_iVisual == 1)
			{
				System.Windows.Controls.DataGrid dataGrid = dataTypeList;
				dataGrid.SelectedIndex = num3;
				object itemAt = dataGrid.Items.GetItemAt(num3);
				dataGrid.ScrollIntoView(itemAt, null);
			}
		}

		private int FindIndex(string sStart)
		{
			int result = -1;
			int num = allWords.Length;
			string value = sStart.ToLower();
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				if (allWords[i].IndexOf(value, 0) == 0)
				{
					return i;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void SetTitle()
		{
			string title = "";
			if (_typeMode == TypeMode.Keyword)
			{
				title = CustomModelsResources.TextEditorContextMenu_CompleteKeyword;
			}
			base.Title = title;
		}

		private void DataTypeList_MouseDoubleClick(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			System.Windows.Controls.DataGrid dataGrid = sender as System.Windows.Controls.DataGrid;
			string text = "";
			if (dataGrid != null && dataGrid.SelectedItems != null)
			{
				string word = (dataGrid.SelectedItem as Data).Word;
				if (_sStart != "")
				{
					string value = _sStart.ToLower();
					int num = word.IndexOf(value, 0);
					if (num == 0)
					{
						int length = _sStart.Length;
						int selectionStart = _EditorCtrl.GetSelectionStart();
						text = word.Substring(length);
						_EditorCtrl.InsertText(text);
						int length2 = text.Length;
						_EditorCtrl.GotoPosition(selectionStart + length2);
					}
					if (num == -1)
					{
						int length3 = _sStart.Length;
						text = word.Substring(length3);
						int num2 = _EditorCtrl.GetSelectionStart() - length3;
						_EditorCtrl.GotoPosition(num2);
						int searchFlags = 0;
						_EditorCtrl.SetSearchFlags(searchFlags);
						_EditorCtrl.SearchNext(_sStart);
						_EditorCtrl.ReplaceSelectedText(word);
						_EditorCtrl.InsertText("");
						int length4 = word.Length;
						_EditorCtrl.GotoPosition(num2 + length4);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					int selectionStart2 = _EditorCtrl.GetSelectionStart();
					_EditorCtrl.InsertText(word);
					int length5 = word.Length;
					_EditorCtrl.GotoPosition(selectionStart2 + length5);
				}
				base.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)

	public class TypeDefinitionWindow : Window, IComponentConnector
	{
		private ACMModel _model;

		private AxEditorCtrl axEdCtrl;

		private string _text;

		private bool _hasLoaded;

		private static readonly Dictionary<Key, long> _specialKeyDic = new Dictionary<Key, long>
		{
			{
				Key.Tab,
				9L
			},
			{
				Key.Left,
				37L
			},
			{
				Key.Up,
				38L
			},
			{
				Key.Right,
				39L
			},
			{
				Key.Down,
				40L
			}
		};

		private const uint WM_KEYDOWN = 256u;

		private ICommand _gotoLineCommand;

		internal WindowsFormsHost formHost;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public ICommand GotoLineCommand
		{
			get
			{
				if (_gotoLineCommand == null)
				{
					_gotoLineCommand = (ICommand)new RelayActionCommand<object>((Action<object>)GotoLineCommand_Executed);
				}
				return _gotoLineCommand;
			}
		}

		public TypeDefinitionWindow(string title, string text, ACMModel model, IUnityContainer container)
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			base.Loaded += TypeDefinitionWindow_Loaded;
			base.Title = title;
			_text = text;
			_model = model;
			base.DataContext = this;
			axEdCtrl = new AxEditorCtrl();
			formHost.Child = axEdCtrl;
			axEdCtrl.CreateControl();
			((CompositePresentationEvent<CustomModelEditorClosedEventArgs>)(object)UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<CustomModelEditorClosedEvent>()).Subscribe((Action<CustomModelEditorClosedEventArgs>)OnCustomModelEditorClosedEvent);
		}

		private void OnCustomModelEditorClosedEvent(CustomModelEditorClosedEventArgs obj)
		{
			if (obj.Model.Equals(_model))
			{
				Close();
			}
		}

		private void TypeDefinitionWindow_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!_hasLoaded)
			{
				axEdCtrl.CreateEditorWithText((int)formHost.ActualWidth, (int)formHost.ActualHeight, _text, bScript: false);
				axEdCtrl.SetDisplayLineNumbers(9);
				axEdCtrl.IsReadOnly = true;
				axEdCtrl.ContextMenuOpening += AxEdCtrl_ContextMenuOpening;
				base.KeyDown += TextEditor_KeyDown;
				_hasLoaded = true;
			}
		}

		private void AxEdCtrl_ContextMenuOpening(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (FindResource("contextMenu") is System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu contextMenu)
			{
				contextMenu.IsOpen = true;
			}
		}

		[DllImport("user32.dll")]
		private static extern int SendMessage(IntPtr hWnd, uint Msg, long wParam, long lParam);

		private void TextEditor_KeyDown(object sender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_specialKeyDic.ContainsKey(e.Key))
			{
				SendMessage(axEdCtrl.Handle, 256u, _specialKeyDic[e.Key], 0L);
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		internal bool CanCopy()
		{
			return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(axEdCtrl.GetSelectedText());
		}

		private void CopyCommand_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.CanExecute = CanCopy();
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private void CopyCommand_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			axEdCtrl.Copy();
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		internal bool CanSelect()
		{
			return axEdCtrl.CanSelect;
		}

		private void SelectAllCommand_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.CanExecute = CanSelect();
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private void SelectAllCommand_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			axEdCtrl.SelectAll();
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private void GotoLineCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			GotoLine gotoLine = new GotoLine(axEdCtrl.GetCurrentLine(), axEdCtrl.GetLineCount())
			{
				Owner = Window.GetWindow(this)
			};
			if (gotoLine.ShowDialog() == true)
			{
				GotoLine(gotoLine.LineNumber);
			}
		}

		internal void GotoLine(int lineNumber)
		{
			axEdCtrl.GotoLine(lineNumber);
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/AspenTech.AspenPlus.CustomModels;component/dialogs/typedefinitionwindow.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				System.Windows.Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		internal Delegate _CreateDelegate(Type delegateType, string handler)
		{
			return Delegate.CreateDelegate(delegateType, this, handler);
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				((CommandBinding)target).CanExecute += CopyCommand_CanExecute;
				((CommandBinding)target).Executed += CopyCommand_Executed;
				break;
			case 2:
				((CommandBinding)target).CanExecute += SelectAllCommand_CanExecute;
				((CommandBinding)target).Executed += SelectAllCommand_Executed;
				break;
			case 3:
				formHost = (WindowsFormsHost)target;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}
	}

namespace AspenTech.AspenPlus.CustomModels.Controls
{
	public static class AcmEditorCreator
	{
		public static IAcmEditor CreateEditor(ACMObject acmObject)
		{
			IAcmEditor acmEditor = null;
			if (acmObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMHelp)
			{
				return new HelpEditor(acmObject);
			}
			if (acmObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMIcon)
			{
				return new IconEditor(acmObject);
			}
			if (acmObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMTable)
			{
				return new TableEditor(acmObject);
			}
			if (acmObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMPlot)
			{
				return new PlotEditor(acmObject);
			}
			return new TextEditor(acmObject);
		}
	}
	[DebuggerDisplay("{Type} - {Text}")]
	public class Token
	{
		public TokenTypes Type { get; set; }

		public string Text { get; set; }

		public int RowNumber { get; set; }

		public int ColumnNumber { get; set; }
	}
	public enum TokenTypes
	{
		Comment,
		Identifier,
		Keyword,
		Number,
		String,
		Punctuator
	}
	public sealed class ACMParser
	{
		private static readonly string[] Keywords = new string[20]
		{
			"Private", "Model", "Stream", "Variable", "Structure", "Procedure", "Port", "Parameter", "Uses", "End",
			"As", "If", "Then", "ElseIF", "Else", "EndIf", "For", "In", "Do", "EndFor"
		};

		private static readonly string[] Punctuators = new string[4] { "(", ")", ";", "," };

		private int _charIndex;

		private string _modelText;

		private int _rowNumber;

		private int _rowStartIndex;

		public readonly List<Token> AllTokens = new List<Token>();

		private List<Token> _validTokens;

		public TypeStatement TypeStatement;

		internal bool Compile(string modelText)
		{
			try
			{
				if (_modelText == modelText)
				{
					return true;
				}
				_charIndex = 0;
				_rowNumber = 1;
				_rowStartIndex = 0;
				AllTokens.Clear();
				_validTokens?.Clear();
				TypeStatement = null;
				if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(modelText))
				{
					return false;
				}
				_modelText = modelText;
				Scan();
				Parse();
				return true;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void Scan()
		{
			while (_charIndex < _modelText.Length)
			{
				char c = _modelText[_charIndex];
				if (char.IsWhiteSpace(c))
				{
					_charIndex++;
					if (c == '\n')
					{
						_rowStartIndex = _charIndex;
						_rowNumber++;
					}
				}
				else if (c == '/')
				{
					ScanComment();
				}
				else if (char.IsLetter(c))
				{
					ScanIdentifier();
				}
				else if (char.IsNumber(c))
				{
					ScanNumber();
				}
				else if (c == '"')
				{
					ScanString();
				}
				else
				{
					_charIndex++;
					AddToken(TokenTypes.Punctuator, c.ToString());
				}
			}
		}

		private void AddToken(TokenTypes type, string text)
		{
			AllTokens.Add(new Token
			{
				Type = type,
				Text = text,
				RowNumber = _rowNumber,
				ColumnNumber = _charIndex - text.Length - _rowStartIndex + 1
			});
		}

		private void ScanComment()
		{
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = false;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append('/');
			_charIndex++;
			if (_charIndex < _modelText.Length)
			{
				char c = _modelText[_charIndex];
				stringBuilder.Append(c);
				switch (c)
				{
				case '/':
					_charIndex++;
					flag = true;
					break;
				case '*':
					_charIndex++;
					flag2 = true;
					break;
				default:
					_charIndex--;
					break;
				}
			}
			while (_charIndex < _modelText.Length)
			{
				char c = _modelText[_charIndex++];
				if (flag)
				{
					if (c == '\r' || c == '\n')
					{
						AddToken(TokenTypes.Comment, stringBuilder.ToString());
						break;
					}
					stringBuilder.Append(c);
					if (_charIndex == _modelText.Length)
					{
						AddToken(TokenTypes.Comment, stringBuilder.ToString());
						break;
					}
					continue;
				}
				if (!flag2)
				{
					break;
				}
				if (c == '*')
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(c);
					if (_charIndex < _modelText.Length)
					{
						c = _modelText[_charIndex++];
						stringBuilder.Append(c);
						if (c == '/')
						{
							AddToken(TokenTypes.Comment, stringBuilder.ToString());
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(c);
				}
				if (_charIndex == _modelText.Length)
				{
					throw new Exception("Block comment should end with */.");
				}
			}
		}

		private void ScanIdentifier()
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			do
			{
				char c = _modelText[_charIndex];
				string[] keywords;
				if (!char.IsLetterOrDigit(c) && c != '_')
				{
					TokenTypes type = TokenTypes.Identifier;
					string text = stringBuilder.ToString();
					keywords = Keywords;
					foreach (string text2 in keywords)
					{
						if (string.Equals(text2, text, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
						{
							type = TokenTypes.Keyword;
							text = text2;
							break;
						}
					}
					AddToken(type, text);
					break;
				}
				stringBuilder.Append(c);
				_charIndex++;
				if (_charIndex != _modelText.Length)
				{
					continue;
				}
				TokenTypes type2 = TokenTypes.Identifier;
				string text3 = stringBuilder.ToString();
				keywords = Keywords;
				foreach (string text4 in keywords)
				{
					if (string.Equals(text4, text3, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
					{
						type2 = TokenTypes.Keyword;
						text3 = text4;
						break;
					}
				}
				AddToken(type2, text3);
				break;
			}
			while (_charIndex < _modelText.Length);
		}

		private void ScanNumber()
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = false;
			do
			{
				char c = _modelText[_charIndex++];
				if (char.IsDigit(c))
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(c);
					if (_charIndex == _modelText.Length)
					{
						AddToken(TokenTypes.Number, stringBuilder.ToString());
						break;
					}
					continue;
				}
				switch (c)
				{
				case '.':
					stringBuilder.Append(c);
					if (flag)
					{
						throw new Exception($"Invalid number at line {_rowNumber}: {stringBuilder.ToString()}");
					}
					flag = true;
					break;
				case 'E':
				case 'e':
					stringBuilder.Append(c);
					if (flag2)
					{
						throw new Exception($"Invalid number at line {_rowNumber}: {stringBuilder.ToString()}");
					}
					flag2 = true;
					c = _modelText[_charIndex++];
					if (c == '-')
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(c);
					}
					break;
				default:
					_charIndex--;
					AddToken(TokenTypes.Number, stringBuilder.ToString());
					return;
				}
			}
			while (_charIndex < _modelText.Length);
		}

		private void ScanString()
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			_charIndex++;
			while (_charIndex < _modelText.Length)
			{
				char c = _modelText[_charIndex++];
				if (c == '"')
				{
					AddToken(TokenTypes.String, stringBuilder.ToString());
					break;
				}
				stringBuilder.Append(c);
				if (_charIndex == _modelText.Length)
				{
					throw new Exception("String should end with \".");
				}
			}
		}

		private void Parse()
		{
			if (AllTokens.Count != 0)
			{
				_validTokens = AllTokens.Where((Token x) => x.Type != TokenTypes.Comment).ToList();
				ParseTypeStatement(null, 0, _validTokens.Count - 1);
			}
		}

		private void ParseTypeStatement(TypeStatement parentStatement, int startIndex, int endIndex)
		{
			TypeStatement typeStatement = null;
			Token token = null;
			endIndex--;
			while (startIndex < endIndex)
			{
				token = _validTokens[startIndex++];
				if (token.Type != TokenTypes.Keyword)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (token.Text == "Model")
				{
					ModelStatement modelStatement = new ModelStatement();
					if (startIndex >= 2)
					{
						token = _validTokens[startIndex - 2];
						if (token.Type == TokenTypes.Keyword && token.Text == "Private")
						{
							modelStatement.IsPrivate = true;
						}
					}
					typeStatement = modelStatement;
				}
				else if (token.Text == "Stream" || token.Text == "Variable" || token.Text == "Structure" || token.Text == "Procedure" || token.Text == "Port" || token.Text == "Parameter")
				{
					typeStatement = new TypeStatement();
				}
				if (typeStatement == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				token = _validTokens[startIndex++];
				typeStatement.Name = token.Text;
				token = _validTokens[startIndex];
				if (token.Type == TokenTypes.Keyword && token.Text == "Uses")
				{
					startIndex++;
					token = _validTokens[startIndex++];
					typeStatement.UsedTypeName = token.Text;
				}
				if (parentStatement == null)
				{
					TypeStatement = typeStatement;
				}
				else
				{
					parentStatement.Statements.Add(typeStatement);
				}
				StatementTypes statementTypes = StatementTypes.None;
				int num = 0;
				while (startIndex < endIndex)
				{
					num = startIndex;
					while (startIndex <= endIndex)
					{
						token = _validTokens[startIndex++];
						if (token.Type == TokenTypes.Keyword)
						{
							if (token.Text == "Stream" || token.Text == "Variable" || token.Text == "Structure" || token.Text == "Procedure" || token.Text == "Port" || token.Text == "Parameter")
							{
								statementTypes = StatementTypes.TypeStatement;
								continue;
							}
							if (token.Text == "As")
							{
								if (statementTypes != StatementTypes.TypeStatement)
								{
									statementTypes = StatementTypes.DeclarationStatement;
								}
								continue;
							}
							if (token.Text == "If")
							{
								statementTypes = StatementTypes.IfStatement;
								continue;
							}
							if (token.Text == "For")
							{
								statementTypes = StatementTypes.ForStatement;
								continue;
							}
							if (token.Text == "End")
							{
								ParseTypeStatement(typeStatement, num, startIndex - 1);
								statementTypes = StatementTypes.None;
								break;
							}
							if (token.Text == "EndIf")
							{
								ParseIfStatement(typeStatement, num, startIndex - 1);
								statementTypes = StatementTypes.None;
								break;
							}
							if (token.Text == "EndFor")
							{
								ParseForStatement(typeStatement, num, startIndex - 1);
								statementTypes = StatementTypes.None;
								break;
							}
						}
						else if (statementTypes != StatementTypes.TypeStatement && token.Type == TokenTypes.Punctuator && token.Text == ";")
						{
							if (statementTypes == StatementTypes.DeclarationStatement)
							{
								ParseDeclarationStatement(typeStatement, num, startIndex - 1);
								statementTypes = StatementTypes.None;
							}
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			if (typeStatement == null)
			{
				throw new Exception("Invalid type.");
			}
			token = _validTokens[endIndex + 1];
			if (token.Type != TokenTypes.Keyword || token.Text != "End")
			{
				throw new Exception("Type should end with end keyword.");
			}
		}

		private void ParseDeclarationStatement(TypeStatement parentStatement, int startIndex, int endIndex)
		{
			DeclarationStatement declarationStatement = new DeclarationStatement();
			List<DeclarationStatement> list = new List<DeclarationStatement>();
			new StringBuilder();
			Token token = null;
			List<Token> list2 = new List<Token>();
			do
			{
				token = _validTokens[startIndex++];
				declarationStatement.Name = token.Text;
				token = _validTokens[startIndex++];
				if (token.Type == TokenTypes.Punctuator && token.Text == "(")
				{
					while (startIndex < endIndex)
					{
						token = _validTokens[startIndex++];
						if (token.Type == TokenTypes.Punctuator && token.Text == ")")
						{
							declarationStatement.ArrayList = list2.ToArray();
							break;
						}
						list2.Add(token);
					}
				}
				list.Add(new DeclarationStatement
				{
					Name = declarationStatement.Name,
					ArrayList = declarationStatement.ArrayList
				});
			}
			while (token.Type == TokenTypes.Punctuator && token.Text == ",");
			while (startIndex < endIndex && (token.Type != TokenTypes.Keyword || !(token.Text == "As")))
			{
				token = _validTokens[startIndex++];
			}
			endIndex--;
			token = _validTokens[endIndex];
			list2.Clear();
			if (token.Type == TokenTypes.Punctuator && token.Text == ")")
			{
				endIndex--;
				int num = 0;
				while (startIndex < endIndex)
				{
					token = _validTokens[endIndex--];
					if (token.Type == TokenTypes.Punctuator && token.Text == ")")
					{
						num++;
					}
					else if (token.Type == TokenTypes.Punctuator && token.Text == "(")
					{
						if (num == 0)
						{
							declarationStatement.AssignmentList = list2.ToArray();
							break;
						}
						num--;
					}
					else
					{
						list2.Insert(0, token);
					}
				}
			}
			token = _validTokens[endIndex];
			declarationStatement.TypeName = token.Text;
			list2.Clear();
			while (startIndex < endIndex)
			{
				list2.Add(token = _validTokens[startIndex++]);
			}
			declarationStatement.FlagList = list2.ToArray();
			if (list.Count > 1)
			{
				foreach (DeclarationStatement item in list)
				{
					parentStatement.Statements.Add(new DeclarationStatement
					{
						Name = item.Name,
						ArrayList = item.ArrayList,
						AssignmentList = declarationStatement.AssignmentList,
						TypeName = declarationStatement.TypeName,
						FlagList = declarationStatement.FlagList
					});
				}
				return;
			}
			parentStatement.Statements.Add(declarationStatement);
		}

		private void ParseIfStatement(TypeStatement parentStatement, int startIndex, int endIndex)
		{
			IfStatement item = new IfStatement();
			List<Token> list = new List<Token>();
			while (startIndex < endIndex)
			{
				Token item2 = _validTokens[startIndex++];
				list.Add(item2);
			}
			parentStatement.Statements.Add(item);
		}

		private void ParseForStatement(TypeStatement parentStatement, int startIndex, int endIndex)
		{
			ForStatement item = new ForStatement();
			List<Token> list = new List<Token>();
			while (startIndex < endIndex)
			{
				Token item2 = _validTokens[startIndex++];
				list.Add(item2);
			}
			parentStatement.Statements.Add(item);
		}
	}
	public abstract class Statement
	{
	}
	[DebuggerDisplay("{Name} - {UsedTypeName}")]
	public class TypeStatement : Statement
	{
		public readonly List<Statement> Statements = new List<Statement>();

		public virtual TypeKeywords Keyword { get; }

		public string Name { get; set; }

		public string UsedTypeName { get; set; }
	}
	public enum TypeKeywords
	{
		Model,
		Stream,
		Variable,
		Structure,
		Procedure,
		Port,
		Parameter
	}
	[DebuggerDisplay("{Name}")]
	public class ModelStatement : TypeStatement
	{
		public override TypeKeywords Keyword => TypeKeywords.Model;

		public bool IsPrivate { get; set; }
	}
	public enum StatementTypes
	{
		None,
		DeclarationStatement,
		TypeStatement,
		IfStatement,
		ForStatement
	}
	[DebuggerDisplay("{Name} - {TypeName}")]
	public class DeclarationStatement : Statement
	{
		public string Name { get; set; }

		public Token[] ArrayList { get; set; }

		public Token[] FlagList { get; set; }

		public string TypeName { get; set; }

		public Token[] AssignmentList { get; set; }
	}
	[DebuggerDisplay("IF")]
	public class IfStatement : Statement
	{
		public Token[] Tokens { get; set; }
	}
	[DebuggerDisplay("FOR")]
	public class ForStatement : Statement
	{
		public Token[] Tokens { get; set; }
	}
	public class ACMTreeNode
	{
		private ImageSource _image;

		public string Text { get; set; }

		public ImageSource Image
		{
			get
			{
				if (_image == null)
				{
					if (ModelObject is ACMHelp)
					{
						_image = ImageResources.ModelHelp16;
					}
					else if (ModelObject is ACMPlot)
					{
						_image = ImageResources.FormPlot16;
					}
					else if (ModelObject is ACMScript)
					{
						_image = ImageResources.Script16;
					}
					else if (ModelObject is ACMTask)
					{
						_image = ImageResources.Task16;
					}
					else if (ModelObject is ACMIcon)
					{
						_image = ImageResources.Icon16;
					}
					else if (ModelObject is ACMTable)
					{
						_image = ImageResources.FormInstance16;
					}
					else if (ModelObject is ACMProcedure)
					{
						_image = ImageResources.Procedures16;
					}
					else if (ModelObject is ACMSubmodel)
					{
						_image = ImageResources.Model16;
					}
					else if (ModelObject is ACMCustomVariableType)
					{
						_image = ImageResources.VariableType16;
					}
					else if (ModelObject is ACMCustomParameterType)
					{
						_image = ImageResources.ParameterType16;
					}
				}
				return _image;
			}
			set
			{
				_image = value;
			}
		}

		public bool IsSelected { get; set; }

		public ObservableCollection<ACMTreeNode> Children { get; set; }

		public ACMObject ModelObject { get; set; }

		public NodeType NodeType { get; set; }
	}
	public enum NodeType
	{
		Form,
		Folder
	}
