	public class CustomTableDataTable : DataTable
	{
		private ACMTable _acmTable;

		public CustomTableDataTable(ACMTable acmTable)
			: base((IEnumerable<DataColumn>)null)
		{
			_acmTable = acmTable;
			InitializeDataTable();
		}

		private void InitializeDataTable()
		{
			InitializeColumns();
			InitializeRows();
		}

		private void InitializeColumns()
		{
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0023: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0031: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0059: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_006a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0076: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0089: Expected O, but got Unknown
			List<DataColumn> list = new List<DataColumn>
			{
				new DataColumn
				{
					Tag = ACMAttribute.Name,
					Header = GetDisplayedTextOfAttribute(ACMAttribute.Name),
					Width = 100.0,
					IsReadOnly = true
				}
			};
			foreach (ACMAttribute attribute in _acmTable.Attributes)
			{
				list.Add(new DataColumn
				{
					Tag = attribute,
					Header = GetDisplayedTextOfAttribute(attribute),
					Width = 100.0
				});
			}
			((DataTable)this).Columns = new ReadOnlyCollection<DataColumn>(list);
		}

		private void InitializeRows()
		{
			//IL_001b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0020: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0028: Expected O, but got Unknown
			foreach (ACMVariable variable in _acmTable.Variables)
			{
				DataRow item = new DataRow((DataTable)(object)this)
				{
					Tag = variable
				};
				((DataTable)this).Rows.Add(item);
			}
		}

		public static string GetDisplayedTextOfAttribute(ACMAttribute attribute)
		{
			return attribute switch
			{
				ACMAttribute.Derivative => CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_AttributeText_Derivative, 
				ACMAttribute.Description => CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_AttributeText_Description, 
				ACMAttribute.Lower => CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_AttributeText_Lower, 
				ACMAttribute.Record => CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_AttributeText_Record, 
				ACMAttribute.Scale => CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_AttributeText_Scale, 
				ACMAttribute.Spec => CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_AttributeText_Spec, 
				ACMAttribute.Tag => CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_AttributeText_Tag, 
				ACMAttribute.Units => CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_AttributeText_Units, 
				ACMAttribute.Upper => CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_AttributeText_Upper, 
				ACMAttribute.Value => CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_AttributeText_Value, 
				ACMAttribute.Name => CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_AttributeText_Name, 
				ACMAttribute.Type => CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_AttributeText_Type, 
				_ => null, 
			};
		}

		public static ACMAttribute GetAttributePerDisplayText(string displayText)
		{
			if (displayText.Equals(CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_AttributeText_Derivative))
			{
				return ACMAttribute.Derivative;
			}
			if (displayText.Equals(CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_AttributeText_Description))
			{
				return ACMAttribute.Description;
			}
			if (displayText.Equals(CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_AttributeText_Lower))
			{
				return ACMAttribute.Lower;
			}
			if (displayText.Equals(CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_AttributeText_Record))
			{
				return ACMAttribute.Record;
			}
			if (displayText.Equals(CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_AttributeText_Scale))
			{
				return ACMAttribute.Scale;
			}
			if (displayText.Equals(CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_AttributeText_Spec))
			{
				return ACMAttribute.Spec;
			}
			if (displayText.Equals(CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_AttributeText_Tag))
			{
				return ACMAttribute.Tag;
			}
			if (displayText.Equals(CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_AttributeText_Units))
			{
				return ACMAttribute.Units;
			}
			if (displayText.Equals(CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_AttributeText_Upper))
			{
				return ACMAttribute.Upper;
			}
			if (displayText.Equals(CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_AttributeText_Value))
			{
				return ACMAttribute.Value;
			}
			if (displayText.Equals(CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_AttributeText_Name))
			{
				return ACMAttribute.Name;
			}
			if (displayText.Equals(CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_AttributeText_Type))
			{
				return ACMAttribute.Type;
			}
			return ACMAttribute.None;
		}

		protected override IDataCell OnCellValueQueried(DataRow row, DataColumn column)
		{
			//IL_003b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0041: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0064: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_006a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_008d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0093: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_00b7: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00bd: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_00e1: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00e7: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_014b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0151: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_011e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0124: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0113: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0119: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_01b5: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01bb: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0188: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_018e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_017d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0183: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_01de: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01e4: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_021a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0220: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_020f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0215: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0256: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_025c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_024b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0251: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ACMVariable aCMVariable = row.Tag as ACMVariable;
			ACMAttribute? aCMAttribute = column.Tag as ACMAttribute?;
			if (aCMAttribute == ACMAttribute.Name)
			{
				return (IDataCell)new DataCellText(aCMVariable.Name);
			}
			if (aCMAttribute == ACMAttribute.Value)
			{
				return (IDataCell)new DataCellText(aCMVariable.Value.ToString());
			}
			if (aCMAttribute == ACMAttribute.Spec)
			{
				return (IDataCell)new DataCellText(aCMVariable.Spec.ToString());
			}
			if (aCMAttribute == ACMAttribute.Lower)
			{
				return (IDataCell)new DataCellText(aCMVariable.Lower.ToString());
			}
			if (aCMAttribute == ACMAttribute.Upper)
			{
				return (IDataCell)new DataCellText(aCMVariable.Upper.ToString());
			}
			if (aCMAttribute == ACMAttribute.Description)
			{
				if (aCMVariable.Description == null)
				{
					return (IDataCell)new DataCellText("");
				}
				return (IDataCell)new DataCellText(aCMVariable.Description.ToString());
			}
			if (aCMAttribute == ACMAttribute.Scale)
			{
				return (IDataCell)new DataCellText(aCMVariable.Scale.ToString());
			}
			if (aCMAttribute == ACMAttribute.Units)
			{
				if (aCMVariable.Units == null)
				{
					return (IDataCell)new DataCellText("");
				}
				return (IDataCell)new DataCellText(aCMVariable.Units.ToString());
			}
			if (aCMAttribute == ACMAttribute.Derivative)
			{
				return (IDataCell)new DataCellText(aCMVariable.Derivative.ToString());
			}
			if (aCMAttribute == ACMAttribute.Record)
			{
				return (IDataCell)new DataCellText(aCMVariable.Record.ToString());
			}
			if (aCMAttribute == ACMAttribute.Tag)
			{
				if (aCMVariable.Tag == null)
				{
					return (IDataCell)new DataCellText("");
				}
				return (IDataCell)new DataCellText(aCMVariable.Tag.ToString());
			}
			if (aCMAttribute == ACMAttribute.Type)
			{
				if (aCMVariable.Type == null)
				{
					return (IDataCell)new DataCellText("");
				}
				return (IDataCell)new DataCellText(aCMVariable.Type.ToString());
			}
			return ((DataTable)this).OnCellValueQueried(row, column);
		}
	}

		public bool AllowMultipleSelection
		{

		private void UpdateSelectedVariableList()
		{
			//IL_003e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0043: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			gridSelectedVars.DataSource.Rows.Clear();

			{

				{
					Tag = selectedVariable
				};
				val[0] = DataRow.CreateDataCell((object)selectedVariable.Name, true, (IEnumerable)null, (CellUIType)0, (object)null, (System.Windows.Media.Brush)null, (Type)null, (string)null, (string)null, (System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment?)null, (string)null, (Thickness?)null, (System.Windows.Media.Brush)null, false);
				val[0].Tooltip = selectedVariable.Name;
				val[1] = DataRow.CreateDataCell((object)selectedVariable.Description, false, (IEnumerable)null, (CellUIType)0, (object)null, (System.Windows.Media.Brush)null, (Type)null, (string)null, (string)null, (System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment?)null, (string)null, (Thickness?)null, (System.Windows.Media.Brush)null, false);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(selectedVariable.Description))
				{

				list.Add(val);
			}
			gridSelectedVars.DataSource.UpdateRows((IEnumerable<DataRow>)list);
		}

		private bool DoesSpecMatch(ACMVariable variable)
		{
			if (IsArrayVarChecked && variable.IsArray)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (IsFixedVarChecked && variable.Spec == "Fixed")
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (IsFreeVarChecked && variable.Spec == "Free")
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (IsParameterChecked && variable.IsParameter)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void UpdateAvaiableVariableList()
		{
			//IL_00dd: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00e2: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00eb: Expected O, but got Unknown
			List<DataRow> list = new List<DataRow>();
			string value = searchBox.SearchValue?.ToLowerInvariant();
			foreach (ACMVariable item in AvailableVariables.OrderBy((ACMVariable v) => v.Name))
			{
				bool num = string.IsNullOrEmpty(value) || item.Name.StartsWith(value, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase);
				bool flag = SelectedVariables != null && SelectedVariables.Contains(item);
				bool flag2 = SelectedPhysicalType.Equals(_physicalType_All) || SelectedPhysicalType.Equals(item.Type);
				bool flag3 = DoesSpecMatch(item);
				if (num && !flag && flag2 && flag3)
				{
					DataRow val = new DataRow(gridAvailableVars.DataSource)
					{
						Tag = item
					};
					val[0] = DataRow.CreateDataCell((object)item.Name, true, (IEnumerable)null, (CellUIType)0, (object)null, (System.Windows.Media.Brush)null, (Type)null, (string)null, (string)null, (System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment?)null, (string)null, (Thickness?)null, (System.Windows.Media.Brush)null, false);
					val[0].Tooltip = item.Name;
					val[1] = DataRow.CreateDataCell((object)item.Description, false, (IEnumerable)null, (CellUIType)0, (object)null, (System.Windows.Media.Brush)null, (Type)null, (string)null, (string)null, (System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment?)null, (string)null, (Thickness?)null, (System.Windows.Media.Brush)null, false);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.Description))
					{
						val[1].Tooltip = item.Description;
					}
					list.Add(val);
				}
			}
			gridAvailableVars.DataSource.UpdateRows((IEnumerable<DataRow>)list);
		}

		private void OnAllowMultipleSelectionChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			if (newValue)
			{
				gridAvailableVars.SelectionMode = DataGridSelectionMode.Extended;
			}
			else
			{
				gridAvailableVars.SelectionMode = DataGridSelectionMode.Single;
			}
		}

		private static void OnSelectedVariablesPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is FindVariablesControl findVariablesControl)
			{
				findVariablesControl.OnSelectedVariablesChanged();
			}
		}

		private void OnAvaiableVariablesChanged()
		{
			Collection<string> first = new Collection<string> { _physicalType_All };
			Collection<string> second = new Collection<string>((from p in AvailableVariables
				where !string.IsNullOrEmpty(p.Type)
				select p.Type).ToArray());
			AllPhysicalTypes = new Collection<string>(first.Union(second).ToArray());
			SelectedPhysicalType = AllPhysicalTypes[0];
			UpdateAvaiableVariableList();
		}

		private void OnSelectedVariablesChanged()
		{
			UpdateSelectedVariableList();
		}

		private bool AddCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (gridAvailableVars.SelectedRows == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!AllowMultipleSelection && (gridAvailableVars.SelectedRows.Count() != 1 || SelectedVariables.Count > 0))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return gridAvailableVars.SelectedRows.Select((DataRow row) => row.Tag as ACMVariable).Any((ACMVariable v) => !SelectedVariables.Contains(v));
		}

		private void AddCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			foreach (ACMVariable item in gridAvailableVars.SelectedRows.Select((DataRow row) => row.Tag as ACMVariable))
			{
				if (!SelectedVariables.Contains(item))
				{
					SelectedVariables.Add(item);
				}
			}
			UpdateSelectedVariableList();
			UpdateAvaiableVariableList();
		}

		private bool RemoveCommand_CanExeucte(object arg)
		{
			if (gridSelectedVars.SelectedRows != null)
			{
				return gridSelectedVars.SelectedRows.Count() > 0;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void RemoveCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			foreach (ACMVariable item in gridSelectedVars.SelectedRows.Select((DataRow row) => row.Tag as ACMVariable))
			{
				if (SelectedVariables.Contains(item))
				{
					SelectedVariables.Remove(item);
				}
				if (!AvailableVariables.Contains(item))
				{
					AvailableVariables.Add(item);
				}
			}
			UpdateSelectedVariableList();
			UpdateAvaiableVariableList();
		}

		private void searchBox_SearchBoxTextChangedEvent(string strNewText)
		{
			UpdateAvaiableVariableList();
		}

		{
			this.PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
		}

		}

		private void OpenFileCommand_Executed(object arg)
		{
			System.Windows.Forms.OpenFileDialog openFileDialog = new System.Windows.Forms.OpenFileDialog
			{
				CheckFileExists = true,
				Filter = CustomModelsResources.HelpEditor_FileFilter
			};
			if (openFileDialog.ShowDialog() == DialogResult.OK)
			{
				HelpObject.FileName = openFileDialog.FileName;
				DisplayHelpContent();
				isHelpModified = true;
			}
		}

		private void DisplayHelpContent()
		{
			if (string.Equals(tbFileName.Text, HelpObject.FileName, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				helpContentBrowser.Refresh();
				return;
			}
			tbFileName.Text = HelpObject.FileName;
			helpContentBrowser.Url = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(HelpObject.FileName) ? null : new Uri(HelpObject.FileName));
		}

		private bool OpenMSWordCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			string name = "SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows\\CurrentVersion\\App Paths\\Winword.exe";
			RegistryKey registryKey = Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey(name, writable: false);
			string text = tbFileName.Text;
			if (registryKey != null)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					return IsSingleFileWebPage(text);
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private bool IsSingleFileWebPage(string fileName)
		{
			if (!Path.GetExtension(fileName).Equals(".mht", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				return Path.GetExtension(fileName).Equals(".mhtml", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase);
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void OpenMSWordCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			if (_wordApp == null)
			{
				_wordApp = (Microsoft.Office.Interop.Word.Application)Activator.CreateInstance(Marshal.GetTypeFromCLSID(new Guid("000209FF-0000-0000-C000-000000000046")));
				new ComAwareEventInfo(typeof(ApplicationEvents4_Event), "Quit").AddEventHandler(_wordApp, new ApplicationEvents4_QuitEventHandler(WordApp_Quit));
				_wordApp.Visible = true;
				_wordApp.Activate();
			}
			try
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(HelpObject.FileName))
				{
					Documents documents = _wordApp.Documents;
					object Template = Type.Missing;
					object NewTemplate = Type.Missing;
					object DocumentType = Type.Missing;
					object Visible = Type.Missing;
					_wordDoc = documents.Add(ref Template, ref NewTemplate, ref DocumentType, ref Visible);
				}
				else
				{
					Documents documents2 = _wordApp.Documents;
					object Visible = HelpObject.FileName;
					object DocumentType = Type.Missing;
					object NewTemplate = Type.Missing;
					object Template = Type.Missing;
					object PasswordDocument = Type.Missing;
					object PasswordTemplate = Type.Missing;
					object Revert = Type.Missing;
					object WritePasswordDocument = Type.Missing;
					object WritePasswordTemplate = Type.Missing;
					object Format = Type.Missing;
					object Encoding = Type.Missing;
					object Visible2 = Type.Missing;
					object OpenAndRepair = Type.Missing;
					object DocumentDirection = Type.Missing;
					object NoEncodingDialog = Type.Missing;
					object XMLTransform = Type.Missing;
					_wordDoc = documents2.Open(ref Visible, ref DocumentType, ref NewTemplate, ref Template, ref PasswordDocument, ref PasswordTemplate, ref Revert, ref WritePasswordDocument, ref WritePasswordTemplate, ref Format, ref Encoding, ref Visible2, ref OpenAndRepair, ref DocumentDirection, ref NoEncodingDialog, ref XMLTransform);
				}
				new ComAwareEventInfo(typeof(DocumentEvents2_Event), "Close").AddEventHandler(_wordDoc, new DocumentEvents2_CloseEventHandler(_wordDoc_DocumentClose));
				_isMSWordOpen = true;
				btnOpenMSWord.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				btnSaveMSWord.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				System.Windows.MessageBox.Show(ex.Message);
			}
		}

		private void SaveMSWordCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			object CompatibilityMode;
			object AddBiDiMarks;
			object LineEnding;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(HelpObject.FileName))
			{
				HelpObject.FileName = Path.Combine(Directory.GetCurrentDirectory(), $"{HelpObject.Name}_Help.mht");
				Document wordDoc = _wordDoc;
				object FileName = HelpObject.FileName;
				object FileFormat = WdSaveFormat.wdFormatWebArchive;
				object LockComments = Type.Missing;
				object Password = Type.Missing;
				object AddToRecentFiles = Type.Missing;
				object WritePassword = Type.Missing;
				object ReadOnlyRecommended = Type.Missing;
				object EmbedTrueTypeFonts = Type.Missing;
				object SaveNativePictureFormat = Type.Missing;
				object SaveFormsData = Type.Missing;
				object SaveAsAOCELetter = Type.Missing;
				object Encoding = Type.Missing;
				object InsertLineBreaks = Type.Missing;
				object AllowSubstitutions = Type.Missing;
				LineEnding = Type.Missing;
				AddBiDiMarks = Type.Missing;
				CompatibilityMode = Type.Missing;
				wordDoc.SaveAs2(ref FileName, ref FileFormat, ref LockComments, ref Password, ref AddToRecentFiles, ref WritePassword, ref ReadOnlyRecommended, ref EmbedTrueTypeFonts, ref SaveNativePictureFormat, ref SaveFormsData, ref SaveAsAOCELetter, ref Encoding, ref InsertLineBreaks, ref AllowSubstitutions, ref LineEnding, ref AddBiDiMarks, ref CompatibilityMode);
			}
			else
			{
				_wordDoc.Save();
			}
			_wordDoc = null;
			Microsoft.Office.Interop.Word.Application wordApp = _wordApp;
			CompatibilityMode = Type.Missing;
			AddBiDiMarks = Type.Missing;
			LineEnding = Type.Missing;
			wordApp.Quit(ref CompatibilityMode, ref AddBiDiMarks, ref LineEnding);
			_wordApp = null;
			_isMSWordOpen = false;
			btnOpenMSWord.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
			btnSaveMSWord.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			DisplayHelpContent();
			isHelpModified = true;
		}

		public void Save()
		{
			//IL_00a4: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00ab: Expected O, but got Unknown
			_ = HelpObject.FileName;
			string sTempDir = HelpObject.sTempDir;
			bool flag = false;
			if (!Directory.Exists(sTempDir))
			{
				Directory.CreateDirectory(sTempDir);
				flag = true;
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				string[] files = Directory.GetFiles(sTempDir, "*.htmlz");
				for (int i = 0; i < files.Length; i++)
				{
					File.Delete(files[i]);
				}
			}
			string text = HelpObject.Name.Replace("Help", "").Trim() + ".htmlz";
			string text2 = sTempDir + "\\" + text;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(HelpObject.FileName))
			{
				AspenPackage val = new AspenPackage();
				if (val.Open(text2, FileMode.Create, true))
				{
					string text3 = "HTML Help File";
					string text4 = "Single File Web Page";
					string text5 = "";
					string extension = Path.GetExtension(HelpObject.FileName);
					extension = extension.ToLower();
					if (extension == ".htm" || extension == ".html")
					{
						text5 = text3;
					}
					if (extension == ".mht" || extension == ".mhtml")
					{
						text5 = text4;
					}
					string fileName = Path.GetFileName(HelpObject.FileName);
					val.CreateRootPartWithSpecifiedUri(fileName, HelpObject.FileName, text5);
				}
				val.Close();
				HelpObject.ParentModel.ACMDoc.SetModelHelp(HelpObject.ParentModel.ModelTypeName, text);
			}
			isHelpModified = false;
		}

				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/AspenTech.AspenPlus.CustomModels;component/controls/helpeditor.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				System.Windows.Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		public List<System.Windows.Media.Color> ColorList { get; } = new List<System.Windows.Media.Color>
		{

		public List<string> IconFontNameList
		{
			get
			{
				if (_iconFontNameList == null)
				{
					_iconFontNameList = new List<string>();
					System.Drawing.FontFamily[] families = new InstalledFontCollection().Families;
					foreach (System.Drawing.FontFamily fontFamily in families)
					{
						_iconFontNameList.Add(fontFamily.Name);
					}
				}
				return _iconFontNameList;
			}
		}

		public List<string> IconFontSizeList
		{
			get
			{
				if (_iconFontSizeList == null)
				{
					_iconFontSizeList = new List<string>();
					for (int i = 1; i <= 30; i++)
					{
						_iconFontSizeList.Add(i.ToString());
					}
				}
				return _iconFontSizeList;
			}
		}

		public bool GetEnableCompileBtn()
		{
			return true;
		}

		public IconEditor(ACMObject acmIcon)
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			AcmObject = acmIcon;
			base.DataContext = this;
		}

		public void OnClosed()
		{
			iconEditor.Dispose();
		}

		public void OnLoaded()
		{
			//IL_00b7: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!_hasLoaded)
			{
				IAModelIcon targetModelIcon = IconObject.TargetModelIcon;
				try
				{
					iconEditor.ResetActiveCtr();
					if (targetModelIcon != null)
					{
						iconEditor.SetACMModelIcon(targetModelIcon);
					}
					iconEditor.SetAskForSaveWhenClose(bAsk: false);
					iconEditor.IconModified += IconEditor_IconModified;
					iconEditor.Selected += IconEditor_Selected;
					iconEditor.HistoryModified += IconEditor_HistoryModified;
					base.KeyDown += IconEditor_KeyDown;
					SetContextMenuShortcutFunction();
					UpdateTextAttributes();
				}
				catch (Exception ex)
				{
					MMMessageBox.Show(ex.Message, MMVersionHelper.MVGetProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
					return;
				}
				_hasLoaded = true;
			}
			GetUndoRedoList();
		}

		private void IconEditor_HistoryModified(object sender, _DIconEditorAxEvents_HistoryModifiedEvent e)
		{
			GetUndoRedoList();
			if ((_undoItems != null && _undoItems.StartsWith("<Item>Chg Fillmode box</Item>")) || (_redoItems != null && _redoItems.EndsWith("<Item>Chg Fillmode box</Item>")))
			{
				UpdateTextAttributes();
			}
		}

		private void IconEditor_Selected(object sender, _DIconEditorAxEvents_SelectedEvent e)
		{
			UpdateTextAttributes();
		}

		private void IconEditor_KeyDown(object sender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (TextEditor.SpecialKeyDic.ContainsKey(e.Key))
			{
				iconEditor.KeyDown((int)TextEditor.SpecialKeyDic[e.Key], 1, 0);
				e.Handled = true;
				return;
			}
			foreach (KeyGesture key in m_dicGests.Keys)
			{
				if (key.Matches(null, e))
				{
					m_dicGests[key](this, e);
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
		}

		private void IconEditor_IconModified(object sender, _DIconEditorAxEvents_IconModifiedEvent e)
		{
			IsModified = true;
		}

		public void OnUnloaded()
		{
		}

		public void OnClosing()
		{
		}

		public bool CanUndo()
		{
			return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_undoItems);
		}

		public bool CanRedo()
		{
			return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_redoItems);
		}

		public void Undo()
		{
			if (CanUndo())
			{
				iconEditor.SetOneStepUndo();
				GetUndoRedoList();
			}
		}

		public void Redo()
		{
			if (CanRedo())
			{
				iconEditor.SetOneStepRedo();
				GetUndoRedoList();
			}
		}

		public void Save()
		{
			if (IsModified)
			{
				iconEditor.SaveCurrentIcon();
				IsModified = false;
			}
		}

		private void SetContextMenuShortcutFunction()
		{
			m_dicGests.Clear();
			m_dicGests.Add(new KeyGesture(Key.Delete), ContextMenuDeleteObject);
			m_dicGests.Add(new KeyGesture(Key.C, ModifierKeys.Control), ContextMenuCopyObject);
			m_dicGests.Add(new KeyGesture(Key.V, ModifierKeys.Control), ContextMenuPasteObject);
			m_dicGests.Add(new KeyGesture(Key.X, ModifierKeys.Control), ContextMenuCutObject);
			m_dicGests.Add(new KeyGesture(Key.A, ModifierKeys.Control), ContextMenuSelectAll);
		}

		private void ContextMenuSelectAll(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (iconEditor != null)
			{
				int command = 57642;
				iconEditor.InvokeMenuItem(command);
			}
		}

		private void btnSnap_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (iconEditor.GetSnap() == 1)
			{
				btnSnap.IsChecked = false;
				iconEditor.SetSnap(0);
			}
			else
			{
				btnSnap.IsChecked = true;
				iconEditor.SetSnap(1);
			}
		}

		private void btnGrid_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (iconEditor.GetGrid() == 1)
			{
				btnGrid.IsChecked = false;
				iconEditor.SetGrid(0);
			}
			else
			{
				btnGrid.IsChecked = true;
				iconEditor.SetGrid(1);
			}
		}

		private void cboGridScale_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			int selectedIndex = cboGridScale.SelectedIndex;
			if (iconEditor != null && selectedIndex >= 0)
			{
				iconEditor.SetGridSizeIndex((short)selectedIndex);
			}
		}

		private void UpdateTextAttributes()
		{
			int isize = 0;
			int istyle = 0;
			int icolor = 0;
			int iorientation = 0;
			string sfontname = "";
			iconEditor.GetTextAttribute(ref sfontname, ref isize, ref istyle, ref iorientation, ref icolor);
			msRFontName = sfontname;
			miRibFontSize = isize;
			miRibFontStyle = istyle;
			miRibOrientation = iorientation;
			miRibFontColor = icolor;
			short num = (short)(istyle & 2);
			short num2 = (short)(istyle & 1);
			short num3 = (short)(istyle & 4);
			if (num != 0)
			{
				btnBold.IsChecked = true;
			}
			else
			{
				btnBold.IsChecked = false;
			}
			if (num2 != 0)
			{
				btnItalic.IsChecked = true;
			}
			else
			{
				btnItalic.IsChecked = false;
			}
			if (num3 != 0)
			{
				btnUnderline.IsChecked = true;
			}
			else
			{
				btnUnderline.IsChecked = false;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			if (isize < iMinFontSizeR6)
			{
				isize = iMinFontSizeR6;
				flag = true;
			}
			if (isize > iMaxFontSizeR30)
			{
				isize = iMaxFontSizeR30;
				flag = true;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				miRibFontSize = isize;
				iconEditor.SetTextAttribute(sfontname, miRibFontSize, istyle, iorientation, icolor, 1);
			}
			cboFontSize.SelectedIndex = isize - iMinFontSizeR6;
			int num4 = IconFontNameList.IndexOf(sfontname);
			if (num4 >= 0)
			{
				cboFont.SelectedIndex = num4;
			}
			else
			{
				string selectedValue = (msRFontName = sDefFontName);
				cboFont.SelectedValue = selectedValue;
				iconEditor.SetTextAttribute(msRFontName, miRibFontSize, istyle, iorientation, icolor, 0);
			}
			cboLine.SelectedIndex = iconEditor.GetObjectLineColorIndex();
			cboFill.SelectedIndex = iconEditor.GetObjectFillColorIndex();
			cboLineType.SelectedIndex = iconEditor.GetObjectLineTypeIndex() - 1;
			short fillMode = iconEditor.GetFillMode();
			if (fillMode == 255 || fillMode == 0)
			{
				cboFill.IsEnabled = false;
				cboFill.SelectedIndex = 7;
			}
			else
			{
				cboFill.IsEnabled = true;
			}
			short rotateEnabled = iconEditor.GetRotateEnabled();
			if (iconEditor.HasSelectedObject() == 0 || rotateEnabled == 0)
			{
				btnRotate.IsEnabled = false;
				btnFlipHorzontaol.IsEnabled = false;
				btnFlipVertical.IsEnabled = false;
			}
			else
			{
				btnRotate.IsEnabled = true;
				btnFlipHorzontaol.IsEnabled = true;
				btnFlipVertical.IsEnabled = true;
			}
			short snap = iconEditor.GetSnap();
			btnSnap.IsChecked = snap == 1;
			short grid = iconEditor.GetGrid();
			btnGrid.IsChecked = grid == 1;
			cboGridScale.SelectedIndex = iconEditor.GetGridSizeIndex();
		}

		private void SetFont(int ifSel)
		{
			if (iconEditor != null && cboFont.SelectedIndex >= 0 && cboFontSize.SelectedIndex >= 0)
			{
				int num = 0;
				string text = IconFontNameList[cboFont.SelectedIndex];
				int num2 = cboFontSize.SelectedIndex + iMinFontSizeR6;
				if (btnBold.IsChecked == true)
				{
					num |= 2;
				}
				if (btnItalic.IsChecked == true)
				{
					num |= 1;
				}
				if (btnUnderline.IsChecked == true)
				{
					num |= 4;
				}
				msRFontName = text;
				miRibFontSize = num2;
				miRibFontStyle = num;
				iconEditor.SetTextAttribute(msRFontName, miRibFontSize, miRibFontStyle, miRibOrientation, miRibFontColor, ifSel);
			}
		}

		private void cboLine_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{

			{
				iconEditor.SetObjectLineColorIndex((short)cboLine.SelectedIndex);
			}
		}

		private void GetUndoRedoList()
		{
			if (iconEditor != null && !iconEditor.IsDisposed)
			{
				string empty = string.Empty;
				string empty2 = string.Empty;
				empty = iconEditor.GetUndoList();
				empty2 = iconEditor.GetRedoList();
				_undoItems = empty;
				_redoItems = empty2;
			}
		}

		private void OnModelLibraryUndoRedoExecuteEvent(ModelLibraryUndoRedoExecuteEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!(e.ActiveScreen is CustomModelsEditor customModelsEditor) || customModelsEditor.rightPanel.Content != this || (iconEditor != null && iconEditor.FocusInTextEdit() == 1))
			{
				return;
			}
			string stepType = e.StepType;
			if (!(stepType == "OneStep"))
			{
				if (stepType == "Steps")
				{
					if (e.ActionType == "Undo")
					{
						iconEditor.SetStepsUndo(e.CurIndex);
					}
					else
					{
						iconEditor.SetStepsRedo(e.CurIndex);
					}
					GetUndoRedoList();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (e.ActionType == "Undo")
				{
					iconEditor.SetOneStepUndo();
				}
				else
				{
					iconEditor.SetOneStepRedo();
				}
				GetUndoRedoList();
			}
		}

		private void OnRetrieveUndoRedoDataEvent(RetrieveUndoRedoDataEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.ActiveScreen is CustomModelsEditor customModelsEditor && customModelsEditor.rightPanel.Content == this)
			{
				GetUndoRedoList();
			}
		}

			}

				btnUnderline = (ToggleButton)target;
				btnUnderline.Checked += btnUnderline_Checked;
				btnUnderline.Unchecked += btnUnderline_Checked;
				break;
			case 25:
				btnRotate = (System.Windows.Controls.Button)target;
				btnRotate.Click += btnRotate_Click;
				break;
			case 26:
				btnFlipHorzontaol = (System.Windows.Controls.Button)target;
				btnFlipHorzontaol.Click += btnFlipHorzontaol_Click;
				break;
			case 27:
				btnFlipVertical = (System.Windows.Controls.Button)target;
				btnFlipVertical.Click += btnFlipVertical_Click;
				break;
			case 28:
				iconEditor = (AxIconEditorAx)target;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	public class CustomTypeInput : System.Windows.Controls.UserControl, INotifyPropertyChanged, IComponentConnector
	{
		private IUnityContainer _container;

		private readonly ACMModel _model;

		private readonly TextType _textType;

		private IEnumerable<ItemInfo> typeList;

		private ItemInfo selectedType;

		private string physicalQuantity;

		private string units;

		private string value;

		private IEnumerable<string> paramValueList;

		private string paramValue;

		private string lowerValue;

		private string upperValue;

		private string scale;

		private ObservableCollection<StringItem> stringParameterValidValues = new ObservableCollection<StringItem>();

		private StringItem stringParameterDefaultValue;

		private ICommand addValidValueCommand;

		private ICommand deleteValidValueCommand;

		private ICommand _showSearchTypeDialogCommand;

		private ICommand viewTypeDefinitionCommand;

		internal NumericTextBox txtValue;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox cboParamValue;

		internal NumericTextBox txtLower;

		internal NumericTextBox txtUpper;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public IEnumerable<ItemInfo> TypeList
		{
			get
			{
				return typeList;
			}
			set
			{
				typeList = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("TypeList");
			}
		}

		public ItemInfo SelectedType
		{
			get
			{
				return selectedType;
			}
			set
			{
				if (selectedType == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				selectedType = value;
				if (_model != null)
				{
					if (_textType == TextType.Variable)
					{
						_model.ACMDoc.GetTypedAssignments(TextTypeToAuTypeDescriptors(_textType), value.Name, out var _, out var num, out var sPhysicalQuantity, out var sUnits, out var lower, out var upper, out var _);
						Units = sUnits;
						PhysicalQuantity = sPhysicalQuantity;
						Value = num.ToString("G6");
						LowerValue = lower.ToString("G6");
						UpperValue = upper.ToString("G6");
					}
					else if (_textType == TextType.Parameter)
					{
						string sDataType;
						string sDescription2;
						string sUnits2;
						string sPhysicalQuantity2;
						string dvalue;
						string sUpper;
						string sLower;
						string[] array = _model.ACMDoc.GetParameterValues(value.Name, out sDataType, out sDescription2, out sUnits2, out sPhysicalQuantity2, out dvalue, out sUpper, out sLower);
						Units = sPhysicalQuantity2;
						PhysicalQuantity = sUnits2;
						ValueDataType = sDataType;
						Value = null;
						LowerValue = null;
						UpperValue = null;
						if (value.Name == "StringParameter")
						{
							array = new string[2] { "Value1", "Value2" };
							dvalue = array[0];
						}
						if (value.Name == "IntegerParameter")
						{
							Value = "1";
							LowerValue = "0";
							UpperValue = "10000";
						}
						else
						{
							if (IsRealOrIntegerValue)
							{
								Value = dvalue;
								LowerValue = sLower;
								UpperValue = sUpper;
							}
							if (array != null)
							{
								List<string> list = new List<string>();
								list.AddRange(array);
								ParamValueList = list;
								ParamValue = dvalue;
								StringParameterValidValues.Clear();
								string[] array2 = array;
								foreach (string text in array2)
								{
									StringParameterValidValues.Add(new StringItem(text));
								}
								StringParameterDefaultValue = StringParameterValidValues.FirstOrDefault((StringItem x) => x.StringValue == dvalue);
							}
						}
						OnPropertyChanged("ValueVisibility");
						OnPropertyChanged("ParamValueVisibility");
						OnPropertyChanged("IsRealOrIntegerValue");
						OnPropertyChanged("StringParameterGridVisibility");
						OnPropertyChanged("OtherTypeGridVisibility");
						OnPropertyChanged("UnitsVisibility");
						OnPropertyChanged("ValueMask");
					}
				}
				OnPropertyChanged("SelectedType");
			}
		}

		public string PhysicalQuantity
		{
			get
			{
				return physicalQuantity;
			}
			set

				OnPropertyChanged("PhysicalQuantity");

		public MaskOptions ValueMask
		{
			get
			{
				if (!(ValueDataType == "INT"))
				{
					return MaskOptions.Real;
				}
				return MaskOptions.Int;
			}
		}

		public Visibility ScaleVisibility => Visibility.Collapsed;

		public Visibility UnitsVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				if (_textType == TextType.Variable || (_textType == TextType.Parameter && ValueDataType == "REAL"))
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
				return Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
		}

		public Visibility ValueVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				if (!IsRealOrIntegerValue)
				{
					return Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				return Visibility.Visible;
			}
		}

		public Visibility ParamValueVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				if (_textType == TextType.Parameter && !IsRealOrIntegerValue)
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
				return Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
		}

		public bool IsRealOrIntegerValue
		{
			get
			{
				if (!(ValueDataType == "INT"))
				{
					return ValueDataType == "REAL";
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public Visibility StringParameterGridVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				if (SelectedType != null && ParameterType == ParameterTypes.String)
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
				return Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
		}

		public Visibility OtherTypeGridVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				if (StringParameterGridVisibility == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					return Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				return Visibility.Visible;
			}
		}

		public ICommand AddValidValueCommand
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_0021: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_002b: Expected O, but got Unknown
				if (addValidValueCommand == null)
				{
					addValidValueCommand = (ICommand)new RelayActionCommand((Action)AddValidValue, (Func<bool>)CanAddValidValue);
				}
				return addValidValueCommand;
			}
		}

		public ICommand DeleteValidValueCommand
		{
			get
			{
				if (deleteValidValueCommand == null)
				{
					deleteValidValueCommand = (ICommand)new RelayActionCommand<StringItem>((Action<StringItem>)DeleteValidValue);
				}
				return deleteValidValueCommand;
			}
		}

		public ICommand ShowSearchTypeDialogCommand
		{
			get
			{
				if (_showSearchTypeDialogCommand == null)
				{
					_showSearchTypeDialogCommand = (ICommand)new RelayActionCommand<object>((Action<object>)ShowSearchTypeDialogCommand_Executed);
				}
				return _showSearchTypeDialogCommand;
			}
		}

		public ICommand ViewTypeDefinitionCommand
		{
			get
			{
				if (viewTypeDefinitionCommand == null)
				{
					viewTypeDefinitionCommand = (ICommand)new RelayActionCommand<object>((Action<object>)ViewTypeDefinitionCommand_Executed, (Func<object, bool>)ViewTypeDefinitionCommand_CanExecute);
				}
				return viewTypeDefinitionCommand;
			}
		}

		public string InheritedType
		{
			get
			{
				if (_textType == TextType.Variable)
				{
					if (SelectedType != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(SelectedType.Name))
					{
						return SelectedType.Name;
					}
					return "RealVariable";
				}
				if (_textType == TextType.Parameter)
				{
					if (SelectedType != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(SelectedType.Name))
					{
						return SelectedType.Name;
					}
					return "IntegerParameter";
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		public ParameterTypes ParameterType
		{
			get
			{
				if (_textType == TextType.Parameter)
				{
					if (ValueDataType == "INT")
					{
						return ParameterTypes.Integer;
					}
					if (ValueDataType == "BOOL")
					{
						return ParameterTypes.Logical;
					}
					if (ValueDataType == "STRING")
					{
						return ParameterTypes.String;
					}
					if (ValueDataType == "REAL")
					{
						return ParameterTypes.Real;
					}
				}
				return ParameterTypes.Integer;
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		private bool CanAddValidValue()
		{
			return StringParameterValidValues.Count < 10;
		}

		private void AddValidValue()
		{
			string name = "Value1";
			int num = 1;
			while (!StringParameterValidValues.All((StringItem x) => !string.Equals(x.StringValue, name, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase)))
			{
				num++;
				name = $"Value{num}";
			}
			StringParameterValidValues.Add(new StringItem(name));
		}

		private void DeleteValidValue(StringItem value)
		{
			StringParameterValidValues.Remove(value);
		}

		private void ShowSearchTypeDialogCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			FindTypeDialog findTypeDialog = new FindTypeDialog(TypeList)
			{
				Owner = Window.GetWindow(this)
			};
			if (findTypeDialog.ShowDialog() == true)
			{
				SelectedType = findTypeDialog.SelectedType;
			}
		}

		private bool ViewTypeDefinitionCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (SelectedType != null)
			{
				return _textType != TextType.Set;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void ViewTypeDefinitionCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			//IL_006f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			try
			{
				string sTitle;
				string typedDefinition = _model.ACMDoc.GetTypedDefinition(TextTypeToAuTypeDescriptors(_textType), SelectedType.Name, out sTitle);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(typedDefinition))
				{
					Window window = Window.GetWindow(this);
					TypeDefinitionWindow typeDefinitionWindow = new TypeDefinitionWindow(sTitle, typedDefinition, _model, _container);
					typeDefinitionWindow.Owner = window;
					typeDefinitionWindow.Show();
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(ex.Message, ApwnResources.CustomModels, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		public string GetText(string name)
		{
			if (_textType == TextType.Variable)
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				stringBuilder.Append("Variable " + name);
				stringBuilder.Append("\n");
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Units) && Units != "-")
				{
					stringBuilder.Append("  /*" + Units + "*/\n");
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(LowerValue) || !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(UpperValue) || !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Value) || !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Scale))
				{
					stringBuilder.Append("  ");
					if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Value))
					{
						stringBuilder.Append("Value: " + Value + "; ");
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(LowerValue))
					{
						stringBuilder.Append("Lower: " + LowerValue + "; ");
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(UpperValue))
					{
						stringBuilder.Append("Upper: " + UpperValue + "; ");
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Scale))
					{
						stringBuilder.Append("Scale: " + Scale + "; ");
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(PhysicalQuantity) && PhysicalQuantity != "-")
					{
						stringBuilder.Append("PhysicalQuantity: \"" + PhysicalQuantity + "\"; ");
					}
					if (InheritedType == "RealVariable")
					{
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(PhysicalQuantity) || PhysicalQuantity == "-")
						{
							stringBuilder.Append("\n  // PhysicalQuantity : \" < physical quantity name > \";");
						}
						if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Scale))
						{
							stringBuilder.Append("\n  // Scale : <scaling value>;");
						}
					}
					stringBuilder.Append("\nEnd");
					return stringBuilder.ToString();
				}
				if (InheritedType != "RealVariable")
				{
					stringBuilder.Append("End");
					return stringBuilder.ToString();
				}
			}
			else if (_textType == TextType.Parameter)
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder2 = new StringBuilder();
				stringBuilder2.Append("Parameter " + name + " Uses " + SelectedType.Name);
				stringBuilder2.Append("\n");
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Units) && Units != "-")
				{
					stringBuilder2.Append("  /*" + Units + "*/\n");
				}
				if (StringParameterGridVisibility == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					stringBuilder2.Append("  ");
					string text = string.Join(",", StringParameterValidValues.Select((StringItem x) => "\"" + x.StringValue + "\""));
					if (InheritedType == "StringParameter")
					{
						stringBuilder2.Append("Valid as StringSet ([" + text + "]);  ");
					}
					else
					{
						stringBuilder2.Append("Valid: [" + text + "];  ");
					}
					if (StringParameterDefaultValue != null)
					{
						stringBuilder2.Append("Value: \"" + StringParameterDefaultValue.StringValue + "\";");
					}
					stringBuilder2.Append("\nEnd");
					return stringBuilder2.ToString();
				}
				if ((IsRealOrIntegerValue && (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(LowerValue) || !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(UpperValue) || !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Value))) || (!IsRealOrIntegerValue && ParamValue != null))
				{
					if (IsRealOrIntegerValue && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Value))
					{
						stringBuilder2.Append("  Value: " + Value + "; ");
					}
					else if (!IsRealOrIntegerValue && ParamValue != null)
					{
						if (ParameterType == ParameterTypes.String)
						{
							stringBuilder2.Append("  Value: \"" + ParamValue + "\"; ");
						}
						else
						{
							stringBuilder2.Append("  Value: " + ParamValue + "; ");
						}
					}
					if (IsRealOrIntegerValue && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(LowerValue))
					{
						stringBuilder2.Append("  Lower: " + LowerValue + "; ");
					}
					if (IsRealOrIntegerValue && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(UpperValue))
					{
						stringBuilder2.Append("  Upper: " + UpperValue + "; ");
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(PhysicalQuantity) && PhysicalQuantity != "-")
					{
						stringBuilder2.Append("PhysicalQuantity: \"" + PhysicalQuantity + "\"; ");
					}
					stringBuilder2.Append("\nEnd");
					return stringBuilder2.ToString();
				}
				if (InheritedType != "IntegerParameter" && InheritedType != "LogicalParameter" && InheritedType != "RealParameter" && InheritedType != "StringParameter")
				{
					stringBuilder2.Append("End");
					return stringBuilder2.ToString();
				}
			}
			return string.Empty;
		}

		public CustomTypeInput(IUnityContainer unityContainer, ACMModel model, TextType textType)
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			base.DataContext = this;
			_container = unityContainer;
			_model = model;
			_textType = textType;
			base.Loaded += CustomTypeInput_Loaded;
		}

		private void CustomTypeInput_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			int typeDescriptor = TextTypeToAuTypeDescriptors(_textType);
			List<ItemInfo> list = new List<ItemInfo>();
			string[] aMTypeList = _model.ACMDoc.GetAMTypeList(typeDescriptor);
			if (_textType == TextType.Variable)
			{
				list.Add(new ItemInfo("RealVariable", ""));
			}
			else if (_textType == TextType.Parameter)
			{
				list.Add(new ItemInfo("IntegerParameter", ""));
				list.Add(new ItemInfo("LogicalParameter", ""));
				list.Add(new ItemInfo("RealParameter", ""));
				list.Add(new ItemInfo("StringParameter", ""));
			}
			string[] array = aMTypeList;
			foreach (string text in array)
			{
				switch (text)
				{
				case "RealVariable":
				case "IntegerParameter":
				case "LogicalParameter":
				case "RealParameter":
				case "StringParameter":
					continue;
				}
				list.Add(new ItemInfo(text, ""));
			}
			TypeList = list;
			if (_textType == TextType.Variable)
			{
				SelectedType = TypeList.FirstOrDefault((ItemInfo x) => x.Name == "RealVariable");
			}
			else if (_textType == TextType.Parameter)
			{
				SelectedType = TypeList.FirstOrDefault((ItemInfo x) => x.Name == "StringParameter");
			}
		}

		private int TextTypeToAuTypeDescriptors(TextType type)
		{
			int result = -1;
			switch (type)
			{
			case TextType.Variable:
				result = 0;
				break;
			case TextType.Procedure:
				result = 1;
				break;
			case TextType.Port:
				result = 2;
				break;
			case TextType.Parameter:
				result = 3;
				break;
			case TextType.SubModel:
				result = 4;
				break;
			case TextType.Structure:
				result = 5;
				break;
			case TextType.Stream:
				result = 6;
				break;
			case TextType.Set:
				result = 7;
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected void OnPropertyChanged([CallerMemberName] string name = null)
		{
			this.PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(name));
		}

			case 3:

				break;

				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}

	public class RangeWrapper : DependencyObject
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty Range1Property = DependencyProperty.Register("Range1", typeof(string), typeof(RangeWrapper), new PropertyMetadata(null));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty Range2Property = DependencyProperty.Register("Range2", typeof(string), typeof(RangeWrapper), new PropertyMetadata(null));

		public string Range1
		{
			get

	public class ValueRangeRule : ValidationRule
	{
		public enum DataTypes
		{
			Lower,
			Value,
			Upper
		}

		public DataTypes DataType { get; set; }

		public RangeWrapper Wrapper { get; set; }

		public override ValidationResult Validate(object value, CultureInfo cultureInfo)
		{
			if (Wrapper != null && double.TryParse(value?.ToString(), out var result))
			{
				if (DataType == DataTypes.Lower)
				{
					if (double.TryParse(Wrapper.Range1, out var result2) && result2 < result)
					{
						return new ValidationResult(isValid: false, CustomModelsResources.LowerValidationError1);
					}
					if (double.TryParse(Wrapper.Range2, out var result3) && result3 < result)
					{
						return new ValidationResult(isValid: false, CustomModelsResources.LowerValidationError2);
					}
				}
				else if (DataType == DataTypes.Value)
				{
					if (double.TryParse(Wrapper.Range1, out var result4) && result4 > result)
					{
						return new ValidationResult(isValid: false, CustomModelsResources.ValueValidationError1);
					}
					if (double.TryParse(Wrapper.Range2, out var result5) && result5 < result)
					{
						return new ValidationResult(isValid: false, CustomModelsResources.ValueValidationError2);
					}
				}
				else if (DataType == DataTypes.Upper)
				{
					if (double.TryParse(Wrapper.Range1, out var result6) && result6 > result)
					{
						return new ValidationResult(isValid: false, CustomModelsResources.UpperValidationError1);
					}
					if (double.TryParse(Wrapper.Range2, out var result7) && result7 > result)
					{
						return new ValidationResult(isValid: false, CustomModelsResources.UpperValidationError2);
					}
				}
			}
			return ValidationResult.ValidResult;
		}
	}
	public class BindingProxy : Freezable
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty DataContextProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DataContext", typeof(object), typeof(BindingProxy), new PropertyMetadata(null));

		public object DataContext
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(DataContextProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DataContextProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected override Freezable CreateInstanceCore()
		{
			return new BindingProxy();
		}
	}

		public ItemInfo SelectedType
		{
			get
			{
				return _selectedType;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_selectedType == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				_selectedType = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("SelectedType");
				Value = null;
				Units = null;
				ParamValue = null;
				LowerValue = null;
				UpperValue = null;
				Description = null;
				Scale = null;
				SelectedSpec = null;
				if (_selectedType != null)
				{
					if (TextType == TextType.Parameter)
					{
						UpdateParameterValues(_selectedType.Name);
					}
					if (TextType == TextType.Variable)
					{
						SetDefaultAssignments(TextType, _selectedType.Name);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		internal ModelAssistant ParentDialog
		{
			get
			{
				if (_parentDialog == null)
				{
					DependencyObject dependencyObject = this;
					do
					{
						dependencyObject = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(dependencyObject);
					}
					while (!(dependencyObject is ModelAssistant) && dependencyObject != null);
					_parentDialog = dependencyObject as ModelAssistant;
				}
				return _parentDialog;
			}
		}

		public ICommand InsertCommand
		{
			get
			{
				if (_insertCommand == null)
				{
					_insertCommand = (ICommand)new RelayActionCommand<object>((Action<object>)InsertCommand_Executed, (Func<object, bool>)InsertCommand_CanExecute);
				}
				return _insertCommand;
			}
		}

		public ICommand CopyCommand
		{
			get
			{
				if (_copyCommand == null)
				{
					_copyCommand = (ICommand)new RelayActionCommand<object>((Action<object>)CopyCommand_Executed, (Func<object, bool>)CopyCommand_CanExecute);
				}
				return _copyCommand;
			}
		}

		public ICommand CloseCommand
		{
			get

		}

		}

		public Visibility ValueVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				if (TextType != TextType.Parameter || ValueDataType == "INT" || ValueDataType == "REAL")
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
				return Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
		}

		public bool IsRealOrIntegerValue
		{
			get
			{
				if (!(ValueDataType == "INT"))
				{
					return ValueDataType == "REAL";
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public Visibility MultiportGroupVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				if (TextType == TextType.Port)
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
				return Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
		}

		public Visibility ParamValueVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				if (TextType == TextType.Parameter && ValueDataType != "INT" && ValueDataType != "REAL")
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
				return Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
		}

		public Visibility NameVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				if (TextType == TextType.Procedure)
				{
					return Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				return Visibility.Visible;
			}
		}

		public Visibility EquateToParentVisbility
		{
			get
			{
				if (TextType == TextType.Structure || TextType == TextType.SubModel || TextType == TextType.Stream)
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
				return Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
		}

		public bool EquateToParentEnabled => Flag != "External";

		public string EquateToParentLabel
		{
			get
			{
				if (TextType == TextType.Structure)
				{
					return CustomModelsResources.ModelAssistant_EquateStructureVarToParent;
				}
				if (TextType == TextType.SubModel)
				{
					return CustomModelsResources.ModelAssistant_EquateSubModelVarToParent;
				}
				if (TextType == TextType.Stream)
				{
					return CustomModelsResources.ModelAssistant_EquateStreamVarToParent;
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		public bool IsSet => TextType == TextType.Set;

		public bool IsSubModel => TextType == TextType.SubModel;

		public Visibility TypeVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				if (TextType == TextType.Set)
				{
					return Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				return Visibility.Visible;
			}
		}

		public Visibility ArrayVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				if (TextType == TextType.Port || TextType == TextType.Procedure || TextType == TextType.Set)
				{
					return Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				return Visibility.Visible;
			}
		}

		public Visibility FlagsVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				if (TextType == TextType.Procedure || TextType == TextType.Set)
				{
					return Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				return Visibility.Visible;
			}
		}

		public Visibility ProcedureCategoryVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				if (TextType == TextType.Procedure)
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
				return Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
		}

		public Visibility AssinmengsVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				if (TextType == TextType.Variable || TextType == TextType.Parameter)
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
				return Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
		}

		public Visibility ScaleVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				if (TextType == TextType.Variable)
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
				return Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
		}

		{
			get
			{
				if (TextType == TextType.Procedure)
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
				return Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
		}

		public Visibility DelienContentsVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				if (TextType == TextType.Set)
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
				return Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
		}

		{
			get
			{
				return _equateSubModelVarToParent;

			{
				_equateSubModelVarToParent = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("EquateToParent");
			}
		}
