		public ICommand ShowSearchTypeDialogCommand
		{
			get
			{
				if (_showSearchTypeDialogCommand == null)
				{
					_showSearchTypeDialogCommand = (ICommand)new RelayActionCommand<object>((Action<object>)ShowSearchTypeDialogCommand_Executed);
				}
				return _showSearchTypeDialogCommand;
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public ModelAssistantInput()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			base.DataContext = this;
			base.Loaded += ModelAssistantInput_Loaded;
		}

		private void ModelAssistantInput_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (TextType == TextType.Set)
			{
				insertPanel.Margin = new Thickness(20.0, 7.0, 0.0, 3.0);
				SelectedType = new ItemInfo("IntegerSet", "");
			}
		}

		private bool InsertCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(ScriptText) && SelectedType != null)
			{
				return !_invalidScripts;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void InsertCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			string scriptText = ScriptText;
			string text = string.Empty;
			int length = scriptText.Length;
			int i;
			int num = (i = 0);
			int num2 = 0;
			for (; i < length; i++)
			{
				if (scriptText[i] == '\t')
				{
					text += scriptText.Substring(num, i - num);
					int num3 = 8 - num2 % 8;
					string text2 = string.Empty;
					for (int j = 0; j < num3; j++)
					{
						text2 += " ";
					}
					text += text2;
					num = i + 1;
					num2 += num3;
				}
				else
				{
					num2 = ((scriptText[i] != '\n') ? (num2 + 1) : 0);
				}
			}
			if (num < i)
			{
				text += scriptText.Substring(num, i - num);
			}
			if (ParentDialog != null)
			{
				ParentDialog.EditorCtrl.InsertText(text);
			}
		}

		private bool CopyCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(ScriptText) && SelectedType != null)
			{
				return !_invalidScripts;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void CopyCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			System.Windows.Clipboard.SetText(ScriptText);
		}

		private void CloseCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
		}

		private void ClearCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			VarName = string.Empty;
			SelectedArray = null;
			SelectedType = null;
			Flag = null;
			HasDescription = false;
			Description = null;
			HasValue = false;
			Value = null;
			HasLowerValue = false;
			LowerValue = null;
			HasUpperValue = false;
			UpperValue = null;
			HasSpec = false;
			SelectedSpec = null;
			HasScale = false;
			Scale = null;
		}

		private bool ViewTypeDefinitionCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (SelectedType != null)
			{
				return TextType != TextType.Set;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void ViewTypeDefinitionCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			//IL_0086: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			try
			{
				string sTitle;
				string typedDefinition = Model.ACMDoc.GetTypedDefinition(TextTypeToAuTypeDescriptors(TextType), SelectedType.Name, out sTitle);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(typedDefinition))
				{
					Window window = Window.GetWindow(this);
					CustomModelsEditor customModelsEditor = Utilities.GetAncestorFromType((DependencyObject)this, typeof(CustomModelsEditor), true) as CustomModelsEditor;
					TypeDefinitionWindow typeDefinitionWindow = new TypeDefinitionWindow(sTitle, typedDefinition, Model, ((ScreenContentControl)customModelsEditor).UnityContainer);
					typeDefinitionWindow.Owner = window;
					typeDefinitionWindow.Show();
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(ex.Message, ApwnResources.CustomModels, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		public VarNameError ValididateName(string name)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(name))
			{
				return VarNameError.EmptyString;
			}
			if (name.Length > 27)
			{
				return VarNameError.TooLong;
			}
			if (Regex.IsMatch(name, "[^\\u0000-\\u007F]"))
			{
				return VarNameError.InvalidCharacter;
			}
			if (!char.IsLetter(name[0]))
			{
				return VarNameError.InvalidStart;
			}
			foreach (char c in name)
			{
				if (!char.IsLetter(c) && !char.IsDigit(c) && c != '_')
				{
					return VarNameError.InvalidCharacter;
				}
			}
			if (Model.ACMDoc.IsKeyword(name) || name.Equals("IntegerParameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) || name.Equals("RealParameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) || name.Equals("StringParameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) || name.Equals("LogicalParameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				return VarNameError.IsKeyword;
			}
			return VarNameError.OK;
		}

		public static string GetErrorDescription(VarNameError err)
		{
			return err switch
			{
				VarNameError.InvalidStart => CustomModelsResources.VarNameError_InvalidStart, 
				VarNameError.TooLong => CustomModelsResources.VarNameError_TooLong, 
				VarNameError.EmptyString => CustomModelsResources.VarNameError_EmptyString, 
				VarNameError.InvalidCharacter => CustomModelsResources.VarNameError_InvalidCharacter, 
				VarNameError.IsKeyword => CustomModelsResources.VarNameError_IsKeyword, 
				_ => string.Empty, 
			};
		}

		private static void OnTextTypePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as ModelAssistantInput).OnTextTypeChanged((TextType)e.OldValue, (TextType)e.NewValue);
		}

		private void OnTextTypeChanged(TextType oldValue, TextType newValue)
		{
			SetControlVisibilities(newValue);
			UpdateTypeList(newValue);
			UpdateFlagsList(newValue);
		}

		private static void OnModelPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as ModelAssistantInput).OnModelChanged((ACMModel)e.OldValue, (ACMModel)e.NewValue);
		}

		private void OnModelChanged(ACMModel oldValue, ACMModel newValue)
		{
			UpdateTypeList(TextType);
		}

		private void SetControlVisibilities(TextType type)
		{
			OnPropertyChanged("NameVisibility");
			OnPropertyChanged("ArrayVisibility");
			OnPropertyChanged("FlagsVisibility");
			OnPropertyChanged("AssinmengsVisibility");
			OnPropertyChanged("ValueVisibility");
			OnPropertyChanged("IsRealOrIntegerValue");
			OnPropertyChanged("ParamValueVisibility");
			OnPropertyChanged("MultiportGroupVisibility");
			OnPropertyChanged("TearProcedureVisibility");
			OnPropertyChanged("DelienContentsVisibility");
		}

		private void UpdateProcedureScripts()
		{
			if (SelectedType == null)
			{
				ScriptText = null;
			}
			else
			{
				ScriptText = Model.ACMDoc.GetProcedureDefinition(SelectedType.Name, IsTearProcedure);
			}
		}

		private void UpdateSetScripts()
		{
			if (SelectedType == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(SelectedType.Name))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (IsDeline)
			{
				if (SelectedType.Name == "IntegerSet")
				{
					ScriptText = $"{VarName} as {SelectedType}([1:<n>]);\r\n";
				}
				else
				{
					ScriptText = $"{VarName} as {SelectedType}([\"<...>\",\"<...>\"]);\r\n";
				}
			}
			else
			{
				ScriptText = $"{VarName} as {SelectedType};\r\n";
			}
		}

		private void UpdateScripts()
		{
			_invalidScripts = false;
			if (TextType == TextType.Procedure)
			{
				UpdateProcedureScripts();
				return;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(VarName) || SelectedType == null)
			{
				ScriptText = string.Empty;
				return;
			}
			string errorDescription = GetErrorDescription(ValididateName(VarName));
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(errorDescription))
			{
				ScriptText = errorDescription;
				_invalidScripts = true;
				return;
			}
			if (TextType == TextType.Set)
			{
				UpdateSetScripts();
				return;
			}
			if (SelectedType != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(SelectedType.Name) && !Model.ACMDoc.ValidateVariableEntries(SelectedType.Name, Value.HasValue, Value.HasValue ? Value.Value : 0.0, LowerValue.HasValue, LowerValue.HasValue ? LowerValue.Value : 0.0, UpperValue.HasValue, UpperValue.HasValue ? UpperValue.Value : 0.0, Scale.HasValue, Scale.HasValue ? Scale.Value : 0.0, out var sError))
			{
				ScriptText = sError;
				_invalidScripts = true;
				return;
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append(VarName);
			stringBuilder.Append(SelectedArray);
			stringBuilder.Append(" as ");
			stringBuilder.AppendFormat("{0} ", Flag);
			if (TextType == TextType.Port && HasMultiport)
			{
				stringBuilder.Append("MULTIPORT OF " + CapitalizeFirstLetter(SelectedType.Name));
			}
			else
			{
				stringBuilder.Append(CapitalizeFirstLetter(SelectedType.Name));
			}
			if (Flag != "External" && Flag != "Global" && TextType != TextType.Port)
			{
				grpAssignments.IsEnabled = true;
				string text = string.Empty;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Description))
				{
					text += $"Description:\"{Description}\"";
				}
				if (UpperValue.HasValue)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						text += ", ";
					}
					text += $"Upper:{UpperValue.Value.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)}";
				}
				if (LowerValue.HasValue)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						text += ", ";
					}
					text += $"Lower:{LowerValue.Value.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)}";
				}
				if ((ValueDataType == "REAL" || ValueDataType == "INT") && Value.HasValue)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						text += ", ";
					}
					text += $"Value:{Value.Value.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)}";
				}
				else if (ValueDataType == "BOOL" && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(ParamValue))
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						text += ", ";
					}
					text += $"Value:{ParamValue}";
				}
				else if (ValueDataType == "STRING" && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(ParamValue))
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						text += ", ";
					}
					text += $"Value:\"{ParamValue}\"";
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(SelectedSpec))
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						text += ", ";
					}
					text += $"Spec:{SelectedSpec}";
				}
				if (Scale.HasValue)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						text += ", ";
					}
					text += $"Scale:{Scale.Value.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)}";
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					stringBuilder.AppendFormat(" ({0})", text);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				grpAssignments.IsEnabled = false;
			}
			if (TextType == TextType.Port)
			{
				string text2 = string.Empty;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Description))
				{
					text2 += $"Description:\"{Description}\"";
				}
				if (HasMultiport)
				{
					if (MinConnections.HasValue)
					{
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
						{
							text2 += ", ";
						}
						text2 += $"MIN_CONNECTIONS:{MinConnections.Value.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)}";
					}
					if (MaxConnections.HasValue)
					{
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
						{
							text2 += ", ";
						}
						text2 += $"MAX_CONNECTIONS:{MaxConnections.Value.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)}";
					}
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					stringBuilder.AppendFormat("({0})", text2);
				}
			}
			if (EquateToParentEnabled && EquateToParent)
			{
				if (TextType == TextType.SubModel)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append("(<SubModelVariable = ModelVariable>, <...> , <SubModelParameter: ModelParameter>, <...> )");
				}
				else if (TextType == TextType.Structure)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append("(<StructureVariable = ModelVariable>, <...> , <StructureVariable: ModelParameter>, <...> )");
				}
				else if (TextType == TextType.Stream)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append("(<StreamVariable = ModelVariable>, <...> , <StreamVariable: ModelParameter>, <...> )");
				}
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";\r\n");
			ScriptText = stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private void UpdateFlagsList(TextType type)
		{
			switch (type)
			{
			case TextType.Variable:
				Flags = new List<string> { "", "External", "Global", "Hidden", "Input", "Input, Output", "Output" };
				break;
			case TextType.Parameter:
				Flags = new List<string> { "", "External", "Global", "Hidden" };
				break;
			case TextType.Port:
				Flags = new List<string> { "", "Input", "Input, Output", "Output" };
				break;
			case TextType.Structure:
				Flags = new List<string> { "", "External", "Hidden" };
				break;
			case TextType.SubModel:
			case TextType.Stream:
				Flags = new List<string> { "", "Hidden" };
				break;
			case TextType.Procedure:
			case TextType.Set:
				break;
			}
		}

		public static string CapitalizeFirstLetter(string s)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(s))
			{
				return s;
			}
			return char.ToUpperInvariant(s[0]) + s.Substring(1);
		}

		private int TextTypeToAuTypeDescriptors(TextType type)
		{
			int result = -1;
			switch (type)
			{
			case TextType.Variable:
				result = 0;
				break;
			case TextType.Procedure:
				result = 1;
				break;
			case TextType.Port:
				result = 2;
				break;
			case TextType.Parameter:
				result = 3;
				break;
			case TextType.SubModel:
				result = 4;
				break;
			case TextType.Structure:
				result = 5;
				break;
			case TextType.Stream:
				result = 6;
				break;
			case TextType.Set:
				result = 7;
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal void UpdateTypeList(TextType type)
		{
			if (Model == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = TextTypeToAuTypeDescriptors(type);
			IEnumerable<string> enumerable;
			if (num == 7)
			{
				enumerable = new string[2] { "IntegerSet", "StringSet" };
			}
			else
			{
				enumerable = Model.ACMDoc.GetAMTypeList(num);
				switch (type)
				{
				case TextType.Procedure:
					_allProcedures = enumerable;
					GenerateProcedureDescriptionsDict(enumerable);
					enumerable = GetProceduresInSelectedCategory();
					break;
				case TextType.SubModel:
					_allSubModels = enumerable;
					GenerateSubModelDescriptionsDict(enumerable);
					enumerable = GetSubModelsInSelectedCategory();
					break;
				case TextType.Structure:
					GenerateStructureDescriptionsDict(enumerable);
					break;
				}
			}
			if (enumerable == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			List<ItemInfo> list = new List<ItemInfo>();
			foreach (string item in enumerable)
			{
				if (type == TextType.Procedure && ProcedureDescriptionDict.ContainsKey(item))
				{
					list.Add(new ItemInfo(item, ProcedureDescriptionDict[item]));
				}
				else if (type == TextType.SubModel && SubModelDescriptionDict.ContainsKey(item))
				{
					list.Add(new ItemInfo(item, SubModelDescriptionDict[item]));
				}
				else if (type == TextType.Structure && StructureDescriptionDict.ContainsKey(item))
				{
					list.Add(new ItemInfo(item, StructureDescriptionDict[item]));
				}
				else
				{
					list.Add(new ItemInfo(item, ""));
				}
			}
			TypeList = list;
		}

		private void GenerateStructureDescriptionsDict(IEnumerable<string> types)
		{
			if (StructureDescriptionDict != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			StructureDescriptionDict = new Dictionary<string, string>();
			int typeDescriptor = TextTypeToAuTypeDescriptors(TextType.Structure);
			foreach (string type in types)
			{
				string text = "";
				string value = "";
				try
				{
					text = Model.ACMDoc.GetTypedDefinition(typeDescriptor, type, out var _);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
				string[] array = text.Split('\n');
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					string text2 = array[i].Trim();
					if (text2.StartsWith("Description:"))
					{
						value = text2.Substring("Description:".Length + 1);
						value = value.Substring(0, value.Length - 2);
						value = value.Trim(' ', '"');
						break;
					}
				}
				StructureDescriptionDict[type] = value;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateProcedureList()
		{
			IEnumerable<string> proceduresInSelectedCategory = GetProceduresInSelectedCategory();
			if (proceduresInSelectedCategory == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			List<ItemInfo> list = new List<ItemInfo>();
			foreach (string item in proceduresInSelectedCategory)
			{
				if (ProcedureDescriptionDict.ContainsKey(item))
				{
					list.Add(new ItemInfo(item, ProcedureDescriptionDict[item]));
				}
				else
				{
					list.Add(new ItemInfo(item, ""));
				}
			}
			TypeList = list;
		}

		private void SetDefaultAssignments(TextType textType, string typeName)
		{
			if (Model != null)
			{
				Model.ACMDoc.GetTypedAssignments(TextTypeToAuTypeDescriptors(textType), typeName, out var _, out var _, out var _, out var sUnits, out var _, out var _, out var _);
				Units = sUnits;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateParameterValues(string typeName)
		{
			string sDataType;
			string sDescription;
			string sUnits;
			string sPhysicalQuantity;
			string sValue;
			string sUpper;
			string sLower;
			string[] parameterValues = Model.ACMDoc.GetParameterValues(typeName, out sDataType, out sDescription, out sUnits, out sPhysicalQuantity, out sValue, out sUpper, out sLower);
			Units = sPhysicalQuantity;
			ValueDataType = sDataType;
			if (parameterValues != null)
			{
				List<string> list = new List<string> { "" };
				list.AddRange(parameterValues);
				ParamValueList = list;
			}
			OnPropertyChanged("ValueVisibility");
			OnPropertyChanged("ParamValueVisibility");
			OnPropertyChanged("IsRealOrIntegerValue");
		}

		protected void OnPropertyChanged(string propName)
		{
			PropertyChangedEventHandler propertyChangedEventHandler = this.PropertyChanged;
			if (propertyChangedEventHandler != null)
			{
				propertyChangedEventHandler(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
				if (propName != "ScriptText" && !propName.EndsWith("Visibility"))
				{
					UpdateScripts();
				}
			}
		}

		private void SelectedArrayChanged(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!HasArray)
			{
				SelectedArray = "";
			}
			else if (rdArray1.IsChecked == true)
			{
				SelectedArray = Arrays[0];
			}
			else if (rdArray2.IsChecked == true)
			{
				SelectedArray = Arrays[1];
			}
			else if (rdArray3.IsChecked == true)
			{
				SelectedArray = Arrays[2];
			}
			else if (rdArray4.IsChecked == true)
			{
				SelectedArray = Arrays[3];
			}
		}

		private IEnumerable<string> GetProceduresInSelectedCategory()
		{
			ProcedureCategory selectedCategory = GetSelectedCategory();
			if (ProcedureCategoryDict.ContainsKey(selectedCategory))
			{
				return ProcedureCategoryDict[selectedCategory];
			}
			return _allProcedures;
		}

		private ProcedureCategory GetSelectedCategory()
		{
			if (procedureCategoryAll.IsChecked == true)
			{
				return ProcedureCategory.All;
			}
			if (procedureCategoryMixProp.IsChecked == true)
			{
				return ProcedureCategory.MixtureProperties;
			}
			if (procedureCategoryFlash.IsChecked == true)
			{
				return ProcedureCategory.Flash;
			}
			if (procedureCategoryMisc.IsChecked == true)
			{
				return ProcedureCategory.Utilities;
			}
			if (procedureCategoryPolymers.IsChecked == true)
			{
				return ProcedureCategory.Polymers;
			}
			return ProcedureCategory.All;
		}

		private void GenerateProcedureDescriptionsDict(IEnumerable<string> procedureTypes)
		{
			if (ProcedureDescriptionDict != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			ProcedureDescriptionDict = new Dictionary<string, string>();
			int typeDescriptor = TextTypeToAuTypeDescriptors(TextType.Procedure);
			foreach (string procedureType in procedureTypes)
			{
				string sTitle;
				string[] array = Model.ACMDoc.GetTypedDefinition(typeDescriptor, procedureType, out sTitle).Split('\n');
				string text = "";
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					string text2 = array[i].Trim();
					if (text2.StartsWith("Description:"))
					{
						text = text2.Substring("Description:".Length + 1);
						text = text.Substring(0, text.Length - 2);
						text = text.Trim(' ', '"');
						break;
					}
					int num = text2.IndexOf("//");
					if (num >= 0)
					{
						if (num <= 0 || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text2.Substring(0, num)))
						{
							text = text2.Substring(text2.IndexOf("//") + 2);
							break;
						}
						continue;
					}
					int num2 = text2.IndexOf("/*");
					if (num2 >= 0)
					{
						string text3 = text2.Substring(num2 + 2);
						if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text3))
						{
							text = text3;
							break;
						}
						if (i < array.Length - 1)
						{
							text = array[i + 1];
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				if (text != null && text.EndsWith("*/"))
				{
					text = text.Substring(0, text.Length - 2);
				}
				ProcedureDescriptionDict[procedureType] = text;
			}
		}

		private void SubModelCategoryChanged(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			IEnumerable<string> subModelsInSelectedCategory = GetSubModelsInSelectedCategory();
			if (subModelsInSelectedCategory == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			List<ItemInfo> list = new List<ItemInfo>();
			foreach (string item in subModelsInSelectedCategory)
			{
				if (SubModelDescriptionDict.ContainsKey(item))
				{
					list.Add(new ItemInfo(item, SubModelDescriptionDict[item]));
				}
				else
				{
					list.Add(new ItemInfo(item, ""));
				}
			}
			TypeList = list;
		}

		private IEnumerable<string> GetSubModelsInSelectedCategory()
		{
			if (submodelCategoryAll.IsChecked == true)
			{
				return _allSubModels;
			}
			return PrivateSubModelDescriptions.Keys;
		}

		private void GenerateSubModelDescriptionsDict(IEnumerable<string> submodelTypes)
		{
			if (SubModelDescriptionDict != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			SubModelDescriptionDict = new Dictionary<string, string>();
			int typeDescriptor = TextTypeToAuTypeDescriptors(TextType.SubModel);
			foreach (string submodelType in submodelTypes)
			{
				string text = "";
				string text2 = "";
				try
				{
					text = Model.ACMDoc.GetTypedDefinition(typeDescriptor, submodelType, out var _);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
				if (PrivateSubModelDescriptions.ContainsKey(submodelType))
				{
					text2 = PrivateSubModelDescriptions[submodelType];
				}
				else
				{
					string[] array = text.Split('\n');
					for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
					{
						string text3 = array[i].Trim();
						if (text3.StartsWith("Description:"))
						{
							text2 = text3.Substring("Description:".Length + 1);
							text2 = text2.Substring(0, text2.Length - 2);
							text2 = text2.Trim(' ', '"');
							break;
						}
						int num = text3.IndexOf("//");
						if (num >= 0)
						{
							if (num <= 0 || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text3.Substring(0, num)))
							{
								text2 = text3.Substring(num + 2);
								break;
							}
							continue;
						}
						int num2 = text3.IndexOf("/*");
						if (num2 < 0)
						{
							continue;
						}
						string text4 = text3.Substring(num2 + 2);
						if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text4))
						{
							text2 = text4;
							break;
						}
						if (i < array.Length - 1)
						{
							text2 = array[i + 1];
							if (!text2.Contains("Aspen Custom Modeler (TM)"))
							{
								break;
							}
							i += 5;
						}
					}
				}
				if (text2 != null && text2.EndsWith("*/"))
				{
					text2 = text2.Substring(0, text2.Length - 2);
				}
				SubModelDescriptionDict[submodelType] = text2;
			}
		}

		private void ProcedureCategoryChanged(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateProcedureList();
		}

		private void SetTypeChanged(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (TextType == TextType.Set)
			{
				if (rdIntegerSet.IsChecked == true)
				{
					SelectedType = new ItemInfo("IntegerSet", "");
				}
				else
				{
					SelectedType = new ItemInfo("StringSet", "");
				}
			}
		}

		private void ShowSearchTypeDialogCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			FindTypeDialog findTypeDialog = new FindTypeDialog(TypeList)
			{
				Owner = Window.GetWindow(this)
			};
			if (findTypeDialog.ShowDialog() == true)
			{
				SelectedType = findTypeDialog.SelectedType;
			}
		}

	[TemplatePart(Name = "PART_ScrollViewer", Type = typeof(ScrollViewer))]
	public class ModelSlideBox : ContentControl
	{
		private ScrollViewer _scrollViewer;

		private const string PART_ScrollViewer = "PART_ScrollViewer";

		public static readonly DependencyProperty OrientationProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty StepSizeProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ContentOffsetProperty;

		public static readonly RoutedUICommand MoveLeftCommand;

		public static readonly RoutedUICommand MoveRightCommand;

		public System.Windows.Controls.Orientation Orientation
		{
			get
			{
				return (System.Windows.Controls.Orientation)GetValue(OrientationProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(OrientationProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public double StepSize
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(StepSizeProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(StepSizeProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public double ContentOffset
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(ContentOffsetProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ContentOffsetProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public virtual bool CanMoveLeft
		{
			get
			{
				if (_scrollViewer != null)
				{
					if (Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal)
					{
						return _scrollViewer.HorizontalOffset > 0.0;
					}
					return _scrollViewer.VerticalOffset > 0.0;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public virtual bool CanMoveRight
		{
			get
			{
				if (_scrollViewer != null)
				{
					if (Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal)
					{
						return _scrollViewer.ScrollableWidth > _scrollViewer.HorizontalOffset;
					}
					return _scrollViewer.ScrollableHeight > _scrollViewer.VerticalOffset;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		static ModelSlideBox()
		{
			OrientationProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Orientation", typeof(System.Windows.Controls.Orientation), typeof(ModelSlideBox), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal));
			StepSizeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("StepSize", typeof(double), typeof(ModelSlideBox), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(74.0));
			ContentOffsetProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ContentOffset", typeof(double), typeof(ModelSlideBox), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0.0, OnContentOffsetPropertyChanged));
			MoveLeftCommand = new RoutedUICommand("Move Left", "MoveLeft", typeof(ModelSlideBox));
			MoveRightCommand = new RoutedUICommand("Move Right", "MoveRight", typeof(ModelSlideBox));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(ModelSlideBox), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(ModelSlideBox)));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(ModelSlideBox), new CommandBinding(MoveLeftCommand, MoveLeftCommand_Executed, MoveLeftCommand_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(ModelSlideBox), new CommandBinding(MoveRightCommand, MoveRightCommand_Executed, MoveRightCommand_CanExecute));
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			_scrollViewer = GetTemplateChild("PART_ScrollViewer") as ScrollViewer;
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
		}

		protected virtual void OnContentOffsetChanged(double oldValue, double newValue)
		{
			if (_scrollViewer != null)
			{
				if (Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal)
				{
					_scrollViewer.ScrollToHorizontalOffset(newValue);
				}
				else
				{
					_scrollViewer.ScrollToVerticalOffset(newValue);
				}
				UpdateLayout();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnMovedLeft()
		{
			if (_scrollViewer != null)
			{
				if (Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal)
				{
					ChangeOffsetSlightly(_scrollViewer.HorizontalOffset, _scrollViewer.HorizontalOffset - StepSize);
				}
				else
				{
					ChangeOffsetSlightly(_scrollViewer.VerticalOffset, _scrollViewer.VerticalOffset - StepSize);
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnMovedRight()
		{
			if (_scrollViewer != null)
			{
				if (Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal)
				{
					ChangeOffsetSlightly(_scrollViewer.HorizontalOffset, _scrollViewer.HorizontalOffset + StepSize);
				}
				else
				{
					ChangeOffsetSlightly(_scrollViewer.VerticalOffset, _scrollViewer.VerticalOffset + StepSize);
				}
			}
		}

		private void ChangeOffsetSlightly(double startOffset, double endOffset)
		{
			DoubleAnimation doubleAnimation = new DoubleAnimation(startOffset, endOffset, new Duration(new TimeSpan(0, 0, 0, 0, 100)), FillBehavior.HoldEnd);
			Storyboard.SetTarget(doubleAnimation, this);
			Storyboard.SetTargetProperty(doubleAnimation, new PropertyPath(ContentOffsetProperty));
			Storyboard storyboard = new Storyboard();
			storyboard.Children.Add(doubleAnimation);
			storyboard.Begin(this);
			if (_scrollViewer != null)
			{
				if (Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal)
				{
					_scrollViewer.ScrollToHorizontalOffset(endOffset);
				}
				else
				{
					_scrollViewer.ScrollToVerticalOffset(endOffset);
				}
				UpdateLayout();
			}
		}

		private static void MoveLeftCommand_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			(sender as ModelSlideBox).OnMovedLeft();
		}

		private static void MoveLeftCommand_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			ModelSlideBox modelSlideBox = sender as ModelSlideBox;
			e.CanExecute = modelSlideBox.CanMoveLeft;
		}

		private static void MoveRightCommand_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			(sender as ModelSlideBox).OnMovedRight();
		}

		private static void MoveRightCommand_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			ModelSlideBox modelSlideBox = sender as ModelSlideBox;
			e.CanExecute = modelSlideBox.CanMoveRight;
		}

		private static void OnContentOffsetPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as ModelSlideBox).OnContentOffsetChanged((double)e.OldValue, (double)e.NewValue);
		}
	}
	public class NumericTextBox : System.Windows.Controls.TextBox
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty MaskProperty;

		public MaskOptions Mask
		{
			get
			{
				return (MaskOptions)GetValue(MaskProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(MaskProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static NumericTextBox()
		{
			MaskProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Mask", typeof(MaskOptions), typeof(NumericTextBox), new PropertyMetadata(MaskOptions.Real));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(NumericTextBox), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(NumericTextBox)));
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewTextInput(TextCompositionEventArgs e)
		{
			_ = base.Text.Length;
			string text = base.Text;
			if (e.Text.Length > 0)
			{
				char c = e.Text[0];
				if (Mask == MaskOptions.Real)
				{
					char c2 = MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol()[0];
					if ((c < '0' || c > '9') && c != c2 && c != '.' && c != '\b' && c != '-' && c != '+' && c != 'd' && c != 'D' && c != 'e' && c != 'E')
					{
						e.Handled = true;
					}
					if ((c == '.' && text.Contains('.')) || (c == c2 && text.Contains(c2)) || (c == 'e' && (text.Contains('e') || text.Contains('E') || text.Contains('d') || text.Contains('D'))) || (c == 'd' && (text.Contains('e') || text.Contains('E') || text.Contains('d') || text.Contains('D'))) || (c == 'E' && (text.Contains('e') || text.Contains('E') || text.Contains('d') || text.Contains('D'))) || (c == 'D' && (text.Contains('e') || text.Contains('E') || text.Contains('d') || text.Contains('D'))))
					{
						e.Handled = true;
					}
				}
				else if (Mask == MaskOptions.Int)
				{
					if ((c < '0' || c > '9') && c != '-')
					{
						e.Handled = true;
					}
					if (c == '-' && text.Contains('-'))
					{
						e.Handled = true;
					}
				}
			}
			base.OnPreviewTextInput(e);
		}
	}
	public class NumericTextBoxTextConverter : IMultiValueConverter
	{
		private NumericTextBox control;

		public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (values.Length != 2 || !(values[0] is string) || !(values[1] is NumericTextBox) || values[0] == null)
			{
				return values[0];
			}
			control = values[1] as NumericTextBox;
			return values[0].ToString().Replace(".", MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol());
		}

		public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			object[] array = new object[2] { null, null };
			if (control != null && value != null)
			{
				string text = value.ToString().Replace(MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol(), ".");
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					text = null;
				}
				array[0] = text;
			}
			else
			{
				array[0] = value;
			}
			return array;
		}
	}
	public enum MaskOptions
	{
		Int,
		Real
	}

		private bool AddCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(newItemTextBox.Text.Trim());
		}

		private void AddCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			string name = newItemTextBox.Text.Trim();
			Items.Add(new ProfileItem
			{
				Name = name,
				Variables = new ObservableCollection<VariableItem>()
			});
			newItemTextBox.Clear();
			SelectedIndex = Items.Count - 1;
		}

		private bool DeleteVariableCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			return true;
		}

		private void DeleteVariableCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			string profileName = obj as string;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(profileName))
			{
				ProfileItem profileItem = Items.FirstOrDefault((ProfileItem item) => item.Name.Equals(profileName, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase));
				if (profileItem != null)
				{
					Items.Remove(profileItem);
				}
			}
		}

		private bool MoveUpCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			return profileistBox.SelectedIndex > 0;
		}

		private void MoveUpCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			int selectedIndex = profileistBox.SelectedIndex;
			Items.Move(selectedIndex, selectedIndex - 1);
		}

		private bool MoveDownCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (profileistBox.SelectedIndex >= 0)
			{
				return profileistBox.SelectedIndex < profileistBox.Items.Count - 1;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void MoveDownCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			int selectedIndex = profileistBox.SelectedIndex;
			Items.Move(selectedIndex, selectedIndex + 1);
		}

		private void VariableBox_SelectedEvent(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			VariableBox variableBox = sender as VariableBox;
			string profileName = variableBox.Variable;
			ProfileItem profileItem = Items.FirstOrDefault((ProfileItem item) => item.Name.Equals(profileName, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase));
			if (profileItem != null)
			{
				int num = Items.IndexOf(profileItem);
				if (num >= 0)
				{
					profileistBox.SelectedIndex = num;
				}
			}
		}

		private static void OnSelectedIndexPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is ProfileListControl profileListControl)
			{
				profileListControl.OnSelectedIndexChanged((int)e.OldValue, (int)e.NewValue);
			}
		}

		private void OnSelectedIndexChanged(int oldValue, int newValue)
		{
			RaiseEvent(new RoutedEventArgs(SelectedIndexChangedEvent, this));
		}

		private static void OnItemsPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is ProfileListControl profileListControl)
			{
				profileListControl.OnItemsChanged((ObservableCollection<ProfileItem>)e.OldValue, (ObservableCollection<ProfileItem>)e.NewValue);
			}
		}

		private void OnItemsChanged(ObservableCollection<ProfileItem> oldValue, ObservableCollection<ProfileItem> newValue)
		{
			if (newValue != null && newValue.Count > 0)
			{
				profileistBox.SelectedIndex = 0;
			}
		}

		private void OnProfileGroupChanaged(ProfileGroup oldValue, ProfileGroup newValue)
		{
			if (newValue != null && newValue.Profiles.Count > 0 && profileListControl.SelectedIndex >= 0)
			{
				variableListControl.IsEnabled = true;
				variableListControl.Items = newValue.Profiles[profileListControl.SelectedIndex].Variables;
			}
			else
			{
				variableListControl.IsEnabled = false;
				variableListControl.Items = new ObservableCollection<VariableItem>();
			}
		}

		private void ProfileListControl_SelectedIndexChanged(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (profileListControl.SelectedIndex >= 0)
			{
				variableListControl.IsEnabled = true;
				variableListControl.Items = ProfileGroup.Profiles[profileListControl.SelectedIndex].Variables;
			}
			else
			{
				variableListControl.IsEnabled = false;
			}
		}

		private bool AddUnitsCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (ProfileGroup != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(ProfileGroup.Name))
			{
				return !ProfileGroup.Name.EndsWith("$UOM", StringComparison.InvariantCulture);
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void AddUnitsCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			ProfileGroup.Name = ProfileGroup.Name.Trim() + "$UOM";
		}

	public class SearchableComboBox : System.Windows.Controls.Control
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty ItemsSourceProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty SelectedItemProperty;

		public IEnumerable<ItemInfo> ItemsSource
		{
			get
			{
				return (IEnumerable<ItemInfo>)GetValue(ItemsSourceProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ItemsSourceProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public ItemInfo SelectedItem
		{
			get
			{
				return (ItemInfo)GetValue(SelectedItemProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(SelectedItemProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static SearchableComboBox()
		{
			ItemsSourceProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemsSource", typeof(IEnumerable<ItemInfo>), typeof(SearchableComboBox), new UIPropertyMetadata(null, OnItemsSourcePropretyChanged));
			SelectedItemProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SelectedItem", typeof(ItemInfo), typeof(SearchableComboBox));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(SearchableComboBox), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(SearchableComboBox)));
		}

		private static void OnItemsSourcePropretyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is SearchableComboBox searchableComboBox)
			{
				searchableComboBox.OnItemsSourceChanged((IEnumerable<ItemInfo>)e.OldValue, (IEnumerable<ItemInfo>)e.NewValue);
			}
		}

		private void OnItemsSourceChanged(IEnumerable<ItemInfo> oldValue, IEnumerable<ItemInfo> newValue)
		{
			if (GetTemplateChild("PART_ComboBox") is System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox comboBox)
			{
				comboBox.ItemsSource = newValue;
				if (SelectedItem != null && !newValue.Contains(SelectedItem))
				{
					SelectedItem = null;
					comboBox.Text = "";
				}
				else
				{
					comboBox.Text = SelectedItem?.Name;
				}
			}
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			if (GetTemplateChild("PART_ComboBox") is System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox comboBox)
			{
				comboBox.SelectionChanged += Cbo_SelectionChanged;
				comboBox.LostFocus += Cbo_LostFocus;
				comboBox.ItemsSource = ItemsSource;
				comboBox.KeyDown += Cbo_KeyDown;
				comboBox.AddHandler(System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.TextBoxBase.TextChangedEvent, new TextChangedEventHandler(TextBox_TextChanged));
			}
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
		}

		private void Cbo_KeyDown(object sender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.Return && sender is System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox cbo)
			{
				AfterLostFocus(cbo);
			}
		}

		private void Cbo_LostFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox cbo)
			{
				AfterLostFocus(cbo);
			}
		}

		private void AfterLostFocus(System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox cbo1)
		{
			string text = cbo1.Text;
			ItemInfo itemInfo = ItemsSource.FirstOrDefault((ItemInfo i) => string.Compare(text, i.Name, ignoreCase: true) == 0);
			if (itemInfo == null)
			{
				cbo1.Text = "";
				SelectedItem = null;
			}
			else
			{
				SelectedItem = itemInfo;
			}
		}

		private void TextBox_TextChanged(object sender, TextChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!(e.OriginalSource is System.Windows.Controls.TextBox textBox))
			{
				return;
			}
			string searchText = textBox.Text.ToUpperInvariant();
			if (GetTemplateChild("PART_ComboBox") is System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox comboBox)
			{
				comboBox.ItemsSource = ItemsSource.Where((ItemInfo p) => p.Description.ToUpperInvariant().Contains(searchText) || p.Name.ToUpperInvariant().Contains(searchText));
			}
		}

		private void Cbo_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox cbo1 = sender as System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox;
			if (cbo1 != null && cbo1.SelectedItem is ItemInfo selectedItem)
			{
				SelectedItem = selectedItem;
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Loaded, (Action)delegate
				{
					cbo1.ItemsSource = ItemsSource;
				});
			}
		}
	}
	public class ItemInfo
	{
		public string Name { get; set; }

		public string Description { get; set; }

		public ItemInfo(string name, string desc)
		{
			Name = name;
			Description = desc;
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return Name;
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (obj is ItemInfo itemInfo)
			{
				return itemInfo.Name.Equals(Name);
			}
			return false;
		}
	}

				return _newCustomAcmObjectCommand;
			}
		}

		public ICommand DuplicateAcmObjectCommand => (ICommand)new RelayActionCommand<object>((Action<object>)DuplicateAcmObjectCommand_Executed, (Func<object, bool>)DuplicateAcmObjectCommand_CanExecute);

		public ICommand RenameCustomAcmObjectCommand
		{
			get
			{
				if (_renameCustomAcmObjectCommand == null)
				{
					_renameCustomAcmObjectCommand = (ICommand)new RelayActionCommand<object>((Action<object>)RenameCustomAcmObjectCommand_Executed, (Func<object, bool>)RenameCustomAcmObjectCommand_CanExecute);
				}
				return _renameCustomAcmObjectCommand;
			}
		}

		public ICommand MakeDefaultCommand
		{
			get
			{
				if (_makeDefaultCommand == null)
				{
					_makeDefaultCommand = (ICommand)new RelayActionCommand<object>((Action<object>)MakeDefaultCommand_Executed, (Func<object, bool>)MakeDefaultCommand_CanExecute);
				}
				return _makeDefaultCommand;
			}
		}

		public ICommand GenerateCodeCommand
		{
			get
			{
				if (_GenerateCodeCommand == null)
				{
					_GenerateCodeCommand = (ICommand)new RelayActionCommand<object>((Action<object>)GenerateCodeCommand_Executed, (Func<object, bool>)GenerateCodeCommand_CanExecute);
				}
				return _GenerateCodeCommand;
			}
		}

		public ICommand ExportCommand
		{
			get
			{
				if (_ExportCommand == null)
				{
					_ExportCommand = (ICommand)new RelayActionCommand<object>((Action<object>)ExportCommand_Executed, (Func<object, bool>)ExportCommand_CanExecute);
				}
				return _ExportCommand;
			}
		}

		public List<int> SelectedRowIds
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_001a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_001f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				List<int> list = new List<int>();
				foreach (DataCellInfo totalOrderedSelectedCell in gridControl.TotalOrderedSelectedCells)
				{
					DataCellInfo current = totalOrderedSelectedCell;
					int num = gridControl.DataSource.Rows.IndexOf(((DataCellInfo)(ref current)).Row);
					if (num >= 0 && !list.Contains(num))
					{
						list.Add(num);
					}
				}
				return list;
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public AcmObjectManager(ACMModel parentModel, CustomModelsEditor parentEditor, ACMTreeNode node)
		{
			this.parentModel = parentModel;
			AcmType = node.ModelObject.Type;
			this.parentEditor = parentEditor;
			acmNode = node;
			InitializeComponent();
			((FrameworkElement)this).DataContext = this;
			UpdateAcmObjectList();
			if (AcmType == ACMObjectTypes.ACMIcon)
			{
				((UIElement)(object)cmdMakeDefault).Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
			}
			else
			{
				((UIElement)(object)cmdMakeDefault).Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			if (AcmType == ACMObjectTypes.ACMSubmodel)
			{
				((UIElement)(object)cmdRename).Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				((UIElement)(object)cmdDuplicate).Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			else if (AcmType == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomVariableType || AcmType == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomParameterType)
			{
				((UIElement)(object)cmdRename).Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				((UIElement)(object)cmdDuplicate).Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
			}
			else
			{
				((UIElement)(object)cmdRename).Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				((UIElement)(object)cmdDuplicate).Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
			}
			if (AcmType == ACMObjectTypes.ACMProcedure)
			{
				((UIElement)(object)cmdGenerateCode).Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				((UIElement)(object)cmdExport).Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
			}
			else
			{
				((UIElement)(object)cmdGenerateCode).Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				((UIElement)(object)cmdExport).Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			InitializeGenerateCommandParameters();
		}

		private bool EditAcmObjectCommand_CanExecute(object obj)
		{
			if (SelectedRowIds.Count != 1)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!(gridControl.DataSource.Rows.ElementAt(SelectedRowIds[0]).Tag is ACMObject aCMObject))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (aCMObject is ACMIcon && aCMObject.IsBuiltIn)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (aCMObject.GetType() == typeof(ACMTable) && aCMObject.Name.Equals("All Variables", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				if (obj != null && obj.GetType() == typeof(MouseButtonEventArgs))
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void EditAcmObjectCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			//IL_005e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (gridControl.DataSource.Rows.ElementAt(SelectedRowIds[0]).Tag is ACMObject aCMObject)
			{
				if (!_allAcmObjects.Contains(aCMObject))
				{
					MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(CustomModelsResources.ACMModelNotExist, aCMObject.Name), MMVersionHelper.MVGetProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
					UpdateAcmObjectList();
				}
				else if (aCMObject != null)
				{
					parentEditor.SelectFormFromObjectManager(aCMObject);
				}
			}
		}

		private bool DeleteAcmObjectCommand_CanExecute(object obj)
		{
			if (!(obj is ACMObject aCMObject))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (aCMObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMTable)
			{
				if (aCMObject.Name.Equals("All Variables", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			else if (aCMObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMIcon)
			{
				if (aCMObject.IsBuiltIn)
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			else if ((aCMObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMSubmodel || aCMObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomParameterType || aCMObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomVariableType || aCMObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMProcedure) && IsSubTextObjectInUse(aCMObject as ACMText))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void DeleteAcmObjectCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			if (obj is ACMObject obj2)
			{
				parentEditor.DeleteFromObjectManager(obj2);
				UpdateAcmObjectList();
			}
		}

		private string CreateNewVariableName(string name, string suffix, int maxLength = 256)
		{
			if (name.Length < maxLength - suffix.Length)
			{
				return name + suffix;
			}
			return name.Substring(0, maxLength - suffix.Length) + suffix;
		}

		private string GenerateDefaultAcmObjectName(IEnumerable<string> lstExistingName, string sNamePrefix)
		{
			int num = 0;
			string newName = null;
			do
			{
				newName = CreateNewVariableName(sNamePrefix, $"{++num}", 27);
			}
			while (lstExistingName != null && lstExistingName.Any((string p) => p.Equals(newName, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase)) && num < 2000);
			return newName;
		}

		private void NewCustomAcmObjectCommand_Executed()
		{
			parentEditor.AddNewFromObjectManager(acmNode);
		}

		private bool DuplicateAcmObjectCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (SelectedRowIds.Count != 1)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if ((gridControl.DataSource.Rows.ElementAt(SelectedRowIds[0]).Tag as ACMObject).GetType() == typeof(ACMSubmodel))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void DuplicateAcmObjectCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			if (gridControl.DataSource.Rows.ElementAt(SelectedRowIds[0]).Tag is ACMObject aCMObject)
			{
				ACMObject objToCopy;
				if (aCMObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMTable)
				{
					ACMTable aCMTable = aCMObject as ACMTable;
					objToCopy = new ACMTable(aCMObject.ParentModel, aCMObject.Name)
					{
						Variables = aCMTable.Variables,
						Attributes = aCMTable.Attributes,
						Description = aCMTable.Description,
						IsAllVariables = false,
						IsBuiltIn = false,
						IsADuplicate = true
					};
				}
				else if (aCMObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMPlot)
				{
					ACMPlot aCMPlot = aCMObject as ACMPlot;
					aCMPlot.LoadDefinition();
					objToCopy = new ACMPlot(aCMObject.ParentModel, aCMObject.Name)
					{
						Content = aCMPlot.Content,
						Is3D = aCMPlot.Is3D,
						Description = aCMPlot.Description,
						PlotInputFile = aCMPlot.PlotInputFile,
						ProfileDimensions = aCMPlot.ProfileDimensions,
						Profiles = aCMPlot.Profiles,
						ProfileVariables = aCMPlot.ProfileVariables,
						VariableNumbers = aCMPlot.VariableNumbers,
						XProfile = aCMPlot.XProfile,
						XVariables = aCMPlot.XVariables,
						YProfile = aCMPlot.YProfile,
						YVariables = aCMPlot.YVariables,
						IsADuplicate = true,
						IsBuiltIn = false
					};
				}
				else if (aCMObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMScript)
				{
					ACMScript aCMScript = aCMObject as ACMScript;
					aCMScript.Text = aCMScript.ParentModel.ACMDoc.GetScriptText(aCMScript.ParentModel.Name, aCMScript.Name);
					objToCopy = new ACMScript(aCMObject.ParentModel, aCMObject.Name, aCMScript.Text)
					{
						Description = aCMScript.Description,
						IsBuiltIn = false,
						IsADuplicate = true
					};
				}
				else if (aCMObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMIcon)
				{
					objToCopy = aCMObject;
				}
				else if (aCMObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMProcedure)
				{
					ACMProcedure aCMProcedure = aCMObject as ACMProcedure;
					aCMProcedure.Text = aCMProcedure.ParentModel.ACMDoc.GetProcedureText(aCMProcedure.Name);
					objToCopy = new ACMProcedure(aCMObject.ParentModel, aCMObject.Name, aCMProcedure.Text)
					{
						Description = aCMProcedure.Description,
						IsBuiltIn = false,
						IsADuplicate = true
					};
				}
				else if (aCMObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomVariableType)
				{
					ACMCustomVariableType aCMCustomVariableType = aCMObject as ACMCustomVariableType;
					aCMCustomVariableType.Text = aCMCustomVariableType.ParentModel.ACMDoc.GetModelText(aCMCustomVariableType.Name);
					objToCopy = new ACMCustomVariableType(aCMObject.ParentModel, aCMObject.Name, aCMCustomVariableType.Text)
					{
						InheritedType = aCMCustomVariableType.InheritedType,
						IsADuplicate = true,
						Value = aCMCustomVariableType.Value,
						Lower = aCMCustomVariableType.Lower,
						Upper = aCMCustomVariableType.Upper,
						PhyscialType = aCMCustomVariableType.PhyscialType,
						Units = aCMCustomVariableType.Units
					};
				}
				else if (aCMObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomParameterType)
				{
					ACMCustomParameterType aCMCustomParameterType = aCMObject as ACMCustomParameterType;
					aCMCustomParameterType.Text = aCMCustomParameterType.ParentModel.ACMDoc.GetModelText(aCMCustomParameterType.Name);
					objToCopy = new ACMCustomParameterType(aCMObject.ParentModel, aCMObject.Name, aCMCustomParameterType.Text)
					{
						IsADuplicate = true,
						ParameterType = aCMCustomParameterType.ParameterType,
						InheritedType = aCMCustomParameterType.InheritedType,
						Value = aCMCustomParameterType.Value,
						Lower = aCMCustomParameterType.Lower,
						Upper = aCMCustomParameterType.Upper,
						PhyscialType = aCMCustomParameterType.PhyscialType,
						Units = aCMCustomParameterType.Units
					};
				}
				else
				{
					objToCopy = aCMObject;
				}
				IEnumerable<ACMObject> allAcmObjects = _allAcmObjects;
				string defaultName = GenerateDefaultAcmObjectName(allAcmObjects.Select((ACMObject p) => p.Name), "DP_" + aCMObject.Name);
				parentEditor.DuplicateFromObjectManager(acmNode, defaultName, objToCopy);
			}
		}

		private bool RenameCustomAcmObjectCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (SelectedRowIds.Count != 1)
			{
				return false;
			}
			ACMObject aCMObject = gridControl.DataSource.Rows.ElementAt(SelectedRowIds[0]).Tag as ACMObject;
			if (aCMObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMSubmodel)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (aCMObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMTable)
			{
				if (aCMObject.Name.Equals("All Variables", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			else if (aCMObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMIcon)
			{
				if (aCMObject.Name.ToLower() == "system")
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			else if ((aCMObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomParameterType || aCMObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomVariableType) && IsSubTextObjectInUse(aCMObject as ACMText))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void RenameCustomAcmObjectCommand_Executed(object arg)
		{
			//IL_023c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0243: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (!(gridControl.DataSource.Rows.ElementAt(SelectedRowIds[0]).Tag is ACMObject aCMObject))
			{
				return;
			}
			ACMObject objToCopy;
			if (aCMObject.GetType() == typeof(ACMTable))
			{
				ACMTable aCMTable = aCMObject as ACMTable;
				objToCopy = new ACMTable(aCMObject.ParentModel, aCMObject.Name)
				{
					Variables = aCMTable.Variables,
					Attributes = aCMTable.Attributes,
					Description = aCMTable.Description,
					IsAllVariables = false,
					IsBuiltIn = false,
					IsADuplicate = true
				};
			}
			else if (aCMObject.GetType() == typeof(ACMPlot))
			{
				ACMPlot aCMPlot = aCMObject as ACMPlot;
				aCMPlot.LoadDefinition();
				objToCopy = new ACMPlot(aCMObject.ParentModel, aCMObject.Name)
				{
					Content = aCMPlot.Content,
					Is3D = aCMPlot.Is3D,
					Description = aCMPlot.Description,
					PlotInputFile = aCMPlot.PlotInputFile,
					ProfileDimensions = aCMPlot.ProfileDimensions,
					Profiles = aCMPlot.Profiles,
					ProfileVariables = aCMPlot.ProfileVariables,
					VariableNumbers = aCMPlot.VariableNumbers,
					XProfile = aCMPlot.XProfile,
					XVariables = aCMPlot.XVariables,
					YProfile = aCMPlot.YProfile,
					YVariables = aCMPlot.YVariables,
					IsADuplicate = true,
					IsBuiltIn = false
				};
			}
			else if (aCMObject.GetType() == typeof(ACMScript))
			{
				ACMScript aCMScript = aCMObject as ACMScript;
				aCMScript.Text = aCMScript.ParentModel.ACMDoc.GetScriptText(aCMScript.ParentModel.Name, aCMScript.Name);
				objToCopy = new ACMScript(aCMObject.ParentModel, aCMObject.Name, aCMScript.Text)
				{
					Description = aCMScript.Description,
					IsBuiltIn = false,
					IsADuplicate = true
				};
			}
			else
			{
				if (aCMObject is ACMIcon aCMIcon)
				{
					string text = string.Format(Resources.RenameConfirmPrompt, aCMIcon.Name);
					OneIdDialog val = new OneIdDialog(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, text, aCMIcon.Name, (string[])null, (maskType)7, Resources.strRenameChild, (int?)27);
					if (((Window)(object)val).ShowDialog() != true)
					{
						return;
					}
					string newID = val.NewID;
					if (!(newID == aCMIcon.Name))
					{
						try
						{
							parentModel.ACMDoc.RenameIcon(parentModel.Name, aCMIcon.TargetModelIcon, newID);
							parentModel.IsModified = true;
							parentEditor.DeleteEditorIfExists(aCMIcon);
							parentEditor.UpdateIconsNode();
							UpdateAcmObjectList();
							return;
						}
						catch (Exception)
						{
							return;
						}
					}
					return;
				}
				objToCopy = aCMObject;
			}
			parentEditor.RenameFromObjectManager(acmNode, aCMObject.Name, objToCopy);
			UpdateAcmObjectList();
		}

		private bool MakeDefaultCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (SelectedRowIds.Count != 1)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (gridControl.DataSource.Rows.ElementAt(SelectedRowIds[0]).Tag is ACMObject aCMObject)
			{
				return !aCMObject.IsDefault;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void MakeDefaultCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			if (gridControl.DataSource.Rows.ElementAt(SelectedRowIds[0]).Tag is ACMIcon aCMIcon)
			{
				parentModel.ACMDoc.SetDefaultIcon(parentModel.Name, aCMIcon.TargetModelIcon);
				parentModel.UpdateIcons();
				parentModel.IsModified = true;
				parentEditor.UpdateCompileButtonStatus(enable: true);
				UpdateAcmObjectList();
			}
		}

		private bool GenerateCodeCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (SelectedRowIds.Count != 1)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (gridControl.DataSource.Rows.ElementAt(SelectedRowIds[0]).Tag is ACMObject aCMObject)
			{
				return !aCMObject.IsDefault;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void GenerateCodeCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			if (gridControl.DataSource.Rows.ElementAt(SelectedRowIds[0]).Tag is ACMProcedure aCMProcedure)
			{
				GenerateCodeDialog generateCodeDialog = new GenerateCodeDialog
				{
					Owner = Window.GetWindow((DependencyObject)(object)this)
				};
				parentModel.ACMDoc.GetWorkingDir(out var workingDir);
				generateCodeDialog.IsGenTemplChecked = generateTemplates;
				generateCodeDialog.IsGenWrappChecked = generateWrappers;
				generateCodeDialog.IsGenMfChecked = generateMakefile;
				generateCodeDialog.IsGenDebugChecked = generateDebugCode;
				generateCodeDialog.IsExeMfChecked = executeMakefile;
				generateCodeDialog.WorkingDir = workingDir;
				if (generateCodeDialog.ShowDialog() == true)
				{
					generateTemplates = generateCodeDialog.IsGenTemplChecked;
					generateWrappers = generateCodeDialog.IsGenWrappChecked;
					generateMakefile = generateCodeDialog.IsGenMfChecked;
					generateDebugCode = generateCodeDialog.IsGenDebugChecked;
					executeMakefile = generateCodeDialog.IsExeMfChecked;
					workingDirectory = generateCodeDialog.WorkingDir;
					parentModel.ACMDoc.GenerateProcedures(parentModel.Name, aCMProcedure.Name, generateTemplates, generateWrappers, generateMakefile, generateDebugCode, executeMakefile, workingDirectory);
				}
			}
		}

		private void InitializeGenerateCommandParameters()
		{
			generateTemplates = true;
			generateWrappers = true;
			generateMakefile = true;
			generateDebugCode = true;
			executeMakefile = true;
			workingDirectory = Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.Personal), "AspenTech", SystemEnvironment.ApplicationNameWithPathVersion);
		}

		private bool ExportCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (SelectedRowIds.Count != 1)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (gridControl.DataSource.Rows.ElementAt(SelectedRowIds[0]).Tag is ACMObject aCMObject)
			{
				return !aCMObject.IsDefault;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void ExportCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			if (gridControl.DataSource.Rows.ElementAt(SelectedRowIds[0]).Tag is ACMProcedure aCMProcedure)
			{
				parentModel.ACMDoc.GetWorkingDir(out var workingDir);
				ExportCodeDialog exportCodeDialog = new ExportCodeDialog
				{
					Owner = Window.GetWindow((DependencyObject)(object)this)
				};
				exportCodeDialog.WorkingDir = workingDir;
				if (exportCodeDialog.ShowDialog() == true)
				{
					ExportworkingDirectory = exportCodeDialog.WorkingDir;
					parentModel.ACMDoc.ExportProcedure(parentModel.Name, aCMProcedure.Name, ExportworkingDirectory);
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateAcmObjectList()
		{
			if (AcmType == ACMObjectTypes.ACMTable)
			{
				_allAcmObjects = parentModel.Tables;
			}
			else if (AcmType == ACMObjectTypes.ACMPlot)
			{
				_allAcmObjects = parentModel.Plots;
				foreach (ACMPlot plot in parentModel.Plots)
				{
					plot.LoadDefinition();
				}
			}
			else if (AcmType == ACMObjectTypes.ACMScript)
			{
				_allAcmObjects = parentModel.Scripts;
			}
			else if (AcmType == ACMObjectTypes.ACMIcon)
			{
				_allAcmObjects = parentModel.Icons;
			}
			else if (AcmType == ACMObjectTypes.ACMSubmodel)
			{
				_allAcmObjects = parentModel.SubModels;
			}
			else if (AcmType == ACMObjectTypes.ACMProcedure)
			{
				_allAcmObjects = parentModel.Procedures;
			}
			else if (AcmType == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomVariableType)
			{
				_allAcmObjects = parentModel.CustomVariableTypes;
			}
			else if (AcmType == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomParameterType)
			{
				_allAcmObjects = parentModel.CustomParameterTypes;
			}
			_allAcmObjects = _allAcmObjects?.OrderBy((ACMObject o) => o.Name);
			RefreshGridControl();
		}

		private DataTemplate CreateCheckmarkTemplate(bool isChecked)
		{
			DataTemplate dataTemplate = new DataTemplate();
			FrameworkElementFactory frameworkElementFactory = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(System.Windows.Controls.Image));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(System.Windows.Controls.Image.SourceProperty, ImageResources.Checkmark);
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(FrameworkElement.MarginProperty, new Thickness(4.0));
			if (isChecked)
			{
				frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, Visibility.Visible);
			}
			else
			{
				frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, Visibility.Collapsed);
			}
			dataTemplate.VisualTree = frameworkElementFactory;
			return dataTemplate;
		}

		private DataTemplate CreateDeleteFieldTemplate(ACMObject acmObjectInfo)
		{
			DataTemplate dataTemplate = new DataTemplate();
			FrameworkElementFactory frameworkElementFactory = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(MMDeleteButton));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(System.Windows.Controls.Control.BackgroundProperty, System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent);
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(System.Windows.Controls.Control.BorderBrushProperty, System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent);
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(FrameworkElement.HorizontalAlignmentProperty, System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Stretch);
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.ButtonBase.CommandProperty, DeleteAcmObjectCommand);
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.ButtonBase.CommandParameterProperty, acmObjectInfo);
			frameworkElementFactory.SetBinding(UIElement.IsEnabledProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Row.Tag")
			{
				Converter = new CanAcmObjectBeDeletedConverter()
			});
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(ToolTipService.ShowOnDisabledProperty, true);
			dataTemplate.VisualTree = frameworkElementFactory;
			return dataTemplate;
		}

		public static bool IsAcmObjectInUse(ACMObject acmObjectInfo)
		{
			return false;
		}

		private void GridControl_CellValueChanged(object sender, CellValueChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ACMObject aCMObject = e.Data.Row.Tag as ACMObject;
			if (e.Data.Column.Tag is string text && text == "Description")
			{
				if (aCMObject != null)
				{
					aCMObject.Description = e.NewValue as string;
				}
				else
				{
					aCMObject.Description = e.NewValue as string;
				}
				((CompositePresentationEvent<EventArgs>)(object)UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<RefreshACMModelManagerEvent>()).Publish(new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		private bool IsSubTextObjectInUse(ACMText subTextObject)
		{
			try
			{
				if (parentEditor.IsSubTextObjectInUse(subTextObject))
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			return false;
		}

		{
			this.PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
		}

		private void GridControl_DefaultContextMenuOpening(object sender, DefaultContextMenuEventArgs e)
		{
			e.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Add(new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem
			{
				Header = ApwnResources.ApwnShellCopy,
				Command = ApplicationCommands.Copy,
				CommandTarget = (IInputElement)gridControl
			});
			e.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Add(new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem
			{
				Header = ApwnResources.ApwnShellPaste,
				Command = ApplicationCommands.Paste,
				CommandTarget = (IInputElement)gridControl
			});
			((RoutedEventArgs)(object)e).Handled = true;
		}

		private void gridControl_PreviewMouseDoubleClick(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (EditAcmObjectCommand.CanExecute(e))
			{
				EditAcmObjectCommand.Execute(e);
			}
		}

	internal class CanAcmObjectBeDeletedConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value is ACMObject acmObjectInfo)
			{
				return !AcmObjectManager.IsAcmObjectInUse(acmObjectInfo);
			}
			return false;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}

		public class ConditionExpression : INotifyPropertyChanged
		{
			private bool isIncluded = true;

			private string rightStringExpression;

			private static readonly string[] RealOperators = new string[7]
			{
				"==",
				">",
				"<",
				">=",

				"<>",

			private static readonly string[] StringOperators = new string[2] { "==", "<>" };

			private readonly ACMModel _model;

			private readonly ACMParser _parser;

			public bool IsBuiltin { get; set; }

			public bool AllowAdd => Keyword == "ElseIf";

			public bool AllowDelete
			{
				get
				{
					if (!IsBuiltin)
					{
						return Keyword == "ElseIf";
					}
					return false;
				}
			}

			public bool AllowIngore
			{
				get
				{
					if (!(Keyword == "ElseIf"))
					{
						return Keyword == "Else";
					}
					return true;
				}
			}

			public bool IsIncluded
			{
				get
				{
					return isIncluded;
				}
				set
				{
					isIncluded = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("IsIncluded");
				}
			}

			public bool HasCondition
			{
				get
				{
					if (!(Keyword == "If"))
					{
						return Keyword == "ElseIf";
					}
					return true;
				}
			}

			public string Keyword { get; set; }

			public string LeftExpression
			{
				get
				{
					return leftExpression;
				}
				set
				{
					if (!(leftExpression != value))
					{
						return;
					}
					leftExpression = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("LeftExpression");
					string text = null;
					bool flag = true;
					if (_parser.TypeStatement != null)
					{
						foreach (Statement statement in _parser.TypeStatement.Statements)
						{
							if (statement is DeclarationStatement declarationStatement && declarationStatement.Name == value)
							{
								text = declarationStatement.TypeName;
								break;
							}
						}
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						string[] array = _model.ACMDoc.GetModelText(text).Split(new char[2] { ' ', '\n' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
						if (array.Length >= 2)
						{
							if (string.Equals(array[0], "Variable", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
							{
								flag = true;
							}
							else if (string.Equals(array[0], "Parameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
							{
								text = array[1];
								string sDataType;
								string sDescription;
								string sUnits;
								string sPhysicalQuantity;
								string sValue;
								string sUpper;
								string sLower;
								string[] parameterValues = _model.ACMDoc.GetParameterValues(text, out sDataType, out sDescription, out sUnits, out sPhysicalQuantity, out sValue, out sUpper, out sLower);
								flag = ((sDataType == "REAL" || sDataType == "INT") ? true : false);
								ValueOptions.Clear();
								RightStringExpression = null;
								if (parameterValues != null)
								{
									string[] array2 = parameterValues;
									foreach (string item in array2)
									{
										ValueOptions.Add(item);
									}
									RightStringExpression = ValueOptions[0];
								}
							}
						}
					}
					IsReal = flag;
				}
			}

			public string Operator
			{
				get
				{
					return op;
				}
				set
				{
					if (op != value)
					{
						op = value;
						OnPropertyChanged("Operator");
						OnPropertyChanged("IsRighExpressionEnabled");
					}
				}
			}

			public bool IsRighExpressionEnabled => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(Operator);

			public string RightRealExpression
			{
				get
				{
					return rightRealExpression;
				}
				set
				{
					if (rightRealExpression != value)
					{
						rightRealExpression = value;
						OnPropertyChanged("RightRealExpression");
					}
				}
			}

			public string RightStringExpression
			{
				get
				{
					return rightStringExpression;
				}
				set
				{
					if (rightStringExpression != value)
					{
						rightStringExpression = value;
						OnPropertyChanged("RightStringExpression");
					}
				}
			}

			public ObservableCollection<string> ValueOptions => valueOptions;

			public bool HasComment => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(Comment);

			public string Comment { get; set; }

			public bool IsReal
			{
				get
				{
					return isReal;
				}
				set
				{
					if (isReal != value)
					{
						isReal = value;
						OnPropertyChanged("IsReal");
						OnPropertyChanged("IsRealVisible");
						OnPropertyChanged("IsStringVisible");
						OnPropertyChanged("Operators");
						Operator = Operators[0];
					}
				}
			}

			public bool IsRealVisible
			{
				get
				{
					if (HasCondition)
					{
						return IsReal;
					}
					return false;
				}
			}

			public bool IsStringVisible
			{
				get
				{
					if (HasCondition)
					{
						return !IsReal;
					}
					return false;
				}
			}

			public string[] Operators
			{
				get
				{
					if (!IsReal)
					{
						return StringOperators;
					}
					return RealOperators;
				}
			}

			public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

			public ConditionExpression(ACMModel model, ACMParser parser)
			{
				_model = model;
				_parser = parser;
				Operator = Operators[0];
			}

			protected void OnPropertyChanged([CallerMemberName] string name = null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(name));
			}
		}

		public MathOperatorGroups SelectedGroup
		{
			get
			{
				return selectedGroup;
			}
			set
			{
				if (selectedGroup != value)
				{
					selectedGroup = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("SelectedGroup");
					MathFunctionsView.Filter = (object x) => x is MathFunction mathFunction && (mathFunction.GroupName & value) == mathFunction.GroupName;
					if (value != MathOperatorGroups.All)
					{
						SelectedMathFunction = MathFunctionsView.CurrentItem as MathFunction;
					}
					OnPropertyChanged("DelayGridVisibility");
					OnPropertyChanged("FunctionGridVisibility");
				}
			}
		}

		public string ACMPreviewText
		{
			get
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				if (OperationType == OperationTypes.For)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(IndexName) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(SetName))
					{
						stringBuilder.AppendLine("For " + IndexName + " In " + SetName + " Do");
						stringBuilder.AppendLine("// Enter equations using elements of the set");
						stringBuilder.AppendLine("EndFor");
					}
				}
				else if (OperationType == OperationTypes.IF)
				{
					foreach (ConditionExpression conditionExpression in ConditionExpressions)
					{
						if (!conditionExpression.IsIncluded)
						{
							continue;
						}
						if (conditionExpression.HasCondition)
						{
							if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(conditionExpression.LeftExpression))
							{
								return null;
							}
							if (conditionExpression.IsReal)
							{
								if (conditionExpression.IsRighExpressionEnabled)
								{
									if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(conditionExpression.RightRealExpression))
									{
										return null;
									}
									stringBuilder.AppendLine(conditionExpression.Keyword + " (" + conditionExpression.LeftExpression + " " + conditionExpression.Operator + " " + conditionExpression.RightRealExpression + ") Then");
								}
								else
								{
									stringBuilder.AppendLine(conditionExpression.Keyword + " (" + conditionExpression.LeftExpression + ") Then");
								}
							}
							else
							{
								if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(conditionExpression.RightStringExpression))
								{
									return null;
								}
								stringBuilder.AppendLine(conditionExpression.Keyword + " (" + conditionExpression.LeftExpression + " " + conditionExpression.Operator + " \"" + conditionExpression.RightStringExpression + "\") Then");
							}
						}
						else
						{
							stringBuilder.AppendLine(conditionExpression.Keyword ?? "");
						}
						if (conditionExpression.HasComment)
						{
							stringBuilder.AppendLine(conditionExpression.Comment);
						}
					}
				}
				else if (OperationType == OperationTypes.Math)
				{
					if (SelectedMathFunction != null)
					{
						if (SelectedMathFunction.Operator == "Time")
						{
							stringBuilder.AppendLine("Time");
						}
						else if (SelectedMathFunction.Operator == "Delay")
						{
							if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(DelayVariable) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(DelayLagTime))
							{
								stringBuilder.AppendLine("Delay " + DelayVariable + " By " + DelayLagTime);
							}
						}
						else if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(MathExpression))
						{
							stringBuilder.AppendLine(SelectedMathFunction.Operator + "(" + MathExpression + ")");
						}
					}
				}
				else if (OperationType == OperationTypes.Sum)
				{
					if (SigmaChecked)
					{
						if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(SigmaExpression))
						{
							stringBuilder.AppendLine("Sigma(" + SigmaExpression + ")");
						}
					}
					else if (SigmaForEachChecked && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(SigmaIndexName) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(SigmaSetName) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(SigmaForEachExpression))
					{
						stringBuilder.AppendLine("Sigma(ForEach(" + SigmaIndexName + " In " + SigmaSetName + ") " + SigmaForEachExpression + ")");
					}
				}
				return stringBuilder.ToString();
			}
			set
			{
				previewText = value;
			}
		}
