		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		private List<string> GetVariables(DataTypes type)
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			HashSet<string> hashSet = new HashSet<string>();
			string[] aMTypeList = Model.ACMDoc.GetAMTypeList(2);
			foreach (string text in aMTypeList)
			{
				hashSet.Add(text.ToUpper());
			}
			HashSet<string> hashSet2 = new HashSet<string>();
			aMTypeList = Model.ACMDoc.GetAMTypeList(4);
			foreach (string text2 in aMTypeList)
			{
				hashSet2.Add(text2.ToUpper());
			}
			if (ParentDialog != null)
			{
				string editorText = ParentDialog.EditorCtrl.GetEditorText();
				_parser.Compile(editorText);
				if (_parser.TypeStatement == null)
				{
					return list;
				}
				foreach (Statement statement in _parser.TypeStatement.Statements)
				{
					if (!(statement is DeclarationStatement declarationStatement) || hashSet2.Contains(declarationStatement.TypeName.ToUpper()) || string.Equals(declarationStatement.TypeName, "LengthDomain", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
					{
						continue;
					}
					if ((type & DataTypes.Set) == DataTypes.Set)
					{
						if (string.Equals(declarationStatement.TypeName, "IntegerSet", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) || string.Equals(declarationStatement.TypeName, "StringSet", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
						{
							list.Add(declarationStatement.Name);
						}
						else if (hashSet.Contains(declarationStatement.TypeName.ToUpper()) && declarationStatement.FlagList != null && declarationStatement.FlagList[declarationStatement.FlagList.Length - 1].Text.Equals("of", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
						{
							list.Add(declarationStatement.Name + ".ConnectionSet");
						}
					}
					if ((type & DataTypes.Variable) == DataTypes.Variable && !hashSet.Contains(declarationStatement.TypeName.ToUpper()))
					{
						if (declarationStatement.ArrayList != null)
						{
							list.Add(declarationStatement.Name + "(<index>)");
						}
						else
						{
							list.Add(declarationStatement.Name);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		private void VariableNames_GotFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!(sender is System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox { Tag: var tag } comboBox))
			{
				return;
			}
			tag?.ToString();
			string text = comboBox.Text;
			comboBox.Items.Clear();
			comboBox.Text = text;
			if (!(comboBox.Tag is DataTypes dataTypes))
			{
				return;
			}
			if ((dataTypes & DataTypes.Set) == DataTypes.Set)
			{
				comboBox.Items.Add("componentlist");
			}
			foreach (string variable in GetVariables(dataTypes))
			{
				comboBox.Items.Add(variable);
			}
		}

		private void ConditionExpressions_SourceUpdated(object sender, DataTransferEventArgs e)
		{
			OnPropertyChanged("ACMPreviewText");
		}

		private bool CanAddConditionExpression(ConditionExpression arg)
		{
			return ConditionExpressions.Count <= 10;
		}

		private void AddConditionExpression(ConditionExpression conditionExpression)
		{
			if (conditionExpression == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("conditionExpression");
			}
			int num = 0;
			foreach (ConditionExpression conditionExpression2 in ConditionExpressions)
			{
				num++;
				if (conditionExpression2 == conditionExpression)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			if (num <= ConditionExpressions.Count)
			{
				ConditionExpression item = new ConditionExpression(Model, _parser)
				{
					Keyword = conditionExpression.Keyword,
					Comment = conditionExpression.Comment
				};
				ConditionExpressions.Insert(num, item);
				OnPropertyChanged("ACMPreviewText");
			}
		}

		private void DeleteConditionExpression(ConditionExpression conditionExpression)
		{
			if (ConditionExpressions.Remove(conditionExpression))
			{
				OnPropertyChanged("ACMPreviewText");
			}
		}

		private bool InsertCommand_CanExecute()
		{
			return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(ACMPreviewText);
		}

		private void InsertCommand_Executed()
		{
			if (ParentDialog != null)
			{
				ParentDialog.EditorCtrl.InsertText(ACMPreviewText);
			}
		}

		private bool CopyCommand_CanExecute()
		{
			return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(ACMPreviewText);
		}

		private void CopyCommand_Executed()
		{
			System.Windows.Clipboard.SetText(ACMPreviewText);
		}

		private void ClearCommand_Executed()
		{
			if (OperationType == OperationTypes.For)
			{
				IndexName = null;
				SetName = null;
				return;
			}
			if (OperationType == OperationTypes.IF)
			{
				foreach (ConditionExpression conditionExpression in ConditionExpressions)
				{
					conditionExpression.LeftExpression = null;
					conditionExpression.RightRealExpression = null;
					conditionExpression.RightStringExpression = null;
				}
				return;
			}
			if (OperationType == OperationTypes.Sum)
			{
				if (SigmaChecked)
				{
					SigmaExpression = null;
				}
				else if (SigmaForEachChecked)
				{
					SigmaIndexName = null;
					SigmaSetName = null;
					SigmaForEachExpression = null;
				}
			}
			else if (OperationType == OperationTypes.Math)
			{
				MathExpression = null;
				DelayVariable = null;
				DelayLagTime = null;
			}
		}

		public SyntaxAssistantInput()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			base.DataContext = this;
			base.Loaded += SyntaxAssistantInput_Loaded;
		}

		private void SyntaxAssistantInput_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!_isLoaded)
			{
				Initialize();
				_isLoaded = true;
			}
		}

		private void Initialize()
		{
			if (OperationType == OperationTypes.For)
			{
				MMHelp.SetHelpId((DependencyObject)this, (int?)1130001);
			}
			else if (OperationType == OperationTypes.IF)
			{
				MMHelp.SetHelpId((DependencyObject)this, (int?)1130002);
				ConditionExpressions.Add(new ConditionExpression(Model, _parser)
				{
					IsBuiltin = true,
					Keyword = "If",
					Comment = "// Enter equations"
				});
				ConditionExpressions.Add(new ConditionExpression(Model, _parser)
				{
					IsBuiltin = true,
					Keyword = "ElseIf",
					Comment = "// Enter matching number of equations"
				});
				ConditionExpressions.Add(new ConditionExpression(Model, _parser)
				{
					IsBuiltin = true,
					Keyword = "Else",
					Comment = "// Enter matching number of equations"
				});
				ConditionExpressions.Add(new ConditionExpression(Model, _parser)
				{
					IsBuiltin = true,
					Keyword = "EndIf"
				});
			}
			else if (OperationType == OperationTypes.Math)
			{
				MMHelp.SetHelpId((DependencyObject)this, (int?)1130003);
			}
			else if (OperationType == OperationTypes.Sum)
			{
				MMHelp.SetHelpId((DependencyObject)this, (int?)1130004);
			}
			else
			{
				MMHelp.SetHelpId((DependencyObject)this, (int?)1073000);
			}
		}

	public class VarNameRule : ValidationRule
	{
		public ModelWrapper Wrapper { get; set; }

		public override ValidationResult Validate(object value, CultureInfo cultureInfo)
		{
			string text = value?.ToString();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return new ValidationResult(isValid: false, CustomModelsResources.VarNameError_EmptyString);
			}
			if (Wrapper != null && Wrapper.IsExpression)
			{
				return ValidationResult.ValidResult;
			}
			if (text.Length > 27)
			{
				return new ValidationResult(isValid: false, CustomModelsResources.VarNameError_TooLong);
			}
			if (!char.IsLetter(text[0]))
			{
				return new ValidationResult(isValid: false, CustomModelsResources.VarNameError_InvalidStart);
			}
			string text2 = text;
			foreach (char c in text2)
			{
				if (!char.IsLetter(c) && !char.IsDigit(c) && c != '_')
				{
					return new ValidationResult(isValid: false, CustomModelsResources.VarNameError_InvalidCharacter);
				}
			}
			if (Wrapper != null)
			{
				ACMModel model = Wrapper.Model;
				if ((model != null && model.ACMDoc.IsKeyword(text)) || text.Equals("IntegerParameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) || text.Equals("RealParameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) || text.Equals("StringParameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) || text.Equals("LogicalParameter", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					return new ValidationResult(isValid: false, CustomModelsResources.VarNameError_IsKeyword);
				}
			}
			return ValidationResult.ValidResult;
		}
	}

	public class MathFunctionTemplateSelector : DataTemplateSelector
	{
		public override DataTemplate SelectTemplate(object item, DependencyObject container)
		{
			ContentPresenter contentPresenter = (ContentPresenter)container;
			if (contentPresenter.TemplatedParent is System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox)
			{
				return (DataTemplate)contentPresenter.FindResource("MathFunctionDisplayTemplate");
			}
			if (contentPresenter.TemplatedParent is ComboBoxItem)
			{
				return (DataTemplate)contentPresenter.FindResource("MathFunctionItemTemplate");
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public class EnumBooleanConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return value.Equals(parameter);
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (!(bool)value)
			{
				return System.Windows.Data.Binding.DoNothing;
			}
			return parameter;
		}
	}

		private bool AddCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(newItemTextBox.Text.Trim());
		}

		private void AddCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			string name = newItemTextBox.Text.Trim();
			Items.Add(new VariableItem(name));
			newItemTextBox.Clear();
		}

		private bool DeleteVariableCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			return true;
		}

		private void DeleteVariableCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			string profileName = obj as string;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(profileName))
			{
				VariableItem variableItem = Items.FirstOrDefault((VariableItem item) => item.Name.Equals(profileName, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase));
				if (variableItem != null)
				{
					Items.Remove(variableItem);
				}
			}
		}

		private bool MoveUpCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			return variableListBox.SelectedIndex > 0;
		}

		private void MoveUpCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			int selectedIndex = variableListBox.SelectedIndex;
			Items.Move(selectedIndex, selectedIndex - 1);
		}

		private bool MoveDownCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (variableListBox.SelectedIndex >= 0)
			{
				return variableListBox.SelectedIndex < variableListBox.Items.Count - 1;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void MoveDownCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			int selectedIndex = variableListBox.SelectedIndex;
			Items.Move(selectedIndex, selectedIndex + 1);
		}

		private void VariableBox_SelectedEvent(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			VariableBox variableBox = sender as VariableBox;
			string variableName = variableBox.Variable;
			VariableItem variableItem = Items.FirstOrDefault((VariableItem item) => item.Name.Equals(variableName, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase));
			if (variableItem != null)
			{
				int num = Items.IndexOf(variableItem);
				if (num >= 0)
				{
					variableListBox.SelectedIndex = num;
				}
			}
		}

		private void searchButton_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			FindVariablesDialog findVariablesDialog = new FindVariablesDialog(Model, Items.Select((VariableItem v) => v.Name))
			{
				Owner = Window.GetWindow(this)
			};
			if (findVariablesDialog.ShowDialog() != true)
			{
				return;
			}
			Items.Clear();
			foreach (ACMVariable selectedVariable in findVariablesDialog.SelectedVariables)
			{
				Items.Add(new VariableItem(selectedVariable.Name));
			}
		}

		public ObservableCollection<VariableItem> Variables
		{
			get
			{

		}

				plot3D.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				plot2D.PlotObject = PlotObject;
				plot2D.Redraw();

		}

	public class TableEditor : System.Windows.Controls.UserControl, IAcmEditor, INotifyPropertyChanged, IComponentConnector
	{
		private Collection<ACMVariable> AllVariables = new Collection<ACMVariable>();

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AvailableVariablesProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AvailableVariables", typeof(ObservableCollection<ACMVariable>), typeof(TableEditor), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnAvaiableVariablesPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty SelectedVariablesProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SelectedVariables", typeof(ObservableCollection<ACMVariable>), typeof(TableEditor), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnSelectedVariablesPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly List<string> AllAttributes = new List<string>
		{
			CustomTableDataTable.GetDisplayedTextOfAttribute(ACMAttribute.Derivative),
			CustomTableDataTable.GetDisplayedTextOfAttribute(ACMAttribute.Description),
			CustomTableDataTable.GetDisplayedTextOfAttribute(ACMAttribute.Lower),
			CustomTableDataTable.GetDisplayedTextOfAttribute(ACMAttribute.Record),
			CustomTableDataTable.GetDisplayedTextOfAttribute(ACMAttribute.Scale),
			CustomTableDataTable.GetDisplayedTextOfAttribute(ACMAttribute.Spec),
			CustomTableDataTable.GetDisplayedTextOfAttribute(ACMAttribute.Tag),
			CustomTableDataTable.GetDisplayedTextOfAttribute(ACMAttribute.Units),
			CustomTableDataTable.GetDisplayedTextOfAttribute(ACMAttribute.Upper),
			CustomTableDataTable.GetDisplayedTextOfAttribute(ACMAttribute.Value)
		};

		private ObservableCollection<string> _availableAttributes;

		private ObservableCollection<string> _selectedAttributes;

		private static RoutedUICommand ShowPropertiesCommand = new RoutedUICommand(CustomModelsResources.TableEditor_MenuHeader_Properties, "ShowProperties", typeof(TableEditor));

		internal TabItem variablesTab;

		internal FindVariablesControl ctrlFindVariables;

		internal TabItem attributesTab;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.CheckBox chkUseGlobalSettings;

		internal MMListPicker attributesPicker;

		internal TabItem tableTab;

		internal GridControl tableGridControl;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public ACMObject AcmObject { get; set; }

		public ACMTable TableObject => AcmObject as ACMTable;

		public string[] ModelVariables { get; set; }

		private string[] ModelParameters { get; set; }

		public ObservableCollection<ACMVariable> AvailableVariables
		{
			get
			{
				return (ObservableCollection<ACMVariable>)GetValue(AvailableVariablesProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(AvailableVariablesProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public ObservableCollection<ACMVariable> SelectedVariables
		{
			get
			{
				return (ObservableCollection<ACMVariable>)GetValue(SelectedVariablesProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(SelectedVariablesProperty, value);
				if (SelectedVariablesProperty != null)
				{
					SelectedVariables.CollectionChanged += _selectedVariables_CollectionChanged;
				}
			}
		}

		public ObservableCollection<string> AvailableAttributes
		{
			get
			{
				return _availableAttributes;
			}
			set
			{
				_availableAttributes = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("AvailableAttributes");
				if (_availableAttributes != null)
				{
					_availableAttributes.CollectionChanged += _availableAttributes_CollectionChanged;
				}
			}
		}

		public ObservableCollection<string> SelectedAttributes
		{
			get
			{
				return _selectedAttributes;
			}
			set
			{
				_selectedAttributes = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("SelectedAttributes");
				if (_selectedAttributes != null)
				{
					_selectedAttributes.CollectionChanged += _selectedAttributes_CollectionChanged;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsModified { get; private set; }

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public bool GetEnableCompileBtn()
		{
			return true;
		}

		public TableEditor(ACMObject acmObject)
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			ctrlFindVariables.chkFixedVars.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			AcmObject = acmObject;
			TableObject.Attributes = new Collection<ACMAttribute>
			{
				ACMAttribute.Value,
				ACMAttribute.Type,
				ACMAttribute.Spec,
				ACMAttribute.Lower,
				ACMAttribute.Upper
			};
			InitializeAttributesList();
			GetAllVariables();
			if (TableObject.IsAllVariables)
			{
				TableObject.Variables = AllVariables;
			}
			else if (!TableObject.IsADuplicate)
			{
				TableObject.Variables = new Collection<ACMVariable>();
				TableObject.ParentModel.ACMDoc.GetTableDefinition(TableObject.ParentModel.ModelTypeName, TableObject.Name, out var aTableVars);
				string[] array = aTableVars;
				foreach (string text in array)
				{
					foreach (ACMVariable allVariable in AllVariables)
					{
						if (allVariable.Name == text)
						{
							TableObject.Variables.Add(allVariable);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			InitializeVariableLists();
			if (TableObject.IsBuiltIn)
			{
				variablesTab.IsEnabled = false;
				tableTab.IsSelected = true;
			}
			tableGridControl.DataSource = (DataTable)(object)new CustomTableDataTable(TableObject);
			tableGridControl.DefaultContextMenuOpening += GridControl_DefaultContextMenuOpening;
			base.Loaded += TablePropertiesDialog_Loaded;
		}

		private void InitializeVariableLists()
		{
			ObservableCollection<ACMVariable> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<ACMVariable>();
			ObservableCollection<ACMVariable> observableCollection2 = new ObservableCollection<ACMVariable>();
			foreach (ACMVariable allVariable in AllVariables)
			{
				if (TableObject.Variables.Contains(allVariable))
				{
					observableCollection.Add(allVariable);
				}
				else
				{
					observableCollection2.Add(allVariable);
				}
			}
			SelectedVariables = observableCollection;
			AvailableVariables = observableCollection2;
			if (!TableObject.IsADuplicate)
			{
				IsModified = false;
			}
			else
			{
				IsModified = true;
			}
		}

		private void InitializeAttributesList()
		{
			ObservableCollection<string> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<string>();
			if (TableObject.Attributes != null)
			{
				foreach (ACMAttribute attribute in TableObject.Attributes)
				{
					observableCollection.Add(CustomTableDataTable.GetDisplayedTextOfAttribute(attribute));
				}
			}
			SelectedAttributes = observableCollection;
			ObservableCollection<string> observableCollection2 = new ObservableCollection<string>();
			foreach (string allAttribute in AllAttributes)
			{
				if (!observableCollection.Contains(allAttribute))
				{
					observableCollection2.Add(allAttribute);
				}
			}
			AvailableAttributes = observableCollection2;
		}

		private void _availableAttributes_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		private void _selectedAttributes_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ObservableCollection<string> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<string>();
			foreach (string allAttribute in AllAttributes)
			{
				if (!_selectedAttributes.Contains(allAttribute))
				{
					observableCollection.Add(allAttribute);
				}
			}
			AvailableAttributes = observableCollection;
			SaveChanges();
		}

		private void GetAllVariables()
		{
			ModelVariables = TableObject.ParentModel.ACMDoc.GetModelVariables(TableObject.ParentModel.ModelTypeName);
			TableObject.ParentModel.ACMDoc.GetModelVariablesAttributes(TableObject.ParentModel.ModelTypeName, out var specArr, out var descArr, out var upperArr, out var lowerArr, out var valueArr, out var typeArr);
			ModelParameters = TableObject.ParentModel.ACMDoc.GetModelParameters(TableObject.ParentModel.ModelTypeName);
			Collection<ACMVariable> collection = new Collection<ACMVariable>();
			for (int i = 0; i < ModelVariables.Length; i++)
			{
				ACMVariable aCMVariable = new ACMVariable(ModelVariables[i]);
				aCMVariable.Spec = specArr[i];
				aCMVariable.Description = descArr[i];
				aCMVariable.Upper = upperArr[i];
				aCMVariable.Lower = lowerArr[i];
				aCMVariable.Value = valueArr[i];
				aCMVariable.Type = typeArr[i];
				aCMVariable.IsParameter = false;
				aCMVariable.IsArray = false;
				collection.Add(aCMVariable);
			}
			AllVariables = collection;
			foreach (ACMVariable allVariable in AllVariables)
			{
				if (allVariable.Name.Contains("(*)"))
				{
					allVariable.IsArray = true;
				}
				else if (allVariable.Spec.Equals(""))
				{
					ModelParameters = new List<string>(ModelParameters) { allVariable.Name }.ToArray();
					allVariable.IsParameter = true;
				}
			}
		}

		private void _selectedVariables_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			SaveChanges();
		}

		private static void OnSelectedVariablesPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is TableEditor tableEditor)
			{
				tableEditor.SaveChanges();
			}
		}

		private static void OnAvaiableVariablesPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			_ = d is TableEditor;
		}

		private void OnPropertyChanged(string propName)
		{
			PropertyChangedEventHandler propertyChangedEventHandler = this.PropertyChanged;
			if (propertyChangedEventHandler != null)
			{
				propertyChangedEventHandler(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
				if (propName.EndsWith("Variables"))
				{
					SaveChanges();
				}
			}
		}

		private void SaveChanges()
		{
			if (SelectedVariables == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			Collection<ACMVariable> collection = new Collection<ACMVariable>();
			foreach (ACMVariable allVariable in AllVariables)
			{
				if (SelectedVariables.Contains(allVariable))
				{
					collection.Add(allVariable);
				}
			}
			TableObject.Variables = collection;
			IsModified = true;
			tableGridControl.DataSource = (DataTable)(object)new CustomTableDataTable(TableObject);
		}

		private void refreshVariableList()
		{
			GetAllVariables();
			if (TableObject.IsAllVariables)
			{
				TableObject.Variables = AllVariables;
			}
			else if (!IsModified)
			{
				TableObject.ParentModel.ACMDoc.GetTableDefinition(TableObject.ParentModel.ModelTypeName, TableObject.Name, out var aTableVars);
				Collection<ACMVariable> collection = new Collection<ACMVariable>();
				string[] array = aTableVars;
				foreach (string text in array)
				{
					foreach (ACMVariable allVariable in AllVariables)
					{
						if (allVariable.Name == text)
						{
							collection.Add(allVariable);
						}
					}
				}
				TableObject.Variables = collection;
			}
			ObservableCollection<ACMVariable> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<ACMVariable>();
			foreach (ACMVariable allVariable2 in AllVariables)
			{
				if (!TableObject.Variables.Contains(allVariable2))
				{
					observableCollection.Add(allVariable2);
				}
			}
			AvailableVariables = observableCollection;
			tableGridControl.DataSource = (DataTable)(object)new CustomTableDataTable(TableObject);
		}

		private void AddVariableButton_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		private void RemoveVariableButton_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		private void GridControl_DefaultContextMenuOpening(object sender, DefaultContextMenuEventArgs e)
		{
			System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem newItem = new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem
			{
				Command = ApplicationCommands.Copy,
				CommandTarget = (IInputElement)tableGridControl
			};
			System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem newItem2 = new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem
			{
				Command = ApplicationCommands.Paste,
				CommandTarget = (IInputElement)tableGridControl
			};
			e.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Clear();
			e.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Add(newItem);
			e.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Add(newItem2);
		}

		private void TablePropertiesDialog_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			InitializeAttributesList();
			base.DataContext = this;
		}

		public void OnClosed()
		{
		}

		public void OnLoaded()
		{
			refreshVariableList();
		}

		public void OnUnloaded()
		{
		}

		public void OnClosing()
		{
		}

		public void Save()
		{
			if (!IsModified)
			{
				return;
			}
			int count = TableObject.Variables.Count;
			int count2 = TableObject.Attributes.Count;
			string[] array = new string[count];
			string[] array2 = new string[count2];
			uint[] array3 = new uint[count2];
			ACMObject acmObject = ((IAcmEditor)this).AcmObject;
			int num = 0;
			foreach (ACMVariable variable in TableObject.Variables)
			{
				array[num] = variable.Name;
				num++;
			}
			num = 0;
			foreach (ACMAttribute attribute in TableObject.Attributes)
			{
				array2[num] = attribute.ToString();
				array3[num] = 10u;
				num++;
			}
			string name = acmObject.Name;
			TableObject.ParentModel.ACMDoc.SetTableDefinition(TableObject.ParentModel.ModelTypeName, name, array, array2, array3);
			IsModified = false;
			if (TableObject.IsADuplicate)
			{
				TableObject.IsADuplicate = false;
			}
		}

		{
		}

		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			//IL_005c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0066: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0076: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0080: Expected O, but got Unknown

			{

				break;
			case 2:

				break;

				break;
			case 6:
				tableTab = (TabItem)target;
				break;
			case 7:

				break;

				}
			}
		}

		public ObservableCollection<string> SelectedAttributes
		{
			get
			{
				return _selectedAttributes;
			}

				if (_selectedAttributes != null)

		public TablePropertiesDialog(ACMTable acmTable)
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			_acmTable = acmTable;
			if (acmTable.IsBuiltIn)
			{
				variablesTab.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			base.Loaded += TablePropertiesDialog_Loaded;
		}

		private void TablePropertiesDialog_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			InitializeAttributesList();
			InitiliazeVariablesList();
			base.DataContext = this;
		}

		private void InitializeAttributesList()
		{
			ObservableCollection<string> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<string>();
			if (_acmTable.Attributes != null)
			{
				foreach (ACMAttribute attribute in _acmTable.Attributes)
				{
					observableCollection.Add(CustomTableDataTable.GetDisplayedTextOfAttribute(attribute));
				}
			}
			SelectedAttributes = observableCollection;
			ObservableCollection<string> observableCollection2 = new ObservableCollection<string>();
			foreach (string allAttribute in AllAttributes)
			{
				if (!observableCollection.Contains(allAttribute))
				{
					observableCollection2.Add(allAttribute);
				}
			}
			AvailableAttributes = observableCollection2;
		}

		private void _availableAttributes_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		private void _selectedAttributes_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ObservableCollection<string> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<string>();
			foreach (string allAttribute in AllAttributes)
			{
				if (!_selectedAttributes.Contains(allAttribute))
				{
					observableCollection.Add(allAttribute);
				}
			}
			AvailableAttributes = observableCollection;
		}

		private void InitiliazeVariablesList()
		{
			ObservableCollection<ACMVariable> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<ACMVariable>();
			if (_acmTable.Variables != null)
			{
				foreach (ACMVariable variable in _acmTable.Variables)
				{
					observableCollection.Add(variable);
				}
			}
			SelectedVariables = observableCollection;
		}

		private void BtnAddVariable_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			ACMVariable item = new ACMVariable(txtEditVariableName.Text.Trim());
			if (!SelectedVariables.Contains(item))
			{
				SelectedVariables.Add(item);
			}
		}

		private bool RemoveVariableCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			return CurrentVariable != null;
		}

		private void RemoveVariableCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			if (SelectedVariables.Contains(CurrentVariable))
			{
				SelectedVariables.Remove(CurrentVariable);
			}
		}

		private bool MoveUpVariablesCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (CurrentVariable != null)
			{
				return SelectedVariables.IndexOf(CurrentVariable) != 0;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void MoveUpVariablesCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			MoveVariable(isMoveUp: true);
		}

		private void MoveVariable(bool isMoveUp)
		{
			int num = SelectedVariables.IndexOf(CurrentVariable);
			if (isMoveUp)
			{
				SelectedVariables.Move(num, num - 1);
			}
			else
			{
				SelectedVariables.Move(num, num + 1);
			}
		}

		private bool MoveDownVariablesCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (CurrentVariable != null)
			{
				return SelectedVariables.IndexOf(CurrentVariable) != SelectedVariables.Count - 1;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void MoveDownVariablesCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			MoveVariable(isMoveUp: false);
		}

		private void OnPropertyChanged(string propName)
		{
			this.PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
		}

		private void BtnOK_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			Collection<ACMAttribute> collection = new Collection<ACMAttribute>();
			foreach (string selectedAttribute in SelectedAttributes)
			{
				collection.Add(CustomTableDataTable.GetAttributePerDisplayText(selectedAttribute));
			}
			_acmTable.Attributes = collection;
			_acmTable.Variables = SelectedVariables;
			base.DialogResult = true;
			Close();
		}

		[DllImport("user32.dll")]
		public static extern int SendMessage(IntPtr hWnd, uint Msg, long wParam, long lParam);

		public TextEditor(ACMObject acmText)
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			axEdCtrl = new AxEditorCtrl();
			formHost.Child = axEdCtrl;
			axEdCtrl.CreateControl();
			axEdCtrl.ContextMenuOpening += AxEdCtrl_ContextMenuOpening;
			AcmObject = acmText;
			if (AcmObject is ACMScript { ParentModel: var parentModel } aCMScript)
			{
				if (!aCMScript.IsADuplicate)
				{
					aCMScript.Text = parentModel.ACMDoc.GetScriptText(parentModel.ModelTypeName, aCMScript.Name);
				}
				expander1.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			else if (AcmObject is ACMProcedure { ParentModel: var parentModel2 } aCMProcedure)
			{
				aCMProcedure.Text = parentModel2.ACMDoc.GetProcedureText(aCMProcedure.Name);
				expander1.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			else if (AcmObject is ACMSubmodel { ParentModel: var parentModel3 } aCMSubmodel)
			{
				aCMSubmodel.Text = parentModel3.ACMDoc.GetModelText(aCMSubmodel.Name);
				ModelAssistant content = new ModelAssistant(TextObject.ParentModel, axEdCtrl);
				expander1.Content = content;
			}
			else if (AcmObject is ACMCustomVariableType { ParentModel: var parentModel4 } aCMCustomVariableType)
			{
				aCMCustomVariableType.Text = parentModel4.ACMDoc.GetModelText(aCMCustomVariableType.Name);
				expander1.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			else if (AcmObject is ACMCustomParameterType { ParentModel: var parentModel5 } aCMCustomParameterType)
			{
				aCMCustomParameterType.Text = parentModel5.ACMDoc.GetModelText(aCMCustomParameterType.Name);
				expander1.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			else
			{
				ModelAssistant content2 = new ModelAssistant(TextObject.ParentModel, axEdCtrl);
				expander1.Content = content2;
			}
			base.DataContext = this;
			formHost.KeyDown += formHost_KeyDown;
		}

		private void formHost_KeyDown(object sender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (SpecialKeyDic.ContainsKey(e.Key))
			{
				SendMessage(axEdCtrl.Handle, 256u, SpecialKeyDic[e.Key], 0L);
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		public void OnClosed()
		{
			if (_findAndReplaceDlg != null)
			{
				_findAndReplaceDlg.Close();
			}
			if (_typeTypeDefinitionDlg != null)
			{
				_typeTypeDefinitionDlg.Close();
			}
		}

		public void Save()
		{
			if (_hasLoaded)
			{
				if (AcmObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMScript)
				{
					string modelTypeName = TextObject.ParentModel.ModelTypeName;
					string name = ((IAcmEditor)this).AcmObject.Name;
					string editorText = axEdCtrl.GetEditorText();
					TextObject.ParentModel.ACMDoc.SetScriptText(modelTypeName, name, editorText);
					axEdCtrl.SetSavePoint();
				}
				else if (AcmObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMSubmodel || AcmObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMProcedure || AcmObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomParameterType || AcmObject.Type == ACMObjectTypes.ACMCustomVariableType)
				{
					string name2 = ((IAcmEditor)this).AcmObject.Name;
					string editorText2 = axEdCtrl.GetEditorText();
					TextObject.ParentModel.ACMDoc.SaveSubModelText(name2, editorText2);
					axEdCtrl.SetSavePoint();
				}
				else if (AcmObject is ACMText && IsModified)
				{
					string name3 = ((IAcmEditor)this).AcmObject.Name;
					string editorText3 = axEdCtrl.GetEditorText();
					TextObject.ParentModel.ACMDoc.SaveSubModelText(name3, editorText3);
				}
			}
		}

		public void OnLoaded()
		{
			if (!_hasLoaded)
			{
				axEdCtrl.CreateEditorWithText((int)formHost.ActualWidth, (int)formHost.ActualHeight, TextObject.Text, bScript: false);
				if (AcmObject is ACMScript)
				{
					SetTypeOfEditor(0);
				}
				else
				{
					SetTypeOfEditor(1);
				}
				_hasLoaded = true;
			}
			SetFont();
		}

		internal bool CanBtnCommentSelected()
		{
			return axEdCtrl.GetSelectedText().Length > 0;
		}

		internal bool CanBtnUnommentSelected()
		{
			bool result = axEdCtrl.IsLineCommented();
			if (axEdCtrl.GetSelectedText().Length > 0)
			{
				return result;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal void BtnUncommentSelected()
		{
			axEdCtrl.UncommentSelectedLines(1);
		}

		internal void BtnCommentSelected()
		{
			axEdCtrl.CommentSelectedLines();
		}

		internal void SaveTextToModel()
		{
			TextObject.ParentModel.ModelText = axEdCtrl.GetEditorText();
		}

		internal bool CanDecreaseIndent()
		{

		public bool GetEnableCompileBtn()
		{
			bool result = false;
			int num = axEdCtrl.IsModelEditor();
			if (num == 1 || num == 0)

				result = true;
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal void SetFont()
		{
			string text = "";
			text = SystemEnvironment.InternalVersionString;
			string name = "Software\\AspenTech\\Aspen Plus\\" + text + "\\Text Editor\\Font";
			RegistryKey registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey(name, writable: true);
			if (registryKey == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text2 = "";
			string text3 = "";
			string text4 = "";
			text2 = (string)registryKey.GetValue("FontName");
			text3 = (string)registryKey.GetValue("FontType");
			text4 = (string)registryKey.GetValue("FontSize");
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2) || string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3) || string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = 0;
			int result = 0;
			if (int.TryParse(text4, out result))
			{
				num = result;
			}
			if (num >= 8 && num <= 72)
			{
				bool bBold = false;
				bool bItalic = false;
				if (text3 == styleFontArray[0])
				{
					bBold = false;
					bItalic = false;
				}
				if (text3 == styleFontArray[1])
				{
					bBold = false;
					bItalic = true;
				}
				if (text3 == styleFontArray[2])
				{
					bBold = true;
					bItalic = false;
				}
				if (text3 == styleFontArray[3])
				{
					bBold = true;
					bItalic = true;
				}
				axEdCtrl.SetFont(text2, num, bBold, bItalic);
			}
		}

		private void FindCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			LaunchFindAndReplaceDialog(SearchMode.Find);
		}

		private void LaunchFindAndReplaceDialog(SearchMode mode)
		{
			if (_findAndReplaceDlg != null && !_findAndReplaceDlg.IsLoaded)
			{
				_findAndReplaceDlg = null;
			}
			if (_findAndReplaceDlg == null)
			{
				_findAndReplaceDlg = new FindAndReplace(axEdCtrl)
				{
					Owner = Window.GetWindow(this)
				};
				_findAndReplaceDlg.Show();
			}
			string selectedText = axEdCtrl.GetSelectedText();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(selectedText))
			{
				_findAndReplaceDlg.SearchText = selectedText;
			}
			_findAndReplaceDlg.SearchMode = mode;
			_findAndReplaceDlg.Activate();
		}

		private void FindNextCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			int count = FindAndReplace._searchStrings.Count;
			if (count <= 0)
			{
				FindCommand.Execute(null);
				return;
			}
			string szText = FindAndReplace._searchStrings[count - 1];
			axEdCtrl.SearchNext(szText);
		}

		private void FindPreviousCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			int count = FindAndReplace._searchStrings.Count;
			if (count <= 0)
			{
				FindCommand.Execute(null);
				return;
			}
			string szText = FindAndReplace._searchStrings[count - 1];
			axEdCtrl.SearchPrevious(szText);
		}

		private void ReplaceCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			LaunchFindAndReplaceDialog(SearchMode.Replace);
		}

		private void InsertFileCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			System.Windows.Forms.OpenFileDialog openFileDialog = new System.Windows.Forms.OpenFileDialog
			{
				CheckFileExists = true,
				Filter = CustomModelsResources.TextEditorContextMenu_InsertFileFilter
			};
			if (openFileDialog.ShowDialog() == DialogResult.OK)
			{
				string text = File.ReadAllText(openFileDialog.FileName);
				axEdCtrl.InsertText(text);
			}
		}

		private void DeleteCommand_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.CanExecute = true;
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private void DeleteCommand_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(axEdCtrl.GetSelectedText()))
			{
				axEdCtrl.Clear();
			}
			else
			{
				int lineCount = axEdCtrl.GetLineCount();
				int currentLine = axEdCtrl.GetCurrentLine();
				int startLinePos = axEdCtrl.GetStartLinePos(currentLine);
				int selectionStart = axEdCtrl.GetSelectionStart();
				string text = axEdCtrl.GetLineText((uint)currentLine).Substring(selectionStart - startLinePos);
				if (lineCount == currentLine)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						string szText = text[0].ToString();
						axEdCtrl.SearchForward(szText);
						axEdCtrl.Clear();
					}
				}
				else
				{
					axEdCtrl.SetSelectionStart(selectionStart);
					axEdCtrl.SetSelectionEnd(selectionStart + 1);
					axEdCtrl.Clear();
				}
			}
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		internal bool CanRevert()
		{
			string name = TextObject.Name;
			bool flag = false;
			if (TextObject.ParentModel.dicSubModNameCompFlag.ContainsKey(name))
			{
				flag = TextObject.ParentModel.dicSubModNameCompFlag[name];
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				return true;
			}
			string compiledModelText = TextObject.ParentModel.CompiledModelText;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(compiledModelText))
			{
				return false;
			}
			compiledModelText = compiledModelText.Trim();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(compiledModelText))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return compiledModelText.Length > 0;
		}

		private bool RevertToLastCompiledCommand_CanExecute(object arg)
		{
			if (axEdCtrl != null)
			{
				return CanRevert();
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void RevertToLastCompiledCommand_Executed(object obj)
		{
			if (axEdCtrl == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string name = TextObject.Name;
			if (TextObject.ParentModel.dicSubModNameCompModText.ContainsKey(name))
			{
				string text = TextObject.ParentModel.dicSubModNameCompModText[name];
				text = text.Trim();
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					axEdCtrl.SetText(text);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				string compiledModelText = TextObject.ParentModel.CompiledModelText;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(compiledModelText))
				{
					axEdCtrl.SetText(compiledModelText);
				}
			}
		}

		private void expander1_Collapsed(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			((FrameworkElement)(object)modelAssistantBorder).Tag = ((FrameworkElement)(object)modelAssistantBorder).ActualHeight;
			((FrameworkElement)(object)modelAssistantBorder).Height = double.NaN;
			((FrameworkElement)(object)modelAssistantBorder).MinHeight = 0.0;
			modelAssistantBorder.IsResizeEnable = false;
		}

		private void expander1_Expanded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (((FrameworkElement)(object)modelAssistantBorder).Tag != null)
			{
				((FrameworkElement)(object)modelAssistantBorder).Height = (double)((FrameworkElement)(object)modelAssistantBorder).Tag;
				modelAssistantBorder.IsResizeEnable = true;
			}
			((FrameworkElement)(object)modelAssistantBorder).MinHeight = 120.0;
		}
